  aliases: "别名/同义词",
  hit_level: "命中后等级",
  hit_score_cap: "命中后最高分",
  rules: "规则明细",
  key: "规则标识",
  rule_key: "规则标识",
  label: "运营名称",
  description: "规则说明",
  signal: "读取哪个结果字段",
  match_any: "命中词",
  exemptions: "豁免词",
  tracks: "赛道",
  default_track: "默认赛道",
  display_name: "运营名称",
  members: "包含的业务类型",
  base_score: "基础分",
  dimension_max: "维度最高分",
  track_cap: "赛道最高分",
  penalties: "各媒介调整值",
  baseline: "基准媒介",
  high_score_veto: "高分限制",
  threshold: "触发分数",
  cap_to: "限制到",
  subcategory_dimensions: "各赛道维度",
  dimension_deduction_rules: "维度扣分规则",
  anchors: "校准锚点",
  max_anchor_images: "最多参考图片数",
  levels: "等级区间",
  level: "等级",
  min_score: "最低分",
  format_version: "内容格式版本（系统维护）",
  version: "规则格式",
  note: "说明",
  source: "结果来源",
}

function labelFor(key: string) {
  return FIELD_LABELS[key] ?? key.replaceAll("_", " ")
}

function clone<T>(value: T): T {
  return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)) as T
}

function setAtPath(root: Record<string, unknown>, path: Path, value: unknown) {
  const next = clone(root)
  let cursor: unknown = next
  path.slice(0, -1).forEach((part) => {
    cursor = (cursor as Record<string | number, unknown>)[part]
  })
  ;(cursor as Record<string | number, unknown>)[path[path.length - 1]] = value
  return next
}

function removeAtPath(root: Record<string, unknown>, path: Path, index: number) {
  const next = clone(root)
  let cursor: unknown = next
  path.forEach((part) => {
    cursor = (cursor as Record<string | number, unknown>)[part]
  })
  ;(cursor as unknown[]).splice(index, 1)
  return next
}

function defaultArrayItem(moduleTypeKey: string, field: string): unknown {
  if (field === "rules") {
    return moduleTypeKey === "eligibility_redline"
      ? {
          key: `rule_${Date.now().toString(36)}`,
          label: "新规则",
          signal: "production_fields.reason",
          match_any: ["请填写命中词"],
          exemptions: [],
          enabled: true,
        }
      : {}
  }
  if (field === "tracks") {
    return {
      key: `track_${Date.now().toString(36)}`,
      label: "新赛道",
      base_score: 0,
      dimension_max: 100,
      track_cap: 100,
      dimension_schema_ref: { schema_key: "请填写", version: "1.0.0" },
    }
  }
  if (field === "levels") {
    return { level: "L5", enabled: true, min_score: 0, display_name: "L5" }
  }
  if (field === "anchors") return { label: "新锚点", score: 0, evidence: "" }
  return ""
}

function PrimitiveField({
  field,
  value,
  path,
  content,
  onChange,
}: {
  field: string
  value: unknown
  path: Path
  content: Record<string, unknown>
  onChange: (next: Record<string, unknown>) => void
}) {
  if (SYSTEM_MANAGED_FIELDS.has(field)) {
    // 内容格式版本由后端目录维护：改动会被校验拒绝，只读展示避免运营误改
    return (
      <div className="border-y border-[var(--line)] py-2 text-sm">
        <span className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{labelFor(field)}</span>
        <span className="visual-code text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{String(value)}</span>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]">系统按此版本号校验这份配置的字段结构，升级由研发在后端目录完成，运营无需修改。</p>
      </div>
    )
  }
  if (typeof value === "boolean") {
    return (
      <label className="visual-native-choice flex min-h-11 items-center justify-between gap-4 border-y border-[var(--line)] py-2">
        <span className="font-semibold">{labelFor(field)}</span>
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          checked={value}
          onChange={(event) => onChange(setAtPath(content, path, event.target.checked))}
          className="visual-native-checkbox size-5"
        />
      </label>
    )
  }
  if (typeof value === "number") {
    return (
      <label>
        <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">{labelFor(field)}</span>
        <Input
          type="number"
          value={value}
          onChange={(event) => onChange(setAtPath(content, path, Number(event.target.value)))}
        />
      </label>
    )
  }
  const stringValue = value == null ? "" : String(value)
  const longText = stringValue.length > 80 || /note|description|comment|formula|policy/.test(field)
  return (
    <label className={longText ? "md:col-span-2" : ""}>
      <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">{labelFor(field)}</span>
      {longText ? (
        <Textarea
          className="min-h-24"
          value={stringValue}
          onChange={(event) => onChange(setAtPath(content, path, event.target.value))}
        />
      ) : (
        <Input
          value={stringValue}
          onChange={(event) => onChange(setAtPath(content, path, event.target.value))}
        />
      )}
    </label>
  )
}

function ArrayField({
  field,
  value,
  path,
  moduleTypeKey,
  content,
  onChange,
}: {
  field: string
  value: unknown[]
  path: Path
  moduleTypeKey: string
  content: Record<string, unknown>
  onChange: (next: Record<string, unknown>) => void
}) {
  const objectItems = OBJECT_ARRAY_FIELDS.has(field)
    || value.some((item) => item != null && typeof item === "object" && !Array.isArray(item))
  if (!objectItems) {
    const numeric = value.length > 0 && value.every((item) => typeof item === "number")
    return (
      <label className="md:col-span-2">
        <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">{labelFor(field)}</span>
        <Textarea
          className="min-h-20"
          value={value.join("\n")}
          placeholder="每行填写一项"
          onChange={(event) => {
            const items = event.target.value.split("\n").map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean)
            onChange(setAtPath(content, path, numeric ? items.map(Number) : items))
          }}
        />
        <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">每行一项，空行会自动忽略。</span>
      </label>
    )
  }
  return (
    <section className="md:col-span-2 border-y border-[var(--line-strong)] py-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div><p className="text-sm font-semibold">{labelFor(field)}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">共 {value.length} 项，可逐项调整。</p></div>
        <Button
          type="button"
          size="sm"
          variant="secondary"
          onClick={() => onChange(setAtPath(content, path, [...value, defaultArrayItem(moduleTypeKey, field)]))}
        >
          <Plus />添加一项
        </Button>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-4 space-y-4">
        {value.map((item, index) => (
          <div key={index} className="border border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 p-4 rounded-md">
            <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold">{labelFor(field)} {index + 1}</p>
              <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => onChange(removeAtPath(content, path, index))}>
                <Trash />移除
              </Button>
            </div>
            <ObjectFields
              value={item as Record<string, unknown>}
              path={[...path, index]}
              moduleTypeKey={moduleTypeKey}
              content={content}
              onChange={onChange}
            />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

function ObjectFields({
  value,
  path,
  moduleTypeKey,
  content,
  onChange,
}: {
  value: Record<string, unknown>
  path: Path
  moduleTypeKey: string
  content: Record<string, unknown>
  onChange: (next: Record<string, unknown>) => void
}) {
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
      {Object.entries(value).map(([field, child]) => {
        const childPath = [...path, field]
        if (Array.isArray(child)) {
          return <ArrayField key={field} field={field} value={child} path={childPath} moduleTypeKey={moduleTypeKey} content={content} onChange={onChange} />
        }
        if (child != null && typeof child === "object") {
          return (
            <section key={field} className="md:col-span-2 border-l-2 border-[var(--line-strong)] pl-4">
              <p className="mb-3 text-sm font-semibold">{labelFor(field)}</p>
              <ObjectFields value={child as Record<string, unknown>} path={childPath} moduleTypeKey={moduleTypeKey} content={content} onChange={onChange} />
            </section>
          )
        }
        return <PrimitiveField key={field} field={field} value={child} path={childPath} content={content} onChange={onChange} />
      })}
    </div>
  )
}

export function SchemaDrivenRuleModuleEditor({
  moduleTypeKey,
  content,
  onChange,
  definition,
}: RuleModuleEditorProps) {
  const starter = DEFAULT_MODULE_CONTENTS[moduleTypeKey]
  return (
    <div>
      {definition && <p className="mb-5 max-w-[78ch] text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">{definition.description}</p>}
      <ObjectFields value={content} path={[]} moduleTypeKey={moduleTypeKey} content={content} onChange={onChange} />
      {!Object.keys(content).length && (
        <div className="border border-dashed border-[var(--line-strong)] px-4 py-8 text-center text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)] rounded-md">
          <p>{starter ? "当前模块还没有内容，可以从一份安全的基础配置开始。" : "当前模块还没有内容，请由研发在后端目录提供初始表单。"}</p>
          {starter && (
            <Button className="mt-4" type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={() => onChange(clone(starter))}>
              <Plus />开始配置此模块
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function OperatorEditorFrame({
  props,
  guidance,
  children,
}: {
  props: RuleModuleEditorProps
  guidance: string
  children: ReactNode
}) {
  const starter = DEFAULT_MODULE_CONTENTS[props.moduleTypeKey]
  if (!Object.keys(props.content).length) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-dashed border-[var(--line-strong)] px-5 py-10 text-center rounded-md">
        <p className="text-sm font-bold">这个模块还没有规则</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">先载入一份安全的基础配置，再按业务语言逐项调整。</p>
        {starter && (
          <Button className="mt-4" type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={() => props.onChange(clone(starter))}>
            <Plus />开始配置此模块
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
    )
  }
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-4">
        <p className="text-sm font-bold">当前规则会怎样工作？</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6">{describeRuleModule(props.moduleTypeKey, props.content)}</p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{guidance}</p>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-6">{children}</div>
      <details className="visual-native-details mt-8 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-4">
        <summary className="font-bold">高级设置（通常不需要修改）</summary>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">这里保留完整兼容字段，供特殊配置和研发排查使用。日常运营只需维护上方表单。</p>
        <div className="mt-5"><SchemaDrivenRuleModuleEditor {...props} /></div>
      </details>
    </div>
  )
}

function OperatorSection({ title, description, action, children }: { title: string; description: string; action?: ReactNode; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <section className="border-y border-[var(--line)] py-5 first:border-t-0">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div><h3 className="text-base font-bold">{title}</h3><p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{description}</p></div>
        {action}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-4">{children}</div>
    </section>
  )
}

function ToggleField({ label, hint, checked, onChange }: { label: string; hint?: string; checked: boolean; onChange: (next: boolean) => void }) {
  return (
    <label className="visual-native-choice flex min-h-12 items-center justify-between gap-5 rounded-md border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-4 py-3">
      <span><span className="block text-sm font-bold">{label}</span>{hint && <span className="mt-1 block text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{hint}</span>}</span>
      <input type="checkbox" className="visual-native-checkbox size-5" checked={checked} onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.checked)} />
    </label>
  )
}

function NumberField({ label, value, min, max, suffix, onChange }: { label: string; value: number; min?: number; max?: number; suffix?: string; onChange: (next: number) => void }) {
  return (
    <label>
      <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-bold">{label}</span>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Input type="number" min={min} max={max} value={value} onChange={(event) => onChange(Number(event.target.value))} />{suffix && <span className="shrink-0 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{suffix}</span>}</div>
    </label>
  )
}

function TextField({ label, value, placeholder, onChange }: { label: string; value: string; placeholder?: string; onChange: (next: string) => void }) {
  return <label><span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-bold">{label}</span><Input value={value} placeholder={placeholder} onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)} /></label>
}

function SelectField({ label, value, options, hint, onChange }: { label: string; value: string; options: { value: string; label: string }[]; hint?: string; onChange: (next: string) => void }) {
  return (
    <label className="block">
      <span className="mb-1 block text-xs font-semibold">{label}</span>
      <select
        className="visual-native-select w-full"
        value={value}
        onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
      >
        {options.map((option) => (
          <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>{option.label}</option>
        ))}
      </select>
      {hint && <span className="mt-1 block text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]">{hint}</span>}
    </label>
  )
}

function LinesField({ label, value, placeholder, onChange }: { label: string; value: unknown; placeholder?: string; onChange: (next: string[]) => void }) {
  const lines = arrayValue(value).filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string")
  return (
    <label>
      <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-bold">{label}</span>
      <Textarea className="min-h-24" value={lines.join("\n")} placeholder={placeholder} onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value.split("\n").map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean))} />
      <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">每行填写一项。</span>
    </label>
  )
}

function RuleSentence({ when, then }: { when: ReactNode; then: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-3 rounded-md border border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 p-4 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto_minmax(0,1fr)] xl:items-start">
      <div><p className="mb-2 text-xs font-bold text-[var(--muted)]">当满足以下情况</p>{when}</div>
      <span className="hidden pt-8 text-sm font-bold text-[var(--muted)] xl:block">→</span>
      <div><p className="mb-2 text-xs font-bold text-[var(--muted)]">系统就会</p>{then}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

function RedlineModuleEditor(props: RuleModuleEditorProps) {
  const rules = arrayValue(props.content.rules).map(recordValue)
  const patch = (path: Path, value: unknown) => props.onChange(updateRulePath(props.content, path, value))
  const keyField = props.content.signal ? "rule_key" : "key"
  return (
    <OperatorEditorFrame props={props} guidance="先写清楚什么情况不能继续评测，再决定命中后的等级、最高分和是否立即停止。">
      <div className="space-y-5">
        <ToggleField label="启用这组红线" hint="关闭后，这个模块保留在工作流里但不会拦截结果。" checked={booleanValue(props.content.enabled, true)} onChange={(value) => patch(["enabled"], value)} />
        <OperatorSection title="命中后的统一处理" description="所有红线默认使用同一种处理结果；特殊情况可拆成另一个模块版本。">
          <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
            <label><span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-bold">判定等级</span><select className="visual-native-select w-full" value={stringValue(props.content.hit_level, "L5")} onChange={(event) => patch(["hit_level"], event.target.value)}>{["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"].map((level) => <option key={level}>{level}</option>)}</select></label>
            <NumberField label="最高保留分" value={numberValue(props.content.hit_score_cap, 49)} min={0} max={100} suffix="分" onChange={(value) => patch(["hit_score_cap"], value)} />
            <ToggleField label="命中后停止后续评测" checked={booleanValue(props.content.terminal, true)} onChange={(value) => patch(["terminal"], value)} />
          </div>
        </OperatorSection>
        <OperatorSection title="红线条件" description="任意一条命中就会执行上面的统一处理。" action={<Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => {
          const next = newRedlineRule(`rule_${Date.now().toString(36)}`)
          if (keyField === "rule_key") {
            const { key, ...fields } = next
            patch(["rules"], [...rules, { ...fields, rule_key: key }])
          } else {
            patch(["rules"], [...rules, next])
          }
        }}><Plus />新增一条规则</Button>}>
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {rules.map((rule, index) => (
              <div key={`${stringValue(rule[keyField], "rule")}-${index}`}>
                <RuleSentence
                  when={<div className="space-y-3"><TextField label="运营名称" value={stringValue(rule.label ?? rule.description, `红线 ${index + 1}`)} onChange={(value) => patch(["rules", index, rule.label === undefined ? "description" : "label"], value)} /><LinesField label="出现任意一个词或信号" value={rule.match_any} placeholder="例如：严重违规" onChange={(value) => patch(["rules", index, "match_any"], value)} /><LinesField label="但出现这些情况时不拦截" value={rule.exemptions} placeholder="可不填" onChange={(value) => patch(["rules", index, "exemptions"], value)} /></div>}
                  then={<div className="space-y-3"><p className="rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-3 text-sm">判为 <strong>{stringValue(props.content.hit_level, "L5")}</strong>，最高 <strong>{numberValue(props.content.hit_score_cap, 49)} 分</strong>{booleanValue(props.content.terminal, true) ? "，并停止后续评测" : "，但继续后续评测"}。</p><Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => patch(["rules"], rules.filter((_, itemIndex) => itemIndex !== index))}><Trash />删除这条规则</Button></div>}
                />
              </div>
            ))}
            {!rules.length && <p className="border border-dashed border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-8 text-center text-xs text-[var(--muted)] rounded-md">还没有红线条件，点击“新增一条规则”。</p>}
          </div>
        </OperatorSection>
      </div>
    </OperatorEditorFrame>
  )
}

function TrackRoutingModuleEditor(props: RuleModuleEditorProps) {
  const rawTracks = props.content.tracks
  const arrayTracks = Array.isArray(rawTracks) ? rawTracks.map(recordValue) : null
  const mappedTracks = arrayTracks ? null : recordValue(rawTracks)
  const patch = (path: Path, value: unknown) => props.onChange(updateRulePath(props.content, path, value))
  const trackKeys = arrayTracks ? arrayTracks.map((track) => stringValue(track.key)).filter(Boolean) : Object.keys(mappedTracks ?? {})
  const fallbackPath: Path = arrayTracks ? ["default_track"] : ["other_resolution", "fallback_track"]
  const fallback = arrayTracks ? stringValue(props.content.default_track) : stringValue(recordValue(props.content.other_resolution).fallback_track)
  const displayTracks: Record<string, unknown>[] = arrayTracks
    ?? Object.entries(mappedTracks ?? {}).map(([key, value]) => ({ ...recordValue(value), key }))
  const adjustments = recordValue(props.content.track_adjustments)
  const trackLabel = (key: string) => {
    if (arrayTracks) return stringValue(arrayTracks.find((item) => item.key === key)?.label, key)
    return stringValue(recordValue(mappedTracks?.[key]).display_name, key)
  }
  return (
    <OperatorEditorFrame props={props} guidance="运营只需维护赛道名称、识别范围和分数上限；系统标识和字段来源保留在高级设置。">
      <OperatorSection title="无法判断时走哪条赛道？" description="选择一个兜底赛道，避免内容卡在流程中。">
        <select className="visual-native-select w-full max-w-md" value={fallback} onChange={(event) => patch(fallbackPath, event.target.value)}>{trackKeys.map((key) => <option key={key} value={key}>{trackLabel(key)}</option>)}</select>
      </OperatorSection>
      <OperatorSection title="赛道识别规则" description="当内容属于某组业务类型时，进入对应赛道。" action={<Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => {
        const key = `track_${Date.now().toString(36)}`
        if (arrayTracks) patch(["tracks"], [...arrayTracks, { key, label: "新赛道", base_score: 0, dimension_max: 100, track_cap: 100, dimension_schema_ref: { schema_key: "default_dimensions", version: "1.0.0" } }])
        else patch(["tracks", key], { display_name: "新赛道", members: [], visual_max: 45, narrative_max: 45, innovation_max: 10 })
      }}><Plus />新增赛道</Button>}>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {displayTracks.map((track, index) => {
            const key = stringValue(track.key, `track_${index + 1}`)
            const basePath: Path = arrayTracks ? ["tracks", index] : ["tracks", key]
            return <RuleSentence key={key} when={<div className="space-y-3"><TextField label="赛道名称" value={stringValue(track.label ?? track.display_name, key)} onChange={(value) => patch([...basePath, arrayTracks ? "label" : "display_name"], value)} />{!arrayTracks && <LinesField label="属于这些业务类型时" value={track.members} onChange={(value) => patch([...basePath, "members"], value)} placeholder="每行一个业务类型" />}</div>} then={<div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">{arrayTracks ? <><NumberField label="基础分" value={numberValue(track.base_score)} min={0} max={100} onChange={(value) => patch([...basePath, "base_score"], value)} /><NumberField label="维度最高分" value={numberValue(track.dimension_max, 100)} min={0} max={100} onChange={(value) => patch([...basePath, "dimension_max"], value)} /><NumberField label="赛道最高分" value={numberValue(track.track_cap, 100)} min={0} max={100} onChange={(value) => patch([...basePath, "track_cap"], value)} /></> : <><NumberField label="视觉最高分" value={numberValue(track.visual_max, 45)} min={0} max={100} onChange={(value) => patch([...basePath, "visual_max"], value)} /><NumberField label="叙事最高分" value={numberValue(track.narrative_max, 45)} min={0} max={100} onChange={(value) => patch([...basePath, "narrative_max"], value)} /><NumberField label="创新最高分" value={numberValue(track.innovation_max, 10)} min={0} max={100} onChange={(value) => patch([...basePath, "innovation_max"], value)} /></>}</div>} />
          })}
        </div>
      </OperatorSection>
      <OperatorSection
        title="赛道固定调整"
        description="按赛道对最终分做固定加减，不影响调用B给出的美感分本身。留空或填 0 表示该赛道不调整。"
      >
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {displayTracks.map((track, index) => {
            const key = stringValue(track.key, `track_${index + 1}`)
            const adjustment = recordValue(adjustments[key])
            const deduction = numberValue(adjustment.deduction, 0)
            const bonus = numberValue(adjustment.bonus, 0)
            const writeAdjustment = (nextDeduction: number, nextBonus: number) => {
              const next = { ...adjustments }
              if (!nextDeduction && !nextBonus) delete next[key]
              else next[key] = { deduction: nextDeduction, bonus: nextBonus }
              patch(["track_adjustments"], next)
            }
            return (
              <RuleSentence
                key={key}
                when={
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <p className="rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-3 text-sm font-bold">
                      判定为「{trackLabel(key)}」时
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                      {deduction || bonus
                        ? `最终分${bonus ? ` 加 ${bonus} 分` : ""}${deduction ? ` 扣 ${deduction} 分` : ""}`
                        : "不做调整"}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                }
                then={
                  <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    <NumberField
                      label="扣分"
                      value={deduction}
                      min={0}
                      max={100}
                      suffix="分"
                      onChange={(value) => writeAdjustment(value, bonus)}
                    />
                    <NumberField
                      label="加分"
                      value={bonus}
                      min={0}
                      max={100}
                      suffix="分"
                      onChange={(value) => writeAdjustment(deduction, value)}
                    />
                  </div>
                }
              />
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </OperatorSection>
    </OperatorEditorFrame>
  )
}

function MediaAdjustmentModuleEditor(props: RuleModuleEditorProps) {
  const [newMedia, setNewMedia] = useState("")
  const penalties = recordValue(props.content.penalties)
  const patch = (path: Path, value: unknown) => props.onChange(updateRulePath(props.content, path, value))
  function removePenalty(key: string) {
    props.onChange(removeMediaPenalty(props.content, key))
  }
  return (
    <OperatorEditorFrame props={props} guidance="基准媒介通常保持 0；其他媒介只填写 0 或负数，表示需要降低多少分。">
      <div className="space-y-5">
        <ToggleField label="启用媒介调整" checked={booleanValue(props.content.enabled, true)} onChange={(value) => patch(["enabled"], value)} />
        <OperatorSection title="媒介调整规则" description="每种媒介单独维护调整值，正数不会通过后端校验。" action={<div className="flex gap-2"><Input className="w-40" value={newMedia} placeholder="新媒介名称" onChange={(event) => setNewMedia(normalizeKey(event.target.value))} /><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!newMedia || newMedia in penalties} onClick={() => { patch(["penalties", newMedia], 0); setNewMedia("") }}><Plus />新增</Button></div>}>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {Object.entries(penalties).map(([media, penalty]) => <RuleSentence key={media} when={<p className="rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-3 text-sm font-bold">媒介是 {media}</p>} then={<div className="flex items-end gap-2"><NumberField label="调整分数" value={numberValue(penalty)} min={-100} max={0} suffix="分" onChange={(value) => patch(["penalties", media], value)} /><Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => removePenalty(media)}><Trash />删除</Button></div>} />)}
          </div>
        </OperatorSection>
        <OperatorSection title="基准媒介" description="基准媒介是比较起点，通常调整为 0。"><select className="visual-native-select w-full max-w-md" value={stringValue(props.content.baseline)} onChange={(event) => patch(["baseline"], event.target.value)}>{Object.keys(penalties).map((key) => <option key={key}>{key}</option>)}</select></OperatorSection>
      </div>
    </OperatorEditorFrame>
  )
}

function vetoTierOptions(veto: Record<string, unknown>) {
  const tiers = recordValue(veto.tiers)
  const keys = Object.keys(tiers)
  const labels: Record<string, string> = {
    A: "A 档（严重）",
    B: "B 档（一般）",
    record_only: "仅记录，不压分",
  }
  return (keys.length ? keys : ["A"]).map((key) => {
    const tier = recordValue(tiers[key])
    const suffix = tier.action === "record_only"
      ? "仅记录"
      : `上限 ${numberValue(tier.cap_to, numberValue(veto.cap_to, 79))} 分`
    return { value: key, label: `${labels[key] ?? key} · ${suffix}` }
  })
}

/** 新增规则要落在一个真实存在的档位上，否则保存时被合同校验拒绝 */
function defaultVetoSeverity(veto: Record<string, unknown>) {
  const keys = Object.keys(recordValue(veto.tiers))
  if (keys.includes("A")) return "A"
  return keys.find((key) => key !== "record_only") ?? keys[0] ?? "A"
}

function QualityCapModuleEditor(props: RuleModuleEditorProps) {
  const veto = recordValue(props.content.high_score_veto)
  const defect = recordValue(props.content.hard_defect_penalty)
  const rules = arrayValue(veto.rules).map(recordValue)
  const patch = (path: Path, value: unknown) => props.onChange(updateRulePath(props.content, path, value))
  return (
    <OperatorEditorFrame props={props} guidance="把高分限制和硬伤扣分分开维护；有业务例外时，新增一条清晰的例外说明。">
      <div className="space-y-5">
        <OperatorSection title="高分限制" description="达到触发条件后，把最终分限制在指定上限。">
          <div className="space-y-4"><ToggleField label="启用高分限制" checked={booleanValue(veto.enabled)} onChange={(value) => patch(["high_score_veto"], value ? enableLegacyHighScoreVeto(veto) : { ...veto, enabled: false })} /><div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2"><NumberField label="从多少分开始检查" value={numberValue(veto.threshold, 80)} min={0} max={100} suffix="分" onChange={(value) => patch(["high_score_veto", "threshold"], value)} /><NumberField label="最高限制到" value={numberValue(veto.cap_to, 79)} min={0} max={100} suffix="分" onChange={(value) => patch(["high_score_veto", "cap_to"], value)} /></div></div>
        </OperatorSection>
        <OperatorSection title="限制或例外条件" description="写成运营能复核的一句话。" action={<Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => patch(["high_score_veto", "rules"], [...rules, { key: `rule_${Date.now().toString(36)}`, description: "新条件", source: "hard_defects", severity: defaultVetoSeverity(veto) }])}><Plus />新增一条规则</Button>}>
          <div className="space-y-3">{rules.map((rule, index) => {
            const { isModifier, severity, capTo, cancelExemption } = qualityRuleEditorState(
              rule,
              veto,
              defaultVetoSeverity(veto),
            )
            const removeRule = () => patch(["high_score_veto", "rules"], rules.filter((_, itemIndex) => itemIndex !== index))
            return <RuleSentence key={index} when={<div className="space-y-3"><TextField label="出现这种质量问题" value={stringValue(rule.description)} onChange={(value) => patch(["high_score_veto", "rules", index, "description"], value)} /><SelectField label="信号来自调用A的哪个字段" value={stringValue(rule.source, "hard_defects")} options={[{ value: "hard_defects", label: "画质硬伤（hard_defects）" }, { value: "image_defects", label: "水印等图片缺陷（image_defects）" }]} onChange={(value) => patch(["high_score_veto", "rules", index, "source"], value)} /></div>} then={isModifier ? <div className="space-y-3"><div className="rounded-md border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-4 py-3"><p className="text-sm font-bold">继承已命中硬伤中的最强档位</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">这条规则只修正同时命中的硬伤，不会单独限制分数。</p></div><ToggleField label="命中时取消高分豁免" hint="开启后，本条条件与任一普通硬伤同时命中时，不再应用高分豁免。" checked={cancelExemption} onChange={(value) => props.onChange(updateQualityModifierCancelExemption(props.content, index, value))} /><div className="flex justify-end"><Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={removeRule}><Trash />删除</Button></div></div> : <div className="space-y-3"><SelectField label="按哪档处理" value={severity} options={vetoTierOptions(veto)} hint="档位与上限在「高级设置」的 tiers 里维护" onChange={(value) => patch(["high_score_veto", "rules", index, "severity"], value)} /><div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-3 text-sm"><span>{capTo === null ? "仅记录，不压分" : `最终分不高于 ${capTo} 分`}</span><Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={removeRule}><Trash />删除</Button></div></div>} />
          })}</div>
        </OperatorSection>
        <OperatorSection title="硬伤扣分" description="每命中一项硬伤，扣除固定分数。"><div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2"><ToggleField label="启用硬伤扣分" checked={booleanValue(defect.enabled, true)} onChange={(value) => patch(["hard_defect_penalty", "enabled"], value)} /><NumberField label="每命中一项扣" value={numberValue(defect.per_hit, 10)} min={0} max={100} suffix="分" onChange={(value) => patch(["hard_defect_penalty", "per_hit"], value)} /></div></OperatorSection>
      </div>
    </OperatorEditorFrame>
  )
}

function DimensionScoringModuleEditor(props: RuleModuleEditorProps) {
  const dimensions = recordValue(props.content.subcategory_dimensions)
  const patch = (path: Path, value: unknown) => props.onChange(updateRulePath(props.content, path, value))
  const profileTracks = Array.isArray(dimensions.tracks) ? dimensions.tracks : null
  const dimensionEntries = Object.entries(dimensions).filter(([, value]) => value != null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value))
  return (
    <OperatorEditorFrame props={props} guidance="这里优先展示赛道和分数上限；复杂维度明细与扣分合同仍可在高级设置中完整维护。">
      <div className="space-y-5">
        {profileTracks && <OperatorSection title="适用赛道" description="每行一个需要使用这套维度计分的赛道。"><LinesField label="赛道" value={profileTracks} onChange={(value) => patch(["subcategory_dimensions", "tracks"], value)} /></OperatorSection>}
        <OperatorSection title="各赛道维度上限" description="模型判断完成后，系统按这里的上限计算维度得分。">
          <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">{dimensionEntries.map(([key, value]) => { const config = recordValue(value); return <div key={key} className="rounded-md border border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 p-4"><p className="text-sm font-bold">{stringValue(config.sub_category_key, key)}</p><div className="mt-3"><NumberField label="维度最高分" value={numberValue(config.dimension_max, 100)} min={0} max={100} suffix="分" onChange={(next) => patch(["subcategory_dimensions", key, "dimension_max"], next)} /></div></div> })}{!dimensionEntries.length && !profileTracks && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">暂无可简化展示的维度，完整内容请查看高级设置。</p>}</div>
        </OperatorSection>
        <div className="rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5"><strong>扣分明细会进入运行证据和纠偏页面。</strong><span className="ml-1 text-[var(--muted)]">请使用业务可理解的维度名称，便于人工逐条纠偏。</span></div>
      </div>
    </OperatorEditorFrame>
  )
}

function AnchorCalibrationModuleEditor(props: RuleModuleEditorProps) {
  const draft: Editable = {
    category_key: props.categoryKey,
    display_name: props.definition?.display_name ?? "锚点校准",
    contract: { anchor_mechanism: clone(props.content) },
    classification_map: {},
    subcategory_dimensions: {},
  }
  const patch = (mutator: (next: Editable) => void) => {
    const next = clone(draft)
    mutator(next)
    const block = recordValue(next.contract.anchor_mechanism)
    props.onChange(Object.keys(block).length ? block : {
      enabled: false,
      max_anchor_images: 5,
      anchors: [],
    })
  }
  return (
    <OperatorEditorFrame props={props} guidance="直接从素材库选择图片并标注它代表的等级；素材身份会随版本一起冻结。">
      <AnchorMechanismEditor draft={draft} onPatch={patch} />
    </OperatorEditorFrame>
  )
}

function ScoreLevelMatcherModuleEditor(props: RuleModuleEditorProps) {
  const levels = arrayValue(props.content.levels).map(recordValue)
  const patch = (path: Path, value: unknown) => props.onChange(updateRulePath(props.content, path, value))
  return (
    <OperatorEditorFrame props={props} guidance="等级按最低分从高到低匹配。调整边界时，请确认区间连续且没有两个等级使用同一最低分。">
      <OperatorSection title="分数对应等级" description="系统固定提供 L1 至 L5；可关闭不使用的等级，并调整其最低分。">
        <div className="space-y-3">{levels.map((level, index) => {
          const key = stringValue(level.level, `L${index + 1}`)
          const enabled = booleanValue(level.enabled, true)
          return <RuleSentence key={key} when={<div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">{enabled ? <NumberField label="总分至少达到" value={numberValue(level.min_score)} min={0} max={100} suffix="分" onChange={(value) => patch(["levels", index, "min_score"], value)} /> : <div className="flex min-h-16 items-center border border-dashed border-[var(--line)] px-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)] rounded-md">停用后不参与分数匹配</div>}<ToggleField label="参与匹配" checked={enabled} onChange={(value) => props.onChange(toggleLevelParticipation(props.content, key, value))} /></div>} then={<div className="min-w-48 space-y-3"><p className="text-xs font-bold">判定等级：{key}</p><TextField label="运营显示名称" value={stringValue(level.display_name, key)} onChange={(value) => patch(["levels", index, "display_name"], value)} /></div>} />
        })}</div>
      </OperatorSection>
    </OperatorEditorFrame>
  )
}

function normalizeKey(value: string) {
  return value.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_]+/g, "_").replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "")
}

const specializedEditors: Partial<Record<string, ComponentType<RuleModuleEditorProps>>> = {
  eligibility_redline: RedlineModuleEditor,
  track_routing: TrackRoutingModuleEditor,
  media_adjustment: MediaAdjustmentModuleEditor,
  quality_cap_exception: QualityCapModuleEditor,
  dimension_scoring: DimensionScoringModuleEditor,
  anchor_calibration: AnchorCalibrationModuleEditor,
  score_level_matcher: ScoreLevelMatcherModuleEditor,
}

export function RuleModuleEditor(props: RuleModuleEditorProps) {
  const Editor = specializedEditors[props.moduleTypeKey] ?? SchemaDrivenRuleModuleEditor
  return <Editor {...props} />
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/mechanism-config/types.ts =====
import type { ComponentType } from "react"

export type JsonObject = Record<string, any>

export type MechanismProfileDescription = {
  profile_type: string | null
  source: "explicit" | "legacy_image_shape" | "unresolved"
  supported: boolean
  editable: boolean
  reason: string | null
  version: string | null
  capabilities: string[]
  editor_route: string | null
  read_only_fallback: boolean
  can_execute: boolean
}

export type MechanismProfileCatalogItem = {
  profile_type: string
  version: string
  capabilities: string[]
  editor_route: string | null
  read_only_fallback: boolean
  editable: boolean
  can_execute: boolean
}

export type ConfigSummary = {
  id: number
  category_key: string
  display_name: string
  status: string
  revision: number
  contract_hash: string
  projected_revision_id: number
  candidate_count: number
  media_penalty_enabled: boolean
  updated_at: string
}

export type ConfigDetail = ConfigSummary & {
  mechanism_profile: MechanismProfileDescription
  contract: JsonObject
  classification_map: JsonObject
  subcategory_dimensions: Record<string, JsonObject>
  dimension_deduction_rules: Record<string, JsonObject>
  created_by: string
  created_at: string
  semantic_tag_applicability?: {
    contract_id: number
    contract_version: number
    contract_hash: string
    field_counts: Record<"required" | "optional" | "not_applicable", number>
    fields: Record<string, "required" | "optional" | "not_applicable">
  } | null
}

export type ConfigRevision = {
  id: number
  category_key: string
  display_name: string
  status: "draft" | "candidate" | "active" | "retired"
  revision: number
  parent_revision_id: number | null
  contract_hash: string
  mechanism_profile: MechanismProfileDescription
  contract: JsonObject
  classification_map: JsonObject
  subcategory_dimensions: Record<string, JsonObject>
  dimension_deduction_rules: Record<string, JsonObject>
  media_penalty_enabled: boolean
  created_by: string
  created_at: string
  updated_at: string
}

export type Editable = {
  category_key: string
  display_name: string
  contract: JsonObject
  classification_map: JsonObject
  subcategory_dimensions: Record<string, JsonObject>
}

export type ValidationErrorItem = {
  target: string
  code: string
  message: string
}

export type MechanismEditorProps = {
  workflowKind: "incremental" | "stock"
  draft: Editable
  runtimeRevision: ConfigRevision | null
  selectedRevision: ConfigRevision | null
  busy: boolean
  banner: string | null
  errors: ValidationErrorItem[]
  onPatch: (mutator: (next: Editable) => void) => void
  onValidate: () => void
  onCreateCandidate: () => void
}

export type MechanismEditorPlugin = {
  profileType: string
  canEdit: boolean
  Editor: ComponentType<MechanismEditorProps>
  buildSummary: (revision: ConfigRevision | null) => string
  prepareForSave?: (draft: Editable) => Editable
}

export function cloneEditable<T>(value: T): T {
  return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)) as T
}

export function revisionToEditable(revision: ConfigRevision): Editable {
  return {
    category_key: revision.category_key,
    display_name: revision.display_name,
    contract: cloneEditable(revision.contract),
    classification_map: cloneEditable(revision.classification_map),
    subcategory_dimensions: cloneEditable(revision.subcategory_dimensions),
  }
}

export function isNewMechanismDraft(
  runtimeRevision: ConfigRevision | null,
  selectedRevision: ConfigRevision | null,
): boolean {
  return runtimeRevision === null && selectedRevision === null
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/mechanism-config/unknown-mechanism-summary.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"

import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"

import type { ConfigRevision } from "./types"

export function UnknownMechanismSummary({
  detail,
  reason,
}: {
  detail: ConfigRevision | null
  reason?: string
}) {
  const [jsonOpen, setJsonOpen] = useState(false)
  const explanation = reason
    ?? detail?.mechanism_profile.reason
    ?? "当前版本不支持结构化编辑，请先确认机制插件与等级规则版本。"

  return (
    <section className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-5 py-6">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <Badge tone="warning">只读安全降级</Badge>
        {detail && <span className="visual-code text-xs">revision {detail.revision}</span>}
      </div>
      <h2 className="visual-card-title mt-4">当前版本不支持结构化编辑</h2>
      <p className="mt-3 max-w-[72ch] text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">{explanation}</p>
      <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">系统不会执行未知代码，也不会猜测字段结构、写回运行时投影或创建候选版本。</p>
      {detail && (
        <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setJsonOpen(true)}>查看完整 JSON</Button>
        </div>
      )}
      {detail && (
        <SecondaryDrawer
          open={jsonOpen}
          onOpenChange={setJsonOpen}
          title={`${detail.category_key} · revision ${detail.revision}`}
          description="只读等级规则工件。未知机制不会猜测字段结构，也不会写回运行时投影。"
        >
          <pre className="overflow-x-auto whitespace-pre-wrap break-words bg-alias-surface-036 p-4 font-data text-xs leading-6">
            {JSON.stringify({
              contract: detail.contract,
              classification_map: detail.classification_map,
              subcategory_dimensions: detail.subcategory_dimensions,
            }, null, 2)}
          </pre>
        </SecondaryDrawer>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/model-cost/hardware-cost-panel.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { api, jsonBody } from "@/lib/api"
import type { HardwareAssumptions, HardwareCostEstimate, HardwareCostProfile } from "@/lib/types"

const root = "/api/local-hardware-cost"
const fields: Array<[keyof Omit<HardwareAssumptions, "evidence_reference">, string]> = [
  ["purchase_yuan", "分摊设备成本（元）"], ["residual_yuan", "预计残值（元）"],
  ["depreciation_months", "折旧期限（月）"], ["allocated_hours_per_month", "每月成本分摊时数（小时）"],
  ["average_power_kw", "整机平均功率（千瓦）"], ["electricity_yuan_per_kwh", "电价（元/千瓦时）"],
  ["operations_yuan_per_month", "每月运维分摊（元）"], ["successful_materials_per_hour", "整机每小时成功素材数"],
]
const yuan = (n: number) => `¥${n.toLocaleString("zh-CN", { maximumFractionDigits: 6 })}`

export function HardwareCostPanel() {
  const profile = useQuery({ queryKey: ["hardware-cost", "qwen205"], queryFn: () => api<HardwareCostProfile>(`${root}/qwen205`) })
  return <section className="rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white p-card">
    <h2 className="visual-card-title">本地 Qwen · 整机成本估算</h2>
    <p className="mt-2 max-w-4xl text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">按设备成本、电价和同规格工作流的有效吞吐量估算。历史 Token 占位计价用于调用预算控制，不能代表本地设备的真实费用。</p>
    {profile.isPending && <p className="mt-4 text-sm">读取成本参数…</p>}
    {profile.isError && <p role="alert" className="mt-4 text-sm text-danger">读取失败：{profile.error.message}</p>}
    {profile.data && <HardwareCostForm key={profile.data.revision} profile={profile.data} onSaved={() => { void profile.refetch() }} />}
  </section>
}

function HardwareCostForm({ profile, onSaved }: { profile: HardwareCostProfile; onSaved: () => void }) {
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false)
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(profile.assumptions ?? {}).map(([k, v]) => [k, String(v)])))
  const [count, setCount] = useState("1")
  const [result, setResult] = useState<HardwareCostEstimate | null>(profile.estimate)
  const complete = fields.every(([k]) => Boolean(draft[k]?.trim())) && Boolean(draft.evidence_reference?.trim())
  const assumptions = () => ({ ...draft, depreciation_months: Number(draft.depreciation_months) }) as HardwareAssumptions
  const preview = useMutation({ mutationFn: () => api<HardwareCostEstimate>(`${root}/estimate`, { method: "POST", ...jsonBody({ assumptions: assumptions(), material_count: Number(count) }) }), onSuccess: setResult, onError: e => toast.error(e.message) })
  const save = useMutation({ mutationFn: () => api(`${root}/qwen205`, { method: "POST", ...jsonBody({ expected_revision: profile.revision, assumptions: assumptions() }) }),
    onSuccess: () => { toast.success("成本参数已保存新版本"); onSaved() }, onError: e => toast.error(e.message) })
  const change = (k: string, v: string) => { setDraft(d => ({ ...d, [k]: v })); setResult(null) }
  return <div className="mt-4 space-y-4 text-sm">
    <p className="break-words">{profile.configured ? `已配置 · 参数版本 ${profile.revision} · ${profile.actor ?? "管理员"}` : "未配置：尚无设备成本、电价和有效吞吐量的可靠参数。"}</p>
    {result && <div className="grid gap-4 border-y border-[var(--line)] py-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
      <p>整机每小时<span className="mt-1 block text-xl font-bold">{yuan(result.hourly_yuan)}</span></p>
      <p>单张成功素材<span className="mt-1 block text-xl font-bold">{yuan(result.per_material_yuan)}</span></p>
      <p>{result.material_count.toLocaleString()} 张情景估算<span className="mt-1 block text-xl font-bold">{yuan(result.total_yuan)}</span><span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">约 {result.estimated_hours.toLocaleString()} 小时</span></p>
    </div>}
    {result && <ul className="space-y-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{result.notes.map(note => <li key={note}>{note}</li>)}</ul>}
    <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setEditing(!editing)}>{editing ? "收起参数" : "查看参数 / 情景测算"}</Button>
    {editing && <div className="space-y-4">
      <p className="max-w-4xl text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">吞吐量按整台机器的成功素材数计算，包含失败、重试和空闲的影响；不能将各并发请求耗时相加后计算整机费用。填写 0 表示该项成本确定为零，留空表示未知。</p>
      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">{fields.map(([k, label]) => <label key={k} className="min-w-0">{label}<Input className="mt-1 w-full" inputMode="decimal" value={draft[k] ?? ""} onChange={e => change(k, e.target.value)} /></label>)}</div>
      <label className="block">参数和吞吐量的依据（文件完整路径、统计区间及配置）<Input className="mt-1" value={draft.evidence_reference ?? ""} maxLength={1000} onChange={e => change("evidence_reference", e.target.value)} /></label>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3"><label className="w-48 max-w-full">本次估算素材数<Input className="mt-1" inputMode="numeric" value={count} onChange={e => { setCount(e.target.value); setResult(null) }} /></label>
        <Button variant="secondary" disabled={!complete || !count.trim() || preview.isPending || save.isPending} onClick={() => preview.mutate()}>计算情景估算</Button>
        {profile.can_edit && <Button disabled={!complete || save.isPending || preview.isPending} onClick={() => save.mutate()}>保存参数新版本</Button>}
      </div>
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">测算不发起模型调用；保存参数不改动历史费用或评测结果。</p>
    </div>}
  </div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/model-registry/anchor-usage-panel.tsx =====
import { ImageSquare, Info, Lightning, Repeat, Resize } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { modelMediaUsageApi } from "@/lib/api"

function formatBytes(value: number) {
  if (value < 1024) return `${value} B`
  if (value < 1024 ** 2) return `${(value / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`
  if (value < 1024 ** 3) return `${(value / 1024 ** 2).toFixed(1)} MB`
  return `${(value / 1024 ** 3).toFixed(2)} GB`
}

function formatCount(value: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("zh-CN").format(value)
}

function formatPricingUnits(micros: number) {
  return (micros / 1_000_000).toLocaleString("zh-CN", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 4,
    maximumFractionDigits: 4,
  })
}

export function AnchorUsagePanel() {
  const summary = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["model-media-usage", 7],
    queryFn: () => modelMediaUsageApi.summary(7),
    refetchInterval: 60_000,
  })
  const totals = summary.data?.totals

  return (
    <section className="rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white" aria-label="图片与锚点用量">
      <div className="grid gap-7 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-6 lg:grid-cols-[230px_1fr] lg:px-7">
        <div>
          <div className="flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-primary">
            <ImageSquare size={21} weight="bold" />
          </div>
          <h2 className="visual-card-title mt-5">图片与锚点用量</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">
            查看最近 7 天真实模型调用中的目标图、锚点图、发送尺寸与图片 Token。这里用于观察和降本，不会因为预估费用中断正常运行。
          </p>
        </div>

        {summary.isPending ? (
          <div className="h-44 animate-pulse bg-surface-sunken" aria-label="正在读取图片用量" />
        ) : summary.isError ? (
          <div className="flex min-h-44 flex-col items-start justify-center border border-warning bg-warning-wash p-5 rounded-md">
            <p className="font-semibold">图片用量暂时读取失败</p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">不影响模型调用，可稍后重新读取。</p>
            <Button className="mt-4" size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => summary.refetch()}>重新读取</Button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="grid gap-px border border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4 rounded-md overflow-hidden">
              <UsageFact
                icon={<ImageSquare size={18} />}
                label="已记录图片"
                value={`${formatCount(totals?.image_count ?? 0)} 张`}
                detail={`目标图 ${formatCount(totals?.target_count ?? 0)} · 锚点图 ${formatCount(totals?.anchor_count ?? 0)}`}
              />
              <UsageFact
                icon={<Lightning size={18} />}
                label="图片 Token"
                value={formatCount(totals?.image_tokens ?? 0)}
                detail={`${formatCount(totals?.invocation_count ?? 0)} 次模型调用`}
              />
              <UsageFact
                icon={<Resize size={18} />}
                label="传输体积减少"
                value={formatBytes(totals?.saved_bytes ?? 0)}
                detail={`${formatBytes(totals?.original_bytes ?? 0)} → ${formatBytes(totals?.sent_bytes ?? 0)}`}
              />
              <UsageFact
                icon={<Repeat size={18} />}
                label="缓存命中"
                value={`${formatCount(totals?.cache_hit_count ?? 0)} 张`}
                detail={`首次生成 ${formatCount(totals?.cache_miss_count ?? 0)} · 回退原图 ${formatCount(totals?.fallback_count ?? 0)}`}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-3">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <Badge tone="success">供应商实际 {formatCount(summary.data?.usage_sources.provider_actual.image_count ?? 0)} 张</Badge>
                <Badge tone="warning">官方估算 {formatCount(summary.data?.usage_sources.official_estimate.image_count ?? 0)} 张</Badge>
                <Badge tone="neutral">供应商未返回 {formatCount(summary.data?.usage_sources.unavailable.image_count ?? 0)} 张</Badge>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold">
                图片输入预估计价 {formatPricingUnits(totals?.estimated_cost_micros ?? 0)} 单位
              </p>
            </div>
            <p className="mt-3 flex items-start gap-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
              <Info className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" size={15} />
              供应商返回分图用量时记为“供应商实际”；只返回整次图片 Token 时，按发送像素分摊并明确标为“官方估算”。
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

function UsageFact({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  detail,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode
  label: string
  value: string
  detail: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 bg-white p-4">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{icon}{label}</div>
      <p className="font-data mt-3 text-2xl font-bold">{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 break-words text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{detail}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/model-registry/pricing-contract-editor.tsx =====
import { Plus, Trash } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import type { ModelPricingContract, ModelPricingTier } from "@/lib/types"
import {
  doubaoSeed20LitePricingPreset,
  isPricingContract,
  microsToYuanPer1k,
  supportsDoubaoSeed20LitePricingPreset,
  validatePricingContract,
  yuanPer1kToMicros,
} from "./pricing-contract"

function nextTier(
  previous: ModelPricingTier | undefined,
  index: number,
  schemaVersion: ModelPricingContract["schema_version"],
): ModelPricingTier {
  const minimum = previous ? previous.max_input_tokens + 1 : 0
  return {
    tier_key: `${minimum}-new-${index + 1}`,
    min_input_tokens: minimum,
    max_input_tokens: minimum + 31_999,
    input_micros_per_1k: previous?.input_micros_per_1k ?? 0,
    ...(schemaVersion === "model-pricing-v3"
      ? { cached_input_micros_per_1k: previous?.cached_input_micros_per_1k ?? 0 }
      : {}),
    output_micros_per_1k: previous?.output_micros_per_1k ?? 0,
  }
}

export function PricingContractEditor({
  value,
  onChange,
  provider,
  modelId,
}: {
  value: ModelPricingContract | Record<string, unknown>
  onChange: (contract: ModelPricingContract | Record<string, never>) => void
  provider: string
  modelId: string
}) {
  const contract = isPricingContract(value) ? value : null
  const supportsOfficialPreset = supportsDoubaoSeed20LitePricingPreset(provider, modelId)
  const validation = contract ? validatePricingContract(contract) : null
  const patch = <K extends keyof ModelPricingContract>(key: K, next: ModelPricingContract[K]) => {
    if (contract) onChange({ ...contract, [key]: next })
  }
  const patchTier = (index: number, next: ModelPricingTier) => {
    if (!contract) return
    patch("tiers", contract.tiers.map((tier, current) => current === index ? next : tier))
  }

  return (
    <section className="space-y-4 border-y border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-4" aria-label="模型阶梯计价">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h3 className="visual-item-title">阶梯计价</h3>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">价格按整次请求的输入 token 选择档位，普通输入价、缓存命中价与输出价均按元 / 1k tokens 填写。</p>
        </div>
        <Badge tone={!contract ? "neutral" : validation?.ok ? "success" : "danger"}>{!contract ? "旧版平价" : validation?.ok ? "合同有效" : "合同有误"}</Badge>
      </div>
      {!contract ? (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">当前保留旧版单一价格。加载官方预设只会写入当前草稿，仍需在抽屉底部显式保存模型才会生效。</p>
          {supportsOfficialPreset ? (
            <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={() => onChange(doubaoSeed20LitePricingPreset())}>加载豆包 Seed 2.0 Lite 官方预设（仍需显式保存）</Button>
          ) : (
            <p className="border-l-2 border-[var(--line-strong)] pl-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">豆包 Seed 2.0 Lite 官方预设仅在供应商和模型 ID 匹配时显示。</p>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Field label="模型计价标识"><Input value={contract.model_key} readOnly aria-readonly="true" /></Field>
            <Field label="生效时间"><Input className="focus-within:outline-2 focus-within:outline-solid focus-within:outline-ink focus-within:outline-offset-2 focus-within:ring-4 focus-within:ring-brand" type="datetime-local" value={contract.effective_at.slice(0, 16)} onChange={(event) => patch("effective_at", `${event.target.value}:00+08:00`)} /></Field>
          </div>
          <Field label="官方来源"><Input type="url" value={contract.source_url} onChange={(event) => patch("source_url", event.target.value)} /></Field>
          <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-[var(--line)] bg-white rounded-md">
            <div className="min-w-[880px]">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_0.75fr_0.75fr_1fr_1fr_1fr_44px] gap-2 border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-[var(--muted)]">
                <span>计价档位</span><span>输入下界</span><span>输入上界</span><span>普通输入价</span><span>缓存命中价</span><span>输出价</span><span />
              </div>
              {contract.tiers.map((tier, index) => (
                <div key={`${tier.tier_key}-${index}`} className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_0.75fr_0.75fr_1fr_1fr_1fr_44px] gap-2 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-2 last:border-b-0">
                  <Input aria-label={`档位 ${index + 1} 标识`} value={tier.tier_key} onChange={(event) => patchTier(index, { ...tier, tier_key: event.target.value })} />
                  <Input aria-label={`档位 ${index + 1} 输入下界`} type="number" min="0" value={tier.min_input_tokens} onChange={(event) => patchTier(index, { ...tier, min_input_tokens: Number(event.target.value) })} />
                  <Input aria-label={`档位 ${index + 1} 输入上界`} type="number" min="0" value={tier.max_input_tokens} onChange={(event) => patchTier(index, { ...tier, max_input_tokens: Number(event.target.value) })} />
                  <PriceInput label={`档位 ${index + 1} 普通输入价格`} value={tier.input_micros_per_1k} onChange={(next) => patchTier(index, { ...tier, input_micros_per_1k: next })} />
                  {contract.schema_version === "model-pricing-v3" ? (
                    <PriceInput label={`档位 ${index + 1} 缓存命中价格`} value={tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k ?? 0} onChange={(next) => patchTier(index, { ...tier, cached_input_micros_per_1k: next })} />
                  ) : (
                    <span className="self-center text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">v2 未提供</span>
                  )}
                  <PriceInput label={`档位 ${index + 1} 输出价格`} value={tier.output_micros_per_1k} onChange={(next) => patchTier(index, { ...tier, output_micros_per_1k: next })} />
                  <Button type="button" size="icon" variant="ghost" aria-label={`删除档位 ${index + 1}`} disabled={contract.tiers.length === 1} onClick={() => patch("tiers", contract.tiers.filter((_item, current) => current !== index))}><Trash /></Button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => patch("tiers", [...contract.tiers, nextTier(contract.tiers.at(-1), contract.tiers.length, contract.schema_version)])}><Plus />增加档位</Button>
            <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => onChange({})}>改用旧版平价</Button>
          </div>
          {!validation?.ok && <p className="border-l-2 border-danger pl-3 text-xs leading-5 text-danger">{validation?.error}</p>}
          <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">阶梯合同会随每次调用冻结；历史费用不会因以后修改价格而变化。此区域不读取或序列化 API Key。</p>
        </>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}

function PriceInput({ label, value, onChange }: { label: string; value: number; onChange: (value: number) => void }) {
  return <Input aria-label={label} inputMode="decimal" value={microsToYuanPer1k(value)} onChange={(event) => { try { onChange(yuanPer1kToMicros(event.target.value)) } catch { /* Keep the last valid value while the operator types. */ } }} />
}

function Field({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return <label className="block"><span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">{label}</span>{children}</label>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/model-registry/pricing-contract.ts =====
import type { ModelPricingContract, ModelPricingTier } from "@/lib/types"

export type PricingValidation = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string }

const OFFICIAL_SOURCE = "https://docs.volcengine.com/docs/82379/1544106?lang=zh"
const VOLCENGINE_PROVIDERS = new Set(["ark", "doubao", "volcengine", "volcengine-ark"])

function normalizedIdentity(value: string): string {
  return value.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "")
}

export function supportsDoubaoSeed20LitePricingPreset(provider: string, modelId: string): boolean {
  const normalizedModel = normalizedIdentity(modelId)
  return VOLCENGINE_PROVIDERS.has(normalizedIdentity(provider))
    && (normalizedModel === "doubao-seed-2-0-lite" || normalizedModel.startsWith("doubao-seed-2-0-lite-"))
}

export function doubaoSeed20LitePricingPreset(): ModelPricingContract {
  return {
    schema_version: "model-pricing-v3",
    model_key: "doubao-seed-2.0-lite",
    currency: "CNY",
    unit_tokens: 1_000,
    source_url: OFFICIAL_SOURCE,
    effective_at: "2026-06-23T17:37:12+08:00",
    tiers: [
      { tier_key: "0-32000", min_input_tokens: 0, max_input_tokens: 32_000, input_micros_per_1k: 600, cached_input_micros_per_1k: 120, output_micros_per_1k: 3_600 },
      { tier_key: "32000-128000", min_input_tokens: 32_001, max_input_tokens: 128_000, input_micros_per_1k: 900, cached_input_micros_per_1k: 180, output_micros_per_1k: 5_400 },
      { tier_key: "128000-256000", min_input_tokens: 128_001, max_input_tokens: 256_000, input_micros_per_1k: 1_800, cached_input_micros_per_1k: 360, output_micros_per_1k: 10_800 },
    ],
  }
}

export function isPricingContract(value: unknown): value is ModelPricingContract {
  return Boolean(value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
    && ["model-pricing-v2", "model-pricing-v3"].includes(
      String((value as { schema_version?: unknown }).schema_version),
    ))
}

function nonnegativeInteger(value: unknown): value is number {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0
}

export function validatePricingContract(contract: ModelPricingContract): PricingValidation {
  if (!["model-pricing-v2", "model-pricing-v3"].includes(contract.schema_version)
    || contract.currency !== "CNY" || contract.unit_tokens !== 1_000) {
    return { ok: false, error: "阶梯计价只支持 CNY / 每 1000 tokens 的 v2 或 v3 合同" }
  }
  if (!contract.model_key.trim()) return { ok: false, error: "模型计价标识不能为空" }
  if (!contract.source_url.startsWith("https://")) return { ok: false, error: "官方来源必须是 HTTPS 地址" }
  if (!contract.effective_at || Number.isNaN(Date.parse(contract.effective_at))) return { ok: false, error: "生效时间必须是带时区的有效时间" }
  if (!contract.tiers.length || contract.tiers[0].min_input_tokens !== 0) return { ok: false, error: "价格档位必须从 0 token 开始" }
  const keys = new Set<string>()
  for (const [index, tier] of contract.tiers.entries()) {
    if (!tier.tier_key.trim() || keys.has(tier.tier_key)) return { ok: false, error: "每个价格档位必须有唯一标识" }
    keys.add(tier.tier_key)
    if (![tier.min_input_tokens, tier.max_input_tokens, tier.input_micros_per_1k, tier.output_micros_per_1k].every(nonnegativeInteger)) {
      return { ok: false, error: "Token 边界和价格必须是非负整数" }
    }
    if (contract.schema_version === "model-pricing-v3" && !nonnegativeInteger(tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k)) {
      return { ok: false, error: "v3 每个档位都必须配置非负整数缓存命中价格" }
    }
    if (contract.schema_version === "model-pricing-v3"
      && tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k! > tier.input_micros_per_1k) {
      return { ok: false, error: "缓存命中价格不得高于普通输入价格" }
    }
    if (tier.max_input_tokens < tier.min_input_tokens) return { ok: false, error: "价格档位上界不能小于下界" }
    if (index > 0 && tier.min_input_tokens !== contract.tiers[index - 1].max_input_tokens + 1) {
      return { ok: false, error: "价格档位必须连续且不可重叠" }
    }
    if (index > 0 && (tier.input_micros_per_1k < contract.tiers[index - 1].input_micros_per_1k
      || tier.output_micros_per_1k < contract.tiers[index - 1].output_micros_per_1k
      || (contract.schema_version === "model-pricing-v3"
        && tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k! < contract.tiers[index - 1].cached_input_micros_per_1k!))) {
      return { ok: false, error: "后一档普通输入、缓存输入与输出价格均不得低于前一档" }
    }
  }
  return { ok: true }
}

export function yuanPer1kToMicros(value: string): number {
  const text = value.trim()
  if (!/^\d+(?:\.\d{1,6})?$/.test(text)) throw new Error("价格最多保留 6 位小数")
  const [yuan, decimals = ""] = text.split(".")
  return Number(yuan) * 1_000_000 + Number(decimals.padEnd(6, "0"))
}

export function microsToYuanPer1k(value: number): string {
  if (!nonnegativeInteger(value)) return "0"
  return (value / 1_000_000).toFixed(6).replace(/\.?0+$/, "")
}

export function formatMicrosAsCny(micros: number): string {
  return `${(micros / 1_000_000).toLocaleString("zh-CN", { minimumFractionDigits: 6, maximumFractionDigits: 6 })} 元`
}

function ceilCost(tokens: number, microsPer1k: number): number {
  return Math.floor((tokens * microsPer1k + 999) / 1_000)
}

export function selectPricingTier(contract: ModelPricingContract, inputTokens: number): ModelPricingTier {
  const tier = contract.tiers.find((candidate) => inputTokens >= candidate.min_input_tokens && inputTokens <= candidate.max_input_tokens)
  if (!tier) throw new Error(`输入长度 ${inputTokens} tokens 不在计价合同覆盖范围内`)
  return tier
}

export function priceModelUsage(
  contract: ModelPricingContract,
  inputTokens: number,
  outputTokens: number,
  cachedInputTokens = 0,
) {
  const validation = validatePricingContract(contract)
  if (!validation.ok) throw new Error(validation.error)
  if (!nonnegativeInteger(inputTokens) || !nonnegativeInteger(outputTokens)
    || !nonnegativeInteger(cachedInputTokens) || cachedInputTokens > inputTokens) {
    throw new Error("输入、输出和缓存 token 必须是有效非负整数，且缓存 token 不得超过输入 token")
  }
  const tier = selectPricingTier(contract, inputTokens)
  const effectiveCachedTokens = contract.schema_version === "model-pricing-v3" ? cachedInputTokens : 0
  const uncachedInputTokens = inputTokens - effectiveCachedTokens
  const uncachedInputCostMicros = ceilCost(uncachedInputTokens, tier.input_micros_per_1k)
  const cachedInputCostMicros = ceilCost(effectiveCachedTokens, tier.cached_input_micros_per_1k ?? 0)
  const inputCostMicros = uncachedInputCostMicros + cachedInputCostMicros
  const outputCostMicros = ceilCost(outputTokens, tier.output_micros_per_1k)
  const fullPriceCostMicros = ceilCost(inputTokens, tier.input_micros_per_1k) + outputCostMicros
  const totalCostMicros = inputCostMicros + outputCostMicros
  return {
    tierKey: tier.tier_key,
    cachedInputTokens: effectiveCachedTokens,
    uncachedInputTokens,
    uncachedInputCostMicros,
    cachedInputCostMicros,
    inputCostMicros,
    outputCostMicros,
    totalCostMicros,
    fullPriceCostMicros,
    cacheSavingsMicros: Math.max(0, fullPriceCostMicros - totalCostMicros),
  }
}

export function pricingContractSummary(value: ModelPricingContract | Record<string, unknown>): string {
  if (!isPricingContract(value)) return "旧版平价"
  return `${value.tiers.length} 档 · ${value.currency} / 1k`
}

import { Link } from "react-router-dom"
import { AssetThumbnail } from "@/components/asset-thumbnail"

export function OperationsAttention({ items, loading, error, capacity, breakers, inline = false }: {
  items: FailureRow[]
  loading: boolean
  error: boolean
  capacity: RuntimeCapacity | undefined
  breakers: CircuitBreaker[]
  inline?: boolean
}) {
  const openBreakers = breakers.filter((item) => item.state === "open")
  return (
    <section data-testid="operations-attention" aria-labelledby="operations-attention-title" className={inline ? "operations-attention-inline min-w-0" : "min-w-0 rounded-lg border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-card py-card shadow-card"}>
      <h2 id="operations-attention-title" className="visual-card-title">需要重点关注</h2>
      <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
        {capacity?.backpressure.accepting_new_work === false && <div data-attention-alert="paused" role="alert" className="rounded-md border border-warning bg-warning-wash px-4 py-3 text-sm"><p className="font-bold">已暂停扩量</p><p className="mt-1 break-words text-warning">{capacity.backpressure.pause_reason}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">请先处理下游积压，再恢复扩量。</p></div>}
        {!!capacity?.outbox.overwrite_rejected && <div data-attention-alert="overwrite" role="alert" className="rounded-md border border-warning bg-warning-wash px-4 py-3 text-sm"><p className="font-bold">覆盖被护栏拒绝 <span className="font-data">{capacity.outbox.overwrite_rejected} 条</span></p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">旧版本迟到、或自动结果试图覆盖人工已审行</p></div>}
        {openBreakers.length > 0 && <div data-attention-alert="breakers" role="alert" className="rounded-md border border-warning bg-warning-wash px-4 py-3 text-sm"><span className="font-bold">熔断器</span><span className="ml-2 font-data">打开熔断 {openBreakers.length} 个</span></div>}
      </div>
      {error && <p role="alert" className="mt-4 text-sm text-danger">读取失败</p>}
      {loading ? <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">正在读取…</p> : items.length ? (
        <ul className="mt-4 divide-y divide-line">
          {items.slice(0, 5).map((row) => (
            <li key={row.production_run_id}>
              <Link data-attention-run={row.production_run_id} to="/workflow/failures" className="flex min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-4 rounded-md py-4 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary focus-visible:ring-offset-2">
                <AssetThumbnail assetId={row.asset_id} alt={row.asset_name || row.run_key} />
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 basis-56"><p className="break-all text-xs text-muted-foreground">{row.asset_name || row.run_key}</p><p className="mt-1 visual-code text-xs">run #{row.production_run_id}{row.asset_id ? ` · #${row.asset_id}` : ""}</p></div>
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 basis-40"><p className="break-all text-sm">{row.failed_step || "—"}</p>{row.error_code && <Badge tone="danger" className="mt-1">{row.error_code}</Badge>}</div>
                <time className="font-data text-xs text-muted-foreground" dateTime={row.updated_at ?? row.created_at ?? undefined}>{row.updated_at || row.created_at ? formatPlatformTime(row.updated_at ?? row.created_at) : "—"}</time>
              </Link>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : !error && <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">素材运行：没有需要操作的失败</p>}
    </section>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/operations-center/downstream-queue.tsx =====
import { ArrowRight } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { Fragment, useState } from "react"
import { Link } from "react-router-dom"
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"
import type { DownstreamPublishLedgerEntry } from "@/lib/types"
import { formatPlatformTime } from "@/lib/platform-time"

const ledgerTriggerLabel: Record<DownstreamPublishLedgerEntry["trigger_kind"], string> = { auto_run: "自动运行", human_correction: "人工纠偏" }
const ledgerDeliveryLabel: Record<DownstreamPublishLedgerEntry["delivery_status"], string> = { pending: "待写入", retrying: "重试中", delivered: "已写入", rejected: "被护栏拒绝", dead_lettered: "失败待处理" }

/** ADR-0064：下游只留最新一行，平台侧按发布逐行留痕（谁、何时、为何、改了哪些字段） */
export function DownstreamQueueSection({ preview = false, showHeading = true }: { preview?: boolean; showHeading?: boolean }) {
  const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState("")
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const trimmed = keyword.trim()
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ limit: "100", include_payload: "true" })
  if (/^\d+$/.test(trimmed)) params.set("source_id", trimmed)
  else if (trimmed) params.set("business_key", trimmed)
  const ledger = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["downstream-publish-ledger", trimmed],
    queryFn: () => api<{ items: DownstreamPublishLedgerEntry[] }>(`/api/downstream-publish-ledger?${params.toString()}`),
    refetchInterval: 10000,
  })
  const allItems = ledger.data?.items ?? []
  const items = preview ? allItems.slice(0, 5) : allItems
  return (
    <section data-testid="downstream-queue" className="min-w-0 rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line)] bg-white">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4">
        {showHeading && <div>
          <h2 className="visual-card-title">{preview ? "下游队列" : "下游写入变更日志"}</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">下游目标表每个素材只保留最新一行；这里按每次发布逐行留痕，同一素材会有多行（自动结果、人工纠偏覆盖、被护栏拒绝的尝试）。</p>
        </div>}
        {preview ? <Button asChild variant="secondary" size="sm"><Link to="/workflow/operations/detail?tab=downstream">查看全部<ArrowRight /></Link></Button> : <input
          value={keyword}
          onChange={(event) => setKeyword(event.target.value)}
          placeholder="按素材 ID 或业务键筛选"
          className="visual-native-input w-64"
        />}
      </div>
      {items.length ? (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className={`w-full table-fixed text-xs ${preview ? "operations-preview-table" : "operations-ledger-table min-w-[1320px]"}`}>
            {!preview && <colgroup>
              <col style={{ width: 160 }} /><col />
              <col style={{ width: 176 }} /><col style={{ width: 180 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 200 }} /><col style={{ width: 96 }} />
              <col style={{ width: 108 }} /><col style={{ width: 116 }} />
            </colgroup>}
            <thead className="bg-[var(--surface-sunken)] text-left text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-2">时间</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-2">素材</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-2">版本</th>
                {!preview && <th className="px-4 py-2">触发</th>}
                {!preview && <th className="px-4 py-2">变更字段</th>}
                {!preview && <th className="px-4 py-2">机制版本</th>}
                <th className="px-4 py-2">投递</th>
                {!preview && <th className="px-4 py-2"><span className="sr-only">操作</span></th>}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {items.map((item) => (
                <Fragment key={item.id}>
                  <tr className="border-t border-[var(--line)] align-top">
                    <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-2 font-data">{item.created_at ? formatPlatformTime(item.created_at) : "—"}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2"><span className="visual-code">{item.asset_identity || item.business_idempotency_key}</span><span className={`${preview ? "ml-1" : "mt-1 block"} text-[var(--muted)]`}>{item.category_key ?? ""}</span></td>
                    <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-2 font-data">r{item.revision}{item.previous_revision != null ? ` ← r${item.previous_revision}` : ""} · <Badge tone={item.review_state === "reviewed" ? "success" : "neutral"}>{item.review_state === "reviewed" ? "人工已审" : "自动"}</Badge></td>
                    {!preview && <td className="px-4 py-2">{ledgerTriggerLabel[item.trigger_kind]}{item.actor ? <span className="block visual-code text-[var(--muted)]">{item.actor}</span> : null}{item.reason ? <span className="block text-[var(--muted)]">{item.reason}</span> : null}</td>}
                    {!preview && <td className="px-4 py-2 font-data">{item.previous_revision == null ? "首次发布" : item.changed_paths.length ? item.changed_paths.slice(0, 6).join("、") + (item.changed_paths.length > 6 ? ` 等 ${item.changed_paths.length} 项` : "") : "内容无变化"}</td>}
                    {!preview && <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-2 visual-code">wv{item.mechanism_version_id}</td>}
                    <td className="px-4 py-2"><Badge tone={item.delivery_status === "delivered" ? "success" : item.delivery_status === "rejected" || item.delivery_status === "dead_lettered" ? "danger" : "warning"}>{ledgerDeliveryLabel[item.delivery_status]}</Badge>{!preview && item.rejection_code ? <span className="mt-1 block visual-code text-[var(--muted)]">{item.rejection_code}</span> : null}</td>
                    {!preview && <td className="px-4 py-2"><Button type="button" variant="secondary" size="sm" aria-expanded={expanded === item.id} onClick={() => setExpanded(expanded === item.id ? null : item.id)}>{expanded === item.id ? "收起" : "完整内容"}</Button></td>}
                  </tr>
                  {expanded === item.id ? (
                    <tr className="border-t border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--surface-sunken)]">
                      <td colSpan={preview ? 4 : 8} className="px-4 py-3">
                        <pre className="max-h-96 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap break-all visual-code text-xs">{JSON.stringify(item.payload ?? {}, null, 2)}</pre>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ) : null}
                </Fragment>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="px-5 py-4 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">{ledger.isLoading ? "加载中…" : "暂无下游写入记录。增量运行写下游后会在这里逐版留痕。"}</p>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}

function threeDDryRunGate(run: ProductionRunSummary) {
  if (run.current_step_key === "human_correction_gate") return "人工纠偏门"
  if (run.current_step_key === "label_fact_gate") return "标签事实发布门"
  if (run.status === "succeeded") return "双人工门已通过"
  if (run.status === "blocked") return "等待人工处理"
  return "自动链路执行中"
}

  const failures = (jobs.data?.items ?? []).filter((job) => job.status === "failed")
