"warning" : "normal"} />
            <CapacityFact label="等待写入下游" value={capacity.data ? `${capacity.data.outbox.queued + capacity.data.outbox.leased} 条` : "—"} hint={`最长已等待 ${queueAge(capacity.data?.outbox.oldest_queued_age_seconds ?? 0)}`} />
            <CapacityFact label="已写下游（自动 / 已审覆盖）" value={capacity.data ? `${capacity.data.outbox.published_auto ?? 0} / ${capacity.data.outbox.published_reviewed ?? 0} 条` : "—"} hint="先发后审：自动结果直接可用，人工纠偏后原地覆盖同一行" />
            <CapacityFact label="覆盖被护栏拒绝" value={capacity.data ? `${capacity.data.outbox.overwrite_rejected ?? 0} 条` : "—"} hint="旧版本迟到、或自动结果试图覆盖人工已审行" tone={capacity.data?.outbox.overwrite_rejected ? "warning" : "normal"} />
          </div>
          <div className="grid gap-px border-t border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--line)] lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_360px]">
            <div className="bg-white px-5 py-5 md:px-6">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold">各处理通道</p>
              <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
                {(capacity.data?.queues ?? []).map((queue) => (
                  <div key={queue.queue_class} className="border border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-3 text-xs rounded-md">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><span className="font-bold">{queueLabels[queue.queue_class]}</span><span className="font-data">{queue.leased}/{queue.effective_limit}</span></div>
                    <p className="mt-2 text-[var(--muted)]">排队 {queue.queued} · 最久 {queueAge(queue.oldest_queued_age_seconds)}</p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">近 5 分钟完成 {queue.completed_last_5m}</p>
                  </div>
                ))}
                {!capacity.data?.queues.length && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">正在读取处理通道…</p>}
              </div>
            </div>
            <aside className={capacity.data?.backpressure.paused ? "bg-warning-wash px-5 py-5 md:px-6" : "bg-active-surface px-5 py-5 md:px-6"}>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold">暂停原因</p>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">{capacity.data?.backpressure.pause_reason ?? "当前没有触发暂停，平台会按通道容量安全排队。"}</p>
              <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{capacity.data?.backpressure.accepting_new_work === false ? "请先处理下游积压，再恢复扩量。" : "可继续接收任务。"}</p>
            </aside>
          </div>
        </section>


        <section className="rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-card py-card">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div><p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">旧版任务调度证据</p><h2 className="visual-card-title mt-1">队列、配额与阻塞</h2></div>
            <Badge tone={queues.data?.control_paused ? "warning" : "success"}>{queues.data?.control_paused ? "全局暂停" : "调度可用"}</Badge>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-5 grid gap-px bg-[var(--line)] lg:grid-cols-5">
            {(queues.data?.queues ?? []).map((queue) => (
              <div key={queue.queue_class} className="bg-white px-4 py-4 text-xs">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2"><span className="font-bold">{queueLabels[queue.queue_class]}</span><Badge>{queue.running}/{queue.effective_limit}</Badge></div>
                <p className="mt-3 font-data text-xl font-bold">{queue.dispatchable_pending}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">可调度 / 总排队 {queue.pending_total}</p>
                <p className="mt-2 text-[var(--muted)]">熔断 {queue.blocked_by_breaker} · 重试延迟 {queue.delayed_by_retry_after}</p>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="visual-panel-group grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-[var(--line)] lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_360px]">
          <div className="bg-white px-5 py-6 md:px-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><ArrowsClockwise size={19} /><h2 className="visual-card-title">运行入口与恢复</h2></div>
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">增量与存量任务共用调度内核，但由工作流上下文隔离；暂停、重试、熔断和恢复不改变 Canonical 事实。</p>
            <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <Button asChild variant="secondary"><Link to="/workflow/incremental">增量运行<ArrowRight /></Link></Button>
              <Button variant="secondary" disabled={!allowRecovery} onClick={() => setFailureOpen(true)}>查看失败与恢复</Button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <aside className="bg-active-surface px-5 py-6 md:px-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Clock size={18} /><p className="text-xs font-bold">恢复事实</p></div>
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">恢复队列 {recoveryJobs.length} 个 · 延迟/重试任务 {retryJobs.length} 个 · 打开熔断 {openBreakers.length} 个。</p>
            <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">最后检查点：{control.data?.updated_at ? formatPlatformTime(control.data.updated_at) : "暂无"}</p>
          </aside>
        </section>

        <section data-testid="three-d-dry-run-summary" className="rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3 px-5 py-5 md:px-6">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">3D/SU 最近运行</p>
              <h2 className="visual-card-title mt-1">当前处理状态</h2>
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">一级只显示当前关口、状态和检查点；步骤尝试、冻结快照、失败恢复与影子证据进入二级抽屉。</p>
            </div>
            <Badge tone={threeDDryRun ? runtimeTone(threeDDryRun.status) : "neutral"}>{threeDDryRun ? runtimeStatusLabel(threeDDryRun.status) : "尚未运行"}</Badge>
          </div>
          {threeDDryRun ? (
            <div className="grid gap-px border-t border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--line)] md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-6">
              <RunFact label="类目" value={threeDDryRun.category_key ?? "model_3d_su"} />
              <RunFact label="工作流版本" value={threeDDryRun.workflow_version} />
              <RunFact label="当前关口" value={threeDDryRunGate(threeDDryRun)} />
              <RunFact label="最后检查点" value={threeDDryRun.last_checkpoint_id ? `#${threeDDryRun.last_checkpoint_id}` : "暂无"} />
              <RunFact label="阻塞原因" value={blockerLabel(threeDDryRun)} />
              <div className="flex items-center justify-end bg-white px-4 py-4">
                <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => onInspect(threeDDryRun)}>查看完整证据</Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="border-t border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">尚未产生运行记录。</div>
          )}
        </section>

        <DeadLetterPanel />
        </div>}
      </details>
      <SecondaryDrawer open={failureOpen} onOpenChange={setFailureOpen} title="失败与恢复" description="仅展示需要操作的任务摘要；完整执行证据仍在对应运行详情中。">
        <div className="space-y-5">
          <section>
            <h3 className="visual-item-title">旧版任务失败与重试</h3>
            <div className="mt-2 space-y-3">
              {failures.length ? failures.map((job) => (
                <div key={job.id} className="border-y border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-3">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3"><span className="font-data text-xs">#{job.id} · {queueLabels[job.queue_class]} · 尝试 {job.technical_attempt + 1}</span><Badge tone="danger">失败</Badge></div>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-danger">{job.error_message || "执行失败，请查看对应运行详情。"}</p>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">重试时间：{job.retry_after_at ? formatPlatformTime(job.retry_after_at) : "未安排"}</p>
                </div>
              )) : <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">暂无失败任务。</p>}
            </div>
          </section>
          <section>
            <h3 className="visual-item-title">熔断器</h3>
            <div className="mt-2 space-y-2">
              {(breakers.data?.items ?? []).length ? breakers.data?.items.map((item) => (
                <div key={item.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-y border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-3 text-xs"><span>{item.scope_type}:{item.scope_key} · {item.reason || "无原因"}</span><Badge tone={item.state === "open" ? "danger" : "success"}>{item.state}</Badge></div>
              )) : <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">暂无熔断记录。</p>}
            </div>
          </section>
        </div>
      </SecondaryDrawer>
    </>
  )
}

function Metric({ label, value, tone = "neutral" }: { label: string; value: string; tone?: "neutral" | "warning" | "success" }) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white px-5 py-4">
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="mt-2 font-data text-2xl font-bold">{value}</p>
      <Badge className="mt-2" tone={tone}>{tone === "success" ? "正常" : tone === "warning" ? "需要处理" : "实时"}</Badge>
    </div>
  )
}

function RunFact({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 bg-white px-4 py-4">
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 break-all font-data text-xs font-semibold">{value}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

function CapacityFact({ label, value, hint, tone = "normal" }: { label: string; value: string; hint: string; tone?: "normal" | "warning" }) {
  return (
    <div className={tone === "warning" ? "bg-warning-wash px-5 py-4" : "bg-white px-5 py-4"}>
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="mt-2 font-data text-xl font-bold">{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{hint}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/operations-center/presentation.ts =====
import type { Job, ProductionRunSummary } from "@/lib/types"

export const queueLabels: Record<Job["queue_class"], string> = {
  validation: "验证",
  interactive: "交互",
  production_batch: "生产批量",
  canary: "金丝雀",
  recovery: "恢复",
}

export function runtimeTone(status: ProductionRunSummary["status"]) {
  if (status === "succeeded") return "success" as const
  if (status === "failed" || status === "blocked") return "danger" as const
  if (status === "retryable" || status === "paused") return "warning" as const
  return "neutral" as const
}

export function blockerLabel(run: ProductionRunSummary) {
  const blocker = run.blockers[0]
  if (typeof blocker === "string") return blocker
  return blocker?.message ?? blocker?.code ?? run.error_message ?? "无"
}

export function humanDuration(seconds: number | null | undefined) {
  if (seconds === null || seconds === undefined) return "等待形成稳定处理速度"
  if (seconds <= 0) return "已清空"
  if (seconds < 60) return `约 ${Math.ceil(seconds)} 秒`
  if (seconds < 3600) return `约 ${Math.ceil(seconds / 60)} 分钟`
  return `约 ${Math.ceil(seconds / 3600)} 小时`
}

export function queueAge(seconds: number) {
  if (seconds <= 0) return "没有等待"
  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds} 秒`
  if (seconds < 3600) return `${Math.ceil(seconds / 60)} 分钟`
  return `${Math.ceil(seconds / 3600)} 小时`
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/operations-center/progress-board.tsx =====
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { Link } from "react-router-dom"
import type { RuntimeCapacity } from "@/lib/types"
import { humanDuration, queueAge } from "./presentation"

export type OperationsRunSummary = {
  queued: number
  running: number
  paused: number
  incremental_failed: number
  waiting_external: number
  generated_at: string
}

export function OperationsProgressBoard({ capacity, summary, capacityError, summaryError, children }: {
  capacity: RuntimeCapacity | undefined
  summary: OperationsRunSummary | undefined
  capacityError: boolean
  summaryError: boolean
  children?: ReactNode
}) {
  return (
    <section data-testid="operations-progress-board" aria-labelledby="operations-progress-title" className="rounded-lg border border-line-strong bg-white px-card py-card shadow-card">
      <h2 id="operations-progress-title" className="visual-card-title">处理进度看板</h2>
      <div className="operations-progress-grid mt-5">
        <ProgressFact
          fact="accepting"
          label="接活状态"
          value={capacityError ? "状态读取失败" : capacity ? capacity.backpressure.accepting_new_work ? "正常" : "已暂停" : "—"}
          hint={capacity?.backpressure.pause_reason ?? (capacity ? capacity.backpressure.accepting_new_work ? "可继续接收任务。" : "请先处理下游积压，再恢复扩量。" : "正在读取…")}
          warning={capacityError || capacity?.backpressure.accepting_new_work === false}
        />
        <ProgressFact fact="queued" label="等待排队" value={String(summary?.queued ?? "—")} hint={summaryError ? "摘要读取失败，请刷新。" : "运行摘要按原生素材运行统计，增量失败单列；处理通道统计的是节点任务。"} numeric />
        <ProgressFact fact="running" label="正在处理" value={String(summary?.running ?? "—")} hint={summaryError ? "摘要读取失败，请刷新。" : "当前处理状态"} numeric />
        <ProgressFact fact="downstream" label="等待下游" value={capacity ? `${capacity.outbox.queued + capacity.outbox.leased} 条` : "—"} hint={capacity ? `最长已等待 ${queueAge(capacity.outbox.oldest_queued_age_seconds)}` : "正在读取…"} numeric />
        <ProgressFact fact="dead-letters" label="失败待处理（增量）" value={capacity ? `${capacity.dead_letters.total} 条` : "—"} hint={<>已停止自动重跑，需人工确认后处理（<Link to="/workflow/operations/detail?tab=processing&legacy=1#dead-letters" className="inline-block underline focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary">调度与下游投递异常</Link>）{capacity?.dead_letters.regression ? `；另有存量回归失败 ${capacity.dead_letters.regression} 条，属实验数据不计入` : ""}</>} warning={!!capacity?.dead_letters.total} numeric />
        <ProgressFact fact="drain" label="预计清空时间" value={capacity ? humanDuration(capacity.estimated_drain_seconds) : "—"} hint="按最近 5 分钟实际处理速度估算" />
      </div>
      {children}
    </section>
  )
}

function ProgressFact({ fact, label, value, hint, warning = false, numeric = false }: {
  fact: string
  label: string
  value: string
  hint: ReactNode
  warning?: boolean
  numeric?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <div data-progress-fact={fact} className={`min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line px-3 py-4 ${warning ? "bg-warning-wash" : "bg-surface-sunken"}`}>
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground">{label}</p>
      <p className={`mt-3 break-words font-bold ${numeric ? "font-editorial text-3xl leading-tight" : "font-data text-lg leading-7"}`}>{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">{hint}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/operations-center/runtime-queue.tsx =====
import { ArrowRight } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { Link } from "react-router-dom"
import { AssetThumbnail } from "@/components/asset-thumbnail"
import type { ImagePreview } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Pager } from "@/components/ui/pager"
import { orderedColumns, TablePreferences } from "@/components/table-preferences"
import { runtimeStatusLabel } from "@/features/workflow-manager/operator-presentation"
import type { Paged } from "@/lib/paging"
import type { ProductionRunSummary, TablePreference } from "@/lib/types"
import { blockerLabel, queueLabels, runtimeTone } from "./presentation"

export const RUNS_PAGE_SIZE = 25
const columns = [
  { key: "image", label: "素材", width: 84, fixed: true },
  { key: "run", label: "运行 / 类目", width: 180 },
  { key: "workflow", label: "工作流版本", width: 108 },
  { key: "step", label: "当前步骤", width: 112 },
  { key: "checkpoint", label: "最后检查点", width: 108 },
  { key: "owner", label: "责任人", width: 92 },
  { key: "blocker", label: "阻塞原因", width: 150 },
  { key: "status", label: "状态 / 队列", width: 144 },
  { key: "actions", label: "操作", width: 116, fixed: true },
]

export function RuntimeQueueSection({ runs, page, loading, fetching, error, runsOffset = 0, preview = false, onInspect, onPreview, onPageChange, settings = {}, onSettingsChange, settingsReady = false, showHeading = true }: {
  showHeading?: boolean
  runs: ProductionRunSummary[]
  page: Paged<ProductionRunSummary> | undefined
  loading: boolean
  fetching: boolean
  error: boolean
  runsOffset?: number
  preview?: boolean
  onInspect: (run: ProductionRunSummary) => void
  onPreview: (preview: ImagePreview) => void
  onPageChange?: (offset: number) => void
  settings?: TablePreference
  onSettingsChange?: (next: Partial<TablePreference>) => void
  settingsReady?: boolean
}) {
  const visibleRuns = preview ? runs.slice(0, 5) : runs
  const visibleColumns = preview ? columns.filter((column) => ["image", "run", "step", "status"].includes(column.key))
    : orderedColumns(columns, settings).filter((column) => column.visible)
  const cell = (run: ProductionRunSummary, key: string) => {
    switch (key) {
      case "image": return <AssetThumbnail assetId={run.asset_id} alt={run.run_key} onPreview={onPreview} />
      case "run": return <><p className="break-all visual-code font-bold">{run.run_key}</p><p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">{run.category_key ?? "平台通用"}</p></>
      case "workflow": return <><p className="visual-code">v{run.workflow_version}</p><p className="mt-1 truncate text-[var(--muted)]" title={run.snapshot_hash}>{run.snapshot_hash.slice(0, 12)}</p></>
      case "step": return run.current_step_key ?? "已结束"
      case "checkpoint": return run.last_checkpoint_id ? "#" + run.last_checkpoint_id : "暂无"
      case "owner": return run.owner
      case "blocker": return blockerLabel(run)
      case "status": return <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2"><Badge tone={runtimeTone(run.status)}>{runtimeStatusLabel(run.status)}</Badge><Badge>{queueLabels[run.queue_class]}</Badge></div>
      case "actions": return <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => onInspect(run)}>查看证据</Button>
    }
  }
  return (
    <section data-testid="runtime-runs" className="min-w-0 rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
      {showHeading && <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3 px-5 py-5 md:px-6">
        <div>
          <h2 className="visual-card-title">{preview ? "处理队列" : "当前运行"}</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]"><span>工作流运行记录 · </span>脚本与工作流版本已冻结；这里只显示操作所需证据，完整快照和尝试历史进入详情抽屉。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {preview && <Button asChild variant="secondary" size="sm"><Link to="/workflow/operations/detail?tab=processing">查看全部<ArrowRight /></Link></Button>}
          <Badge tone={error ? "warning" : "success"}>{error ? "读取失败" : (page?.total ?? runs.length) + " 个运行"}</Badge>
        </div>
      </div>
      }
      {!preview && onSettingsChange && <div className="px-5 pb-4"><TablePreferences columns={columns} value={settings} onChange={onSettingsChange} disabled={!settingsReady} /></div>}
      <div className="overflow-x-auto border-t border-[var(--line)]">
        <table className={`w-full table-fixed text-left text-xs ${!preview && settings.density === "compact" ? "[&_td]:py-2" : ""}`} style={preview ? undefined : { minWidth: visibleColumns.reduce((sum, column) => sum + column.width, 0) }}>
          <colgroup>{visibleColumns.map((column) => <col key={column.key} style={preview ? undefined : { width: column.width }} />)}</colgroup>
          <thead className="bg-surface-sunken text-[var(--muted)]">
            <tr>
              {visibleColumns.map((column) => <th key={column.key} className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold">{column.label}</th>)}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {visibleRuns.map((run) => (
              <tr key={run.run_key} className="border-t border-[var(--line)] align-top">
                {visibleColumns.map((column) => <td key={column.key} className="break-words px-4 py-4">{cell(run, column.key)}</td>)}
              </tr>
            ))}
            {!runs.length && !loading && runsOffset > 0 && <tr><td colSpan={visibleColumns.length} className="px-5 py-8 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">这一页已经没有运行了。<Button className="ml-3" size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => onPageChange?.(0)}>回到第一页</Button></td></tr>}
            {!runs.length && !loading && runsOffset === 0 && <tr><td colSpan={visibleColumns.length} className="px-5 py-8 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">暂无本地 dry-run 运行。</td></tr>}
            {loading && <tr><td colSpan={visibleColumns.length} className="px-5 py-8 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">正在读取运行状态…</td></tr>}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
      {!preview && page && page.total > 0 && onPageChange && (
        <div className="border-t border-[var(--line)]">
          <Pager page={page} fallbackLimit={RUNS_PAGE_SIZE} onChange={onPageChange} loading={fetching} unit="个运行" />
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}

const LEVELS: BaselineLevel[] = ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"]
const LEVEL_LABELS: Record<BaselineLevel, string> = { L1: "好", L2: "中等", L3: "中差", L4: "极差", L5: "过滤" }
export const TRUTH_PAGE_SIZE = 50

/**
 * 基准集真值维护工作台（质量资产域）。
 *
 * 为什么不在存量回归页：真值是基准集本体的属性，与哪一轮回归无关；塞在
 * 发起回归的动线里运营找不到，也打断了跑回归的流程。
 * 为什么必须带图：不看图改等级等于盲改，运营只能凭文件名里的旧等级词猜。
 *
 * 条目由工作台自己分页取（页面级基准集详情是 include_items=false 的轻量版，
 * 上线首日就因为直接吃它而显示「没有匹配的素材」）；筛选走服务端 q 参数，
 * 文件名里本来就带导入时的等级词（好_/中等_/中差_/极差_/过滤_），按它筛就是按导入等级筛。
 * 每次保存追加一版修订，下一轮回归生效；已完成轮次不改写。
 */
export function BaselineTruthWorkbench({
  setId,
  contracts,
}: {
  setId: number
  contracts: readonly BaselineTruthContract[]
}) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState("")
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("")
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0)
  const [openIndex, setOpenIndex] = useState<number | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => { setQuery(keyword.trim()); setPage(0) }, 300)
    return () => clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [keyword])
  useEffect(() => { setPage(0); setKeyword(""); setQuery(""); setOpenIndex(null) }, [setId])

  const itemsQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["baseline-set-items", setId, page, query],
    queryFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.getSet(setId, true, {
      offset: page * TRUTH_PAGE_SIZE,
      limit: TRUTH_PAGE_SIZE,
      q: query,
    }),
    enabled: setId > 0,
    placeholderData: (previous) => previous,
  })
  const items: readonly BaselineSetItem[] = itemsQuery.data?.items ?? []
  const total = itemsQuery.data?.pagination.total ?? 0
  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / TRUTH_PAGE_SIZE))
  // 跨页翻页时 placeholderData 仍是上一页：加载完成前灯箱显示加载态，不能把上一页的素材当成当前素材
  const pageLoading = itemsQuery.isPlaceholderData
  const opened = truthLightboxItem(items, openIndex, pageLoading)

  const goPrev = () => {
    if (openIndex == null) return
    if (openIndex > 0) { setOpenIndex(openIndex - 1); return }
    if (page > 0) { setPage(page - 1); setOpenIndex(TRUTH_PAGE_SIZE - 1) }
  }
  const goNext = () => {
    if (openIndex == null) return
    if (openIndex < items.length - 1) { setOpenIndex(openIndex + 1); return }
    if (page + 1 < pageCount) { setPage(page + 1); setOpenIndex(0) }
  }
  // 翻到下一页后条目数可能少于上一页的索引，钳回去
  useEffect(() => {
    if (pageLoading) return
    if (openIndex != null && items.length && openIndex >= items.length) setOpenIndex(items.length - 1)
  }, [items.length, openIndex, pageLoading])

  return (
    <section aria-label="基准集真值维护" data-testid="baseline-truth-workbench">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] pb-3">
        <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
          点缩略图看原图并改等级；每次保存追加一版修订，<strong>下一轮回归生效</strong>，已完成轮次不改写。
          文件名前缀是导入时的等级词，输入「过滤_」即可只看导入为 L5 的素材。
        </p>
        <input
          aria-label="按素材名筛选"
          className="visual-native-input w-64"
          placeholder="按素材名筛选，如 过滤_ 或 15094385"
          value={keyword}
          onChange={(event) => setKeyword(event.target.value)}
        />
      </div>

      {!items.length ? (
        <p className="px-3 py-10 text-center text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          {itemsQuery.isPending ? "正在加载素材…" : itemsQuery.isError ? `素材加载失败：${(itemsQuery.error as Error).message}` : "没有匹配的素材"}
        </p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4" data-testid="truth-thumbnail-grid">
          {items.map((item, index) => {
            const previewUrl = truthPreviewUrl(item)
            return (
            <li key={item.id} className="flex gap-3 rounded-md border border-[var(--line)] bg-white p-2" data-testid={`truth-card-${item.id}`}>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label={`查看素材并改真值：${item.asset.name}`}
                className="visual-native-button p-0 shrink-0 self-start cursor-zoom-in focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
                onClick={() => setOpenIndex(index)}
              >
                {previewUrl ? (
                  <HoverPreview src={previewUrl} alt={item.asset.name}><img src={previewUrl} alt="" loading="lazy" className="block size-24 border border-[var(--line)] bg-white object-contain" /></HoverPreview>
                ) : (
                  <span className="flex size-24 items-center justify-center border border-[var(--line)] bg-white text-xs text-[var(--muted)] rounded-md">案例 / 文本</span>
                )}
              </button>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <p className="file-name break-all text-sm">{item.asset.name}</p>
                <p className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">素材 #<span className="visual-code">{item.asset_id}</span></p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs">{truthRowSummary(item)}</p>
                {(item.truth_revision ?? 0) > 0 && <Badge tone="active" className="mt-1">已修订</Badge>}
                <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" className="mt-2 h-auto min-h-11 w-full whitespace-normal px-2 py-2" onClick={() => setOpenIndex(index)}>
                  <PencilSimple />{item.truth_kind === "level" ? "看图改等级" : "查看 / 改字段"}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </li>
          )})}
        </ul>
      )}

      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]" data-testid="truth-pagination">
        <span>
          {query ? `筛选「${query}」匹配 ${total.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} 张` : `共 ${total.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} 张`}
          {total > 0 && ` · 第 ${page * TRUTH_PAGE_SIZE + 1}–${Math.min(total, (page + 1) * TRUTH_PAGE_SIZE)} 张`}
        </span>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" disabled={page <= 0} onClick={() => setPage((value) => Math.max(0, value - 1))}>上一页</Button>
          <span className="font-data">{page + 1} / {pageCount}</span>
          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" disabled={page + 1 >= pageCount} onClick={() => setPage((value) => value + 1)}>下一页</Button>
        </span>
      </div>

      <TruthLightbox
        setId={setId}
        open={openIndex != null}
        pageLoading={pageLoading}
        item={opened}
        contracts={contracts}
        position={openIndex != null ? { index: page * TRUTH_PAGE_SIZE + openIndex + 1, total } : null}
        hasPrev={!pageLoading && openIndex != null && (openIndex > 0 || page > 0)}
        hasNext={!pageLoading && openIndex != null && (openIndex < items.length - 1 || page + 1 < pageCount)}
        onPrev={goPrev}
        onNext={goNext}
        onClose={() => setOpenIndex(null)}
        onSaved={async () => {
          await Promise.all([
            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-set-items", setId] }),
            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-set", setId] }),
          ])
        }}
      />
    </section>
  )
}

/** 原图灯箱 + 就地改真值：看着图选等级、填原因、保存，然后直接翻下一张。 */
function TruthLightbox({
  setId,
  open,
  pageLoading,
  item,
  contracts,
  position,
  hasPrev,
  hasNext,
  onPrev,
  onNext,
  onClose,
  onSaved,
}: {
  setId: number
  open: boolean
  pageLoading: boolean
  item: BaselineSetItem | null
  contracts: readonly BaselineTruthContract[]
  position: { index: number; total: number } | null
  hasPrev: boolean
  hasNext: boolean
  onPrev: () => void
  onNext: () => void
  onClose: () => void
  onSaved: () => Promise<void>
}) {
  const [level, setLevel] = useState<BaselineLevel | "">("")
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<StructuredTruthDraft | null>(null)
  const [gradeDraft, setGradeDraft] = useState<ImageGradeDraft | null>(null)
  const [drawerOpen, setDrawerOpen] = useState(false)

  // 换到另一张时重置草稿，避免把上一张的选择带过去
  useEffect(() => {
    setLevel(item?.truth_kind === "level" ? (item.effective_truth?.expected_level ?? item.expected_level ?? "") : "")
    // 逐图档位是几十个平级取值，装不进通用的「字段 → 文本」草稿，单走一份。
    const imageGrade = item?.truth_contract_key === DIMENSION_GRADE_CONTRACT_KEY
    setDraft(
      item && item.truth_kind === "structured_fields" && !imageGrade
        ? truthDraftFromItem(item, contracts)
        : null,
    )
    setGradeDraft(item && imageGrade ? imageGradeDraftFromItem(item) : null)
    setReason("")
    setDrawerOpen(false)
  }, [item?.id]) // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  // 保存回调里判断“是否仍停留在被保存的那张”只能读 ref，闭包可能已被同步成下一张
  const currentItemId = useRef<number | null>(item?.id ?? null)
  currentItemId.current = item?.id ?? null

  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ item }: { item: BaselineSetItem | null }) => {
      if (!item) throw new Error("请先选择素材")
      if (!reason.trim()) throw new Error("请填写修改原因，会记入真值修订历史")
      const editKey = newEditKey(item.id)
      const payload = item.truth_kind === "level"
        ? (level ? levelTruthEditPayload(level, reason, editKey) : null)
        : gradeDraft
        ? imageGradeEditPayload(gradeDraft, reason, editKey)
        : (draft ? structuredTruthEditPayload(draft, contracts, reason, editKey) : null)
      if (!payload) {
        throw new Error(
          item.truth_kind === "level"
            ? "请选择等级"
            : gradeDraft
            ? "案例档位与每张图的档位都要选好才能保存"
            : "请先填写字段真值",
        )
      }
      return baselineRegressionApi.editItemTruth(setId, item.id, payload)
    },
    onSuccess: async (updated, { item: savedItem }) => {
      toast.success(`已保存人工真值（第 ${updated.truth_revision ?? 1} 版），下一轮回归生效`)
      await onSaved()
      if (savedItem && shouldAdvanceAfterTruthSave(savedItem.id, currentItemId.current, hasNext)) onNext()
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!item) return
    const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (drawerOpen || save.isPending || (event.target as HTMLElement | null)?.tagName === "INPUT") return
      if (event.key === "ArrowLeft" && hasPrev) onPrev()
      if (event.key === "ArrowRight" && hasNext) onNext()
    }
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey)
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey)
  }, [item, hasPrev, hasNext, onPrev, onNext, drawerOpen, save.isPending])

  const previewUrl = item ? truthPreviewUrl(item) : null
  const currentLevel = item?.effective_truth?.expected_level ?? item?.expected_level ?? null
  const changed = item?.truth_kind === "level"
    ? level !== "" && level !== currentLevel
    : gradeDraft != null || draft != null

  return (
    <Dialog.Root open={open} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && onClose()}>
      <Dialog.Portal>
        <Dialog.Overlay className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70" />
        <Dialog.Content
          aria-describedby={undefined}
          data-testid="truth-lightbox"
          className="fixed left-1/2 top-1/2 z-50 grid max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)] w-[min(96vw,96rem)] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 grid-rows-[auto_minmax(0,1fr)_auto] overflow-hidden border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white rounded-lg shadow-float focus:outline-none"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-4 py-3">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <Dialog.Title className="truncate text-sm font-bold">{item?.asset.name ?? (pageLoading ? "正在加载素材…" : "原图")}</Dialog.Title>
              <p className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                素材 #<span className="visual-code">{item?.asset_id}</span>{position ? ` · 第 ${position.index.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} / ${position.total.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} 张` : ""}
                {item && ` · ${truthRowSummary(item)}`}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" aria-label="上一张" disabled={!hasPrev || save.isPending} onClick={onPrev}><CaretLeft />上一张</Button>
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" aria-label="下一张" disabled={!hasNext || save.isPending} onClick={onNext}>下一张<CaretRight /></Button>
              <Dialog.Close
                aria-label="关闭原图"
                className="flex size-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white text-foreground hover:bg-alias-surface-009 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </Dialog.Close>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div
            className="flex min-h-64 items-center justify-center overflow-auto bg-white p-3 rounded-md"
          >
            {item && previewUrl ? (
              <img
                key={item.id}
                data-testid="truth-lightbox-image"
                src={previewUrl}
                alt={item.asset.name}
                className="block h-auto max-h-[calc(100dvh-16rem)] w-auto max-w-full border border-black/60 object-contain"
              />
            ) : item ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">案例 / 文本素材，请打开字段编辑器查看内容。</p>
            ) : (
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]" role="status">{pageLoading ? "正在加载这一页的素材…" : "没有可显示的素材"}</p>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-3" data-testid="truth-edit-form">
            {item?.truth_kind === "level" ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1" role="radiogroup" aria-label="人工真值等级">
                {LEVELS.map((value) => (
                  <button
                    key={value}
                    type="button"
                    role="radio"
                    aria-checked={level === value}
                    className={"visual-native-button " + ("border px-3 " +
                      (level === value
                        ? "border-alias-surface-002 bg-alias-surface-002 text-white"
                        : "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white hover:bg-alias-surface-009"))}
                    onClick={() => setLevel(value)}
                  >
                    {value} {LEVEL_LABELS[value]}{currentLevel === value ? " ·当前" : ""}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : gradeDraft ? (
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setDrawerOpen(true)}>
                查看 / 修改逐图档位（{gradeDraft.images.length} 张
                {gradeDraft.images.some((image) => !image.level)
                  ? `，未选 ${gradeDraft.images.filter((image) => !image.level).length} 张`
                  : ""}
                ）
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setDrawerOpen(true)}>
                {draft ? "查看 / 修改字段" : "填写字段"}
              </Button>
            )}
            <label className="min-w-64 flex-1 text-xs">
              修改原因（必填，记入修订历史）
              <input
                aria-label="修改原因"
                className="visual-native-input mt-1 w-full"
                value={reason}
                onChange={(event) => setReason(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="例如：复核图片后确认为 L1"
              />
            </label>
            <Button onClick={() => save.mutate({ item })} disabled={save.isPending || !changed || !item}>
              {save.isPending ? "保存中" : "保存为新一版真值"}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </Dialog.Content>
      </Dialog.Portal>
      {item && gradeDraft && (
        <ImageGradeDrawer
          open={drawerOpen}
          onOpenChange={setDrawerOpen}
          caseName={item.asset.name}
          draft={gradeDraft}
          onChange={setGradeDraft}
        />
      )}
      {item && item.truth_kind === "structured_fields" && draft && (
        <StructuredTruthDrawer
          open={drawerOpen}
          onOpenChange={setDrawerOpen}
          assetName={item.asset.name}
          draft={draft}
          contracts={contracts}
          selectedAssets={[]}
          onChange={(next) => setDraft({ ...next, source: "manual" })}
        />
      )}
    </Dialog.Root>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/quality-assets/truth-workbench-nav.ts =====
/**
 * 基准集真值灯箱的翻页/保存导航规则（纯函数，便于单测）。
 *
 * - 跨页翻页时列表用 placeholderData 保留上一页，加载完成前不能把上一页的素材当成当前素材显示；
 * - 保存成功后只有仍停留在被保存的那张时才自动前进，避免“保存中手动下一张 + 保存后再前进”跳过一张。
 */
export function truthLightboxItem<T>(
  items: readonly T[],
  openIndex: number | null,
  pageLoading: boolean,
): T | null {
  if (openIndex == null || pageLoading) return null
  return items[openIndex] ?? null
}

export function shouldAdvanceAfterTruthSave(
  savedItemId: number,
  currentItemId: number | null | undefined,
  hasNext: boolean,
): boolean {
  return hasNext && currentItemId === savedItemId
}

import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"

import { api, jsonBody } from "@/lib/api"
import type {
  ComponentVersionSummary,
  ComponentWorkingCopy,
  CategoryLevelScaleResponse,
  ConnectorAdapterOption,
  ConnectorContractDraft,
  ConnectorContractSummary,
  ConnectorPauseSummary,
  ConnectorProbeResult,
  EvaluationComponentAsset,
  IncrementalMechanismEvent,
  IncrementalMechanismState,
  ModuleTypeDefinition,
  WorkflowDefinitionSummary,
  WorkflowValidationReport,
  WorkflowVersionSummary,
  WorkflowWorkingCopy,
  WorkflowEditableTemplate,
  WorkflowV3Manifest,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/types"

export function getCategoryLevelScale(categoryKey: string) {
  return api<CategoryLevelScaleResponse>(
    `/api/category-evaluation/v3-config/${encodeURIComponent(categoryKey)}/level-scale`,
  )
}

const connectorRoot = "/api/connector-governance"

export function listConnectorContracts(
  categoryKey?: string,
  direction?: "upstream" | "downstream",
) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams()
  if (categoryKey) params.set("category_key", categoryKey)
  if (direction) params.set("direction", direction)
  const query = params.size ? `?${params.toString()}` : ""
  return api<{ items: ConnectorContractSummary[] }>(
    `${connectorRoot}/contracts${query}`,
  )
}

export function listConnectorAdapters() {
  return api<{ items: ConnectorAdapterOption[] }>(`${connectorRoot}/adapters`)
}

/** Registers a brand new interface. The platform fills in the structure digest
 *  for adapters that own one; only a live-schema adapter still needs it. */
export function createConnectorContract(draft: ConnectorContractDraft) {
  return api<ConnectorContractSummary>(`${connectorRoot}/contracts`, {
    method: "POST", ...jsonBody(draft),
  })
}

/** Adds a version to an existing interface. Category and direction are fixed by
 *  the first version and the server refuses to change them. */
export function createConnectorContractVersion(
  contractKey: string,
  draft: Omit<ConnectorContractDraft, "contract_key">,
) {
  return api<ConnectorContractSummary>(
    `${connectorRoot}/contracts/${encodeURIComponent(contractKey)}/versions`,
    { method: "POST", ...jsonBody(draft) },
  )
}

export function probeConnectorContract(contractId: number) {
  return api<ConnectorProbeResult>(
    `${connectorRoot}/contracts/${contractId}/probe`,
    { method: "POST" },
  )
}

export function changeConnectorPauseState(
  contractKey: string,
  action: "pause" | "resume",
  payload: {
    category_key: string
    mechanism_key?: string
    reason: string
    expected_revision: number | null
  },
) {
  return api<ConnectorPauseSummary>(
    `${connectorRoot}/contracts/${encodeURIComponent(contractKey)}/${action}`,
    { method: "PUT", ...jsonBody({ mechanism_key: "", ...payload }) },
  )
}

export function useConnectorContracts(categoryKey: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["connector-contracts", categoryKey],
    queryFn: () => listConnectorContracts(categoryKey),
    enabled: Boolean(categoryKey),
  })
}

  })
}

  return api<{ items: ModuleTypeDefinition[] }>(`${componentRoot}/module-types`)
}

const workflowRoot = "/api/workflows"

export function listWorkflowDefinitions() {
  return api<{ items: WorkflowDefinitionSummary[] }>(`${workflowRoot}/`)
}

export function listWorkflowVersions(workflowKey: string) {
  return api<{ items: WorkflowVersionSummary[] }>(
    `${workflowRoot}/${encodeURIComponent(workflowKey)}/versions`,
  )
}

export function createWorkflowDefinition(payload: {
  workflow_key: string
  name: string
  description: string
  owner: string
  allowed_categories: string[]
}) {
  return api<WorkflowDefinitionSummary>(`${workflowRoot}/`, {
    method: "POST",
    ...jsonBody(payload),
  })
}

export function getWorkflowWorkingCopy(workflowKey: string) {
  return api<WorkflowWorkingCopy>(
    `${workflowRoot}/${encodeURIComponent(workflowKey)}/working-copy`,
  )
}

export function getWorkflowEditableTemplate(workflowKey: string) {
  return api<WorkflowEditableTemplate>(
    `${workflowRoot}/${encodeURIComponent(workflowKey)}/editable-template`,
  )
}

export function saveWorkflowWorkingCopy(
  workflowKey: string,
  expectedLockVersion: number,
  manifest: WorkflowV3Manifest,
) {
  return api<WorkflowWorkingCopy>(
    `${workflowRoot}/${encodeURIComponent(workflowKey)}/working-copy`,
    {
      method: "PUT",
      ...jsonBody({ expected_lock_version: expectedLockVersion, manifest }),
    },
  )
}

export function validateWorkflowWorkingCopy(workflowKey: string) {
  return api<{ validation_report: WorkflowValidationReport }>(
    `${workflowRoot}/${encodeURIComponent(workflowKey)}/working-copy/validate`,
    { method: "POST" },
  )
}

export function generateEvaluationMechanismVersion(
  workflowKey: string,
  expectedLockVersion: number,
) {
  return api<WorkflowVersionSummary>(
    `${workflowRoot}/${encodeURIComponent(workflowKey)}/generate-version`,
    {
      method: "POST",
      ...jsonBody({ expected_lock_version: expectedLockVersion }),
    },
  )
}

const incrementalRoot = "/api/incremental-mechanisms"

export function getIncrementalMechanism(categoryKey: string) {
  return api<IncrementalMechanismState>(
    `${incrementalRoot}/${encodeURIComponent(categoryKey)}`,
  )
}

export function listIncrementalMechanismEvents(categoryKey: string) {
  return api<{ category_key: string; items: IncrementalMechanismEvent[] }>(
    `${incrementalRoot}/${encodeURIComponent(categoryKey)}/events`,
  )
}

export function setIncrementalMechanism(
  categoryKey: string,
  payload: {
    workflow_version_id: number
    model_execution_mode: "real" | "simulated"
    expected_lock_version: number | null
    reason: string
  },
) {
  return api<IncrementalMechanismState>(
    `${incrementalRoot}/${encodeURIComponent(categoryKey)}`,
    { method: "PUT", ...jsonBody(payload) },
  )
}

export function rollbackIncrementalMechanism(
  categoryKey: string,
  payload: {
    event_id: number
    expected_lock_version: number
    reason: string
  },
) {
  return api<IncrementalMechanismState>(
    `${incrementalRoot}/${encodeURIComponent(categoryKey)}/rollback`,
    { method: "POST", ...jsonBody(payload) },
  )
}

export function useComponentAssets(categoryKey: string, includeHidden = true) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["evaluation-components", categoryKey, includeHidden],
    queryFn: () => listComponentAssets(categoryKey, includeHidden),
    enabled: Boolean(categoryKey),
  })
}

export function useComponentWorkingCopy(assetId: number | null) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["evaluation-component-working-copy", assetId],
    queryFn: () => getComponentWorkingCopy(assetId!),
    enabled: assetId != null,
  })
}

export function useComponentVersions(assetId: number | null) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["evaluation-component-versions", assetId],
    queryFn: () => listComponentVersions(assetId!),
    enabled: assetId != null,
  })
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/category-asset-manager.tsx =====
import * as DropdownMenu from "@radix-ui/react-dropdown-menu"
import {
  ArrowSquareOut,
  Copy,
  DotsThree,
  MagicWand,
  Plus,
  ShieldCheck,
} from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"
import { useAccess } from "@/lib/access"
import {
  assetEditorHref,
  buildCopyAssetPayload,
  buildCreateAssetPayload,
  groupCategoryAssets,
  nextAssetKey,
  normalizeAssetKey,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/category-assets"
import {
  copyComponentAsset,
  createComponentAsset,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/api"
import type {
  EvaluationComponentAsset,
  ModuleTypeDefinition,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/types"

type CategoryOption = {
  category_key: string
  display_name: string
  status: string
}

type DrawerState =
  | { mode: "create" }
  | { mode: "copy"; asset: EvaluationComponentAsset }
  | null

export function CategoryAssetManager({
  categoryKey,
  assets,
  moduleTypes,
  categories,
}: {
  categoryKey: string
  assets: EvaluationComponentAsset[]
  moduleTypes: ModuleTypeDefinition[]
  categories: CategoryOption[]
}) {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const canEdit = useAccess().can("drafts:write")
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const grouped = groupCategoryAssets(assets, moduleTypes)
  const [drawer, setDrawer] = useState<DrawerState>(null)
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<"prompt" | "rule_module">("prompt")
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState("")
  const [promptStage, setPromptStage] = useState("")
  const [moduleTypeKey, setModuleTypeKey] = useState(moduleTypes[0]?.key ?? "")
  const [assetKey, setAssetKey] = useState("")
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("")
  const [targetCategoryKey, setTargetCategoryKey] = useState("")
  const [keyWasEdited, setKeyWasEdited] = useState(false)

  const targetCategories = categories.filter(
    (category) => category.status !== "retired" && category.category_key !== categoryKey,
  )

  function openCreate(nextKind: "prompt" | "rule_module" = "prompt") {
    if (!canEdit) return
    setKind(nextKind)
    setDisplayName("")
    setPromptStage("")
    setModuleTypeKey(moduleTypes[0]?.key ?? "")
    setAssetKey("")
    setDescription("")
    setTargetCategoryKey("")
    setKeyWasEdited(false)
    setDrawer({ mode: "create" })
  }

  function openCopy(asset: EvaluationComponentAsset) {
    if (!canEdit) return
    setDisplayName(`${asset.display_name} 副本`)
    setAssetKey(`${asset.asset_key}_copy`)
    setDescription(asset.description)
    setTargetCategoryKey("")
    setKeyWasEdited(false)
    setDrawer({ mode: "copy", asset })
  }

  function updateSuggestedName(value: string) {
    setDisplayName(value)
    if (!keyWasEdited && kind === "rule_module") {
      setAssetKey(nextAssetKey(normalizeAssetKey(value, moduleTypeKey || "rule_module"), assets.map((asset) => asset.asset_key)))
    }
  }

  function updateStage(value: string) {
    const stage = value.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9_-]/g, "")
    setPromptStage(stage)
    if (!keyWasEdited) {
      setAssetKey(nextAssetKey(`prompt_${normalizeAssetKey(stage, "new")}`, assets.map((asset) => asset.asset_key)))
    }
  }

  const createAsset = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => createComponentAsset(buildCreateAssetPayload({
      categoryKey,
      kind,
      displayName,
      assetKey,
      promptStage,
      moduleTypeKey,
      description,
    })),
    onSuccess: async (created) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["evaluation-components", categoryKey] })
      setDrawer(null)
      toast.success(`${created.display_name}已创建，可以开始编辑内容`)
      navigate(assetEditorHref(created))
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const copyAsset = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => {
      if (drawer?.mode !== "copy") throw new Error("请选择要复制的节点")
      return copyComponentAsset(drawer.asset.id, buildCopyAssetPayload({
        targetCategoryKey,
        targetAssetKey: assetKey,
        displayName,
      }))
    },
    onSuccess: async (created) => {
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["evaluation-components", categoryKey] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["evaluation-components", created.category_key] }),
      ])
      setDrawer(null)
      toast.success("复制完成；新节点的内容和版本将独立管理")
      navigate(assetEditorHref(created))
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const createReady = Boolean(
    displayName.trim()
      && assetKey.trim()
      && (kind === "prompt" ? promptStage.trim() : moduleTypeKey),
  )
  const copyReady = Boolean(targetCategoryKey && displayName.trim() && assetKey.trim())

  return (
    <section
      data-testid="workflow-manager-tabs"
      className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-5 py-5"
    >
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[var(--line)] pb-4">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">类目节点</p>
          <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-bold">管理这套评测会用到的内容</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 max-w-[72ch] text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">
            在这里新建提示词和规则模块；进入流程图后只需选择版本、模型和运行参数。
          </p>
        </div>
        <Button disabled={!canEdit} onClick={() => openCreate()}><Plus />新建节点</Button>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-5 grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[210px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">完整机制</p>
          <Button className="mt-2 w-full justify-start" size="sm">
            <MagicWand />评测机制版本
          </Button>
        </div>

        <div className="min-w-0 space-y-6">
          <div id="prompt-assets" className="scroll-mt-16">
            <AssetGroup
              title="提示词节点"
              description="模型需要理解和完成的任务内容"
              count={grouped.promptAssets.length}
              emptyText="还没有提示词节点。"
              onCreate={() => openCreate("prompt")}
            >
              {grouped.promptAssets.map((asset) => (
                <AssetCard key={asset.id} asset={asset} typeLabel="提示词" onCopy={() => openCopy(asset)} />
              ))}
            </AssetGroup>
          </div>

          <div id="rule-assets" className="scroll-mt-16">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold">规则模块</p>
                  <Badge tone="neutral">{grouped.ruleGroups.reduce((sum, group) => sum + group.assets.length, 0)}</Badge>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">按业务职责分类，可在一条流程里多次取用不同版本。</p>
              </div>
              {!grouped.ruleGroups.length && (
                <Button disabled={!canEdit} size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => openCreate("rule_module")}>
                  <Plus />新建规则模块
                </Button>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3 space-y-4">
              {grouped.ruleGroups.map((group) => (
                <div key={group.key} className="border-l-2 border-alias-edge-028 pl-3">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-2">
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold">{group.displayName}</p>
                    <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{group.description}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-2 grid gap-2 lg:grid-cols-2 2xl:grid-cols-3">
                    {group.assets.map((asset) => (
                      <AssetCard key={asset.id} asset={asset} typeLabel={group.displayName} onCopy={() => openCopy(asset)} />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <SecondaryDrawer
        open={drawer != null}
        onOpenChange={(open) => !open && setDrawer(null)}
        title={drawer?.mode === "copy" ? "复制到其他类目" : "新建节点"}
        description={drawer?.mode === "copy"
          ? "复制会带入当前内容，但新节点之后独立编辑、独立生成版本。"
          : "选择一种业务节点并填写名称，创建后即可进入内容编辑。"}
        footer={
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setDrawer(null)}>取消</Button>
            {drawer?.mode === "copy" ? (
              <Button disabled={!canEdit || !copyReady || copyAsset.isPending} onClick={() => copyAsset.mutate()}>
                <Copy />复制并打开
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <Button disabled={!canEdit || !createReady || createAsset.isPending} onClick={() => createAsset.mutate()}>
                <Plus />创建并编辑
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        }
      >
        {drawer?.mode === "copy" ? (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <div className="rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-3">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold">来源：{drawer.asset.display_name}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">复制后不会跟随来源更新，可放心按目标类目调整。</p>
            </div>
            <FormField label="目标类目" hint="请选择需要独立维护这份节点的类目">
              <select
                autoFocus
                className="visual-native-select w-full"
                value={targetCategoryKey}
                onChange={(event) => setTargetCategoryKey(event.target.value)}
              >
                <option value="">请选择类目</option>
                {targetCategories.map((category) => (
                  <option key={category.category_key} value={category.category_key}>{category.display_name}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </FormField>
            <FormField label="复制后的名称"><Input value={displayName} onChange={(event) => setDisplayName(event.target.value)} /></FormField>
            <FormField label="系统标识" hint="只用于系统识别，通常无需修改">
              <Input value={assetKey} onChange={(event) => setAssetKey(normalizeAssetKey(event.target.value))} />
            </FormField>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <fieldset>
              <legend className="text-sm font-bold">要新建哪种节点？</legend>
              <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <NodeKindChoice
                  active={kind === "prompt"}
                  title="提示词节点"
                  description="让模型理解内容并生成判断"
                  icon={<MagicWand />}
                  onClick={() => { setKind("prompt"); setAssetKey(""); setKeyWasEdited(false) }}
                />
                <NodeKindChoice
                  active={kind === "rule_module"}
                  title="规则模块"
                  description="按明确条件过滤、修正或撮合"
                  icon={<ShieldCheck />}
                  onClick={() => { setKind("rule_module"); setAssetKey(""); setKeyWasEdited(false) }}
                />
              </div>
            </fieldset>

            <FormField label="节点名称" hint="用运营同学一眼能看懂的名称">
              <Input autoFocus value={displayName} onChange={(event) => updateSuggestedName(event.target.value)} placeholder={kind === "prompt" ? "例如：知识图谱字段提取" : "例如：低清图片降权"} />
            </FormField>

            {kind === "prompt" ? (
              <FormField label="调用标识" hint="例如 A、B、C；创建后固定">
                <Input value={promptStage} onChange={(event) => updateStage(event.target.value)} placeholder="例如：C" maxLength={20} />
              </FormField>
            ) : (
              <FormField label="规则用途" hint="选择最接近这条规则职责的分类">
                <select
                  className="visual-native-select w-full"
                  value={moduleTypeKey}
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    const value = event.target.value
                    setModuleTypeKey(value)
                    if (!keyWasEdited && !displayName.trim()) setAssetKey(nextAssetKey(value, assets.map((asset) => asset.asset_key)))
                  }}
                >
                  {moduleTypes.map((definition) => (
                    <option key={definition.key} value={definition.key}>{definition.display_name}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </FormField>
            )}

            <FormField label="用途说明" hint="会显示在节点库中，帮助其他运营选择">
              <Textarea className="min-h-24" value={description} onChange={(event) => setDescription(event.target.value)} placeholder="一句话说明这个节点什么时候使用" />
            </FormField>

            <details className="visual-native-details border-y border-[var(--line)] py-3">
              <summary className="font-bold">高级设置（通常不需要修改）</summary>
              <FormField className="mt-4" label="系统标识" hint="同一类目内不可重复">
                <Input
                  value={assetKey}
                  onChange={(event) => { setKeyWasEdited(true); setAssetKey(normalizeAssetKey(event.target.value)) }}
                  placeholder="系统会自动生成"
                />
              </FormField>
            </details>
          </div>
        )}
      </SecondaryDrawer>
    </section>
  )
}

function AssetGroup({
  title,
  description,
  count,
  emptyText,
  onCreate,
  children,
}: {
  title: string
  description: string
  count: number
  emptyText: string
  onCreate: () => void
  children: React.ReactNode
}) {
  const canEdit = useAccess().can("drafts:write")
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><p className="text-sm font-bold">{title}</p><Badge tone="neutral">{count}</Badge></div>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{description}</p>
        </div>
        {!count && <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!canEdit} onClick={onCreate}><Plus />新建</Button>}
      </div>
      {count ? <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2 lg:grid-cols-2 2xl:grid-cols-3">{children}</div> : <p className="mt-3 border border-dashed border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-5 text-center text-xs text-[var(--muted)] rounded-md">{emptyText}</p>}
    </div>
  )
}

function AssetCard({
  asset,
  typeLabel,
  onCopy,
}: {
  asset: EvaluationComponentAsset
  typeLabel: string
  onCopy: () => void
}) {
  const canEdit = useAccess().can("drafts:write")
  return (
    <article className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3 rounded-md border border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-3 py-3">
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
          <Link className="truncate text-sm font-bold hover:underline" to={assetEditorHref(asset)}>{asset.display_name}</Link>
          {!asset.is_discoverable && <Badge tone="neutral">已隐藏</Badge>}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1 truncate text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{typeLabel} · <span className="visual-code">{asset.asset_key}</span></p>
      </div>
      <DropdownMenu.Root>
        <DropdownMenu.Trigger asChild>
          <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" aria-label={`更多操作：${asset.display_name}`}>
            <DotsThree />更多操作
          </Button>
        </DropdownMenu.Trigger>
        <DropdownMenu.Portal>
          <DropdownMenu.Content align="end" sideOffset={6} className="z-50 min-w-48 rounded-md border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white p-1 shadow-float">
            <DropdownMenu.Item asChild>
              <Link className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-sm px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:bg-alias-surface-009" to={assetEditorHref(asset)}>
                <ArrowSquareOut />编辑内容与版本
              </Link>
            </DropdownMenu.Item>
            <DropdownMenu.Item
              className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-sm px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:bg-alias-surface-009"
              disabled={!canEdit}
              onSelect={onCopy}
            >
              <Copy />复制到其他类目
            </DropdownMenu.Item>
          </DropdownMenu.Content>
        </DropdownMenu.Portal>
      </DropdownMenu.Root>
    </article>
  )
}

function NodeKindChoice({
  active,
  title,
  description,
  icon,
  onClick,
}: {
  active: boolean
  title: string
  description: string
  icon: React.ReactNode
  onClick: () => void
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      aria-pressed={active}
      className={`visual-native-button block px-4 py-4 text-left ${active ? "border-alias-edge-014 bg-alias-surface-027" : "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white hover:bg-alias-surface-047"}`}
      onClick={onClick}
    >
      <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold">{icon}{title}</span>
      <span className="mt-2 block text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{description}</span>
    </button>
  )
}

function FormField({
  label,
  hint,
  className = "",
  children,
}: {
  label: string
  hint?: string
  className?: string
  children: React.ReactNode
}) {
  return (
    <label className={className}>
      <span className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2 text-sm font-bold">
        {label}
        {hint && <span className="text-xs font-normal text-[var(--muted)]">{hint}</span>}
      </span>
      {children}
    </label>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/category-assets.ts =====
import type {
  EvaluationComponentAsset,
  ModuleTypeDefinition,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/types"

export type CategoryRuleAssetGroup = {
  key: string
  displayName: string
  description: string
  assets: EvaluationComponentAsset[]
}

export type CategoryAssetGroups = {
  promptAssets: EvaluationComponentAsset[]
  ruleGroups: CategoryRuleAssetGroup[]
}

type CreateAssetInput = {
  categoryKey: string
  kind: "prompt" | "rule_module"
  displayName: string
  assetKey: string
  promptStage?: string
  moduleTypeKey?: string
  description?: string
}

type CopyAssetInput = {
  targetCategoryKey: string
  targetAssetKey: string
  displayName: string
}

function byDisplayName(left: EvaluationComponentAsset, right: EvaluationComponentAsset) {
  return left.display_name.localeCompare(right.display_name, "zh-CN")
}

export function groupCategoryAssets(
  assets: EvaluationComponentAsset[],
  moduleTypes: ModuleTypeDefinition[],
): CategoryAssetGroups {
  const promptAssets = assets
    .filter((asset) => asset.kind === "prompt")
    .sort(byDisplayName)
  const rules = assets.filter((asset) => asset.kind === "rule_module")
  const knownGroups = moduleTypes
    .map((definition) => ({
      key: definition.key,
      displayName: definition.display_name,
      description: definition.description,
      assets: rules
        .filter((asset) => asset.module_type_key === definition.key)
        .sort(byDisplayName),
    }))
    .filter((group) => group.assets.length > 0)
  const knownKeys = new Set(moduleTypes.map((definition) => definition.key))
  const unknownAssets = rules
    .filter((asset) => !asset.module_type_key || !knownKeys.has(asset.module_type_key))
    .sort(byDisplayName)

  return {
    promptAssets,
    ruleGroups: unknownAssets.length
      ? [
          ...knownGroups,
          {
            key: "unregistered",
            displayName: "其他规则模块",
            description: "尚未登记中文类型的兼容模块",
            assets: unknownAssets,
          },
        ]
      : knownGroups,
  }
}

export function normalizeAssetKey(value: string, fallback = "node") {
  const normalized = value
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9_]+/g, "_")
    .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "")
    .replace(/_+/g, "_")
  return normalized || fallback
}

export function nextAssetKey(value: string, existingKeys: string[]) {
  const base = normalizeAssetKey(value)
  const occupied = new Set(existingKeys)
  if (!occupied.has(base)) return base
  let suffix = 2
  while (occupied.has(`${base}_${suffix}`)) suffix += 1
  return `${base}_${suffix}`
}

export function buildCreateAssetPayload(input: CreateAssetInput) {
  const common = {
    category_key: input.categoryKey,
    kind: input.kind,
    display_name: input.displayName.trim(),
    asset_key: normalizeAssetKey(input.assetKey),
    description: input.description?.trim() ?? "",
  }
  if (input.kind === "prompt") {
    return {
      ...common,
      prompt_stage: input.promptStage?.trim() ?? "",
    }
  }
  return {
    ...common,
    module_type_key: input.moduleTypeKey ?? "",
  }
}

export function buildCopyAssetPayload(input: CopyAssetInput) {
  return {
    target_category_key: input.targetCategoryKey,
    target_asset_key: normalizeAssetKey(input.targetAssetKey),
    display_name: input.displayName.trim(),
  }
}

export function assetEditorHref(asset: EvaluationComponentAsset) {
  if (asset.kind === "prompt") {
    const params = new URLSearchParams({
      category_key: asset.category_key,
      asset_key: asset.asset_key,
    })
    return `/workflow/governance/workflow-manager/prompts?${params.toString()}`
  }
  return `/workflow/governance/workflow-manager/components/${asset.id}`
}
