===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/composer-logic.ts =====
import type { WorkflowEdge } from "@/features/workflow-manager/types"

type GraphNode = {
  node_instance_id: string
  kind: string
  config: Record<string, unknown>
}

type LabeledGraphNode = GraphNode & {
  display_name: string
}

export function connectionOptionLabel(
  node: LabeledGraphNode,
  nodes: LabeledGraphNode[],
  edges: Array<Pick<WorkflowEdge, "from" | "to">>,
) {
  const sameName = nodes.filter((candidate) => candidate.display_name === node.display_name)
  if (sameName.length < 2) return node.display_name
  const predecessorLabels = Array.from(new Set(
    edges
      .filter((edge) => edge.to === node.node_instance_id)
      .map((edge) => nodes.find((candidate) => candidate.node_instance_id === edge.from)?.display_name)
      .filter((label): label is string => Boolean(label)),
  ))
  if (predecessorLabels.length === 1) {
    return `${node.display_name}（在${predecessorLabels[0]}之后）`
  }
  if (predecessorLabels.length > 1) {
    return `${node.display_name}（汇合${predecessorLabels.join("、")}）`
  }
  const ordinal = sameName.findIndex((candidate) => candidate.node_instance_id === node.node_instance_id) + 1
  return `${node.display_name}（第 ${ordinal} 个同名步骤）`
}

export function workflowComposerIsLoading(
  workflowKey: string,
  pending: {
    workingCopy: boolean
    versions: boolean
    compatibilityTemplate: boolean
  },
) {
  return Boolean(
    workflowKey
      && workflowKey !== "new"
      && (pending.workingCopy || pending.versions || pending.compatibilityTemplate),
  )
}

export async function persistBeforeFollowUp<TSaved, TResult>(
  persist: () => Promise<TSaved>,
  acceptPersisted: (saved: TSaved) => void,
  followUp: (saved: TSaved) => Promise<TResult>,
) {
  const saved = await persist()
  acceptPersisted(saved)
  return { saved, result: await followUp(saved) }
}

export function confirmEditorNavigation(
  hasUnsavedChanges: boolean,
  confirmDiscard: () => boolean,
) {
  return !hasUnsavedChanges || confirmDiscard()
}

export function workflowDefinitionKnown(
  workflowKey: string,
  definitions: ReadonlyArray<{ workflow_key: string }>,
  createdInSession: ReadonlySet<string>,
) {
  return createdInSession.has(workflowKey)
    || definitions.some((item) => item.workflow_key === workflowKey)
}

export function connectionProblem(
  from: string,
  to: string,
  nodes: GraphNode[],
  edges: WorkflowEdge[],
) {
  if (from === to) return "步骤不能连接到自己"
  if (edges.some((edge) => edge.from === from && edge.to === to)) return "这两个步骤已经连接"
  const source = nodes.find((node) => node.node_instance_id === from)
  const target = nodes.find((node) => node.node_instance_id === to)
  if (!source || !target) return "所选步骤已经不存在，请刷新后重试"
  if (source.kind === "downstream_projection") {
    const completionMode = source.config.completion_mode ?? "continue"
    if (completionMode === "await_follow_up") {
      const paired = target.kind === "upstream_connector"
        && target.config.input_mode === "continuation_event"
        && target.config.continuation_key === source.config.continuation_key
        && target.node_instance_id === source.config.resume_node_instance_id
      if (!paired) return "等待对方处理的步骤只能连接匹配的“接收外部处理结果”步骤"
    }
  }
  if (target.kind === "upstream_connector") {
    const wouldCycle = reaches(to, from, edges)
    if (wouldCycle) return "这样连接会形成循环，请调整先后顺序"
    if (target.config.input_mode !== "continuation_event") return "读取上游必须放在流程起点"
    const paired = source.kind === "downstream_projection"
      && source.config.completion_mode === "await_follow_up"
      && source.config.continuation_key === target.config.continuation_key
      && source.config.resume_node_instance_id === target.node_instance_id
    if (!paired) return "接收外部处理结果必须连接在匹配的等待步骤之后"
  }
  if (source.kind === "branch") {
    const branches = Array.isArray(source.config.branches) ? source.config.branches : []
    const outgoing = edges.filter((edge) => edge.from === from)
    if (outgoing.length >= branches.length) return "这组路线已经全部连接，如需更多路线请先修改路线名称"
  }
  if (reaches(to, from, edges)) return "这样连接会形成循环，请调整先后顺序"
  return null
}

function reaches(start: string, target: string, edges: WorkflowEdge[]) {
  const adjacency = new Map<string, string[]>()
  for (const edge of edges) adjacency.set(edge.from, [...(adjacency.get(edge.from) ?? []), edge.to])
  const pending = [start]
  const visited = new Set<string>()
  while (pending.length) {
    const current = pending.pop()!
    if (current === target) return true
    if (visited.has(current)) continue
    visited.add(current)
    pending.push(...(adjacency.get(current) ?? []))
  }
  return false
}

export function duplicateRuleConflicts(nodes: GraphNode[]) {
  const seen = new Map<string, string>()
  const conflicts = new Map<string, string>()
  for (const node of nodes) {
    if (node.kind !== "rule_module") continue
    const versionId = Number(node.config.rule_module_version_id ?? 0)
    if (!versionId) continue
    const fingerprint = JSON.stringify({
      rule_module_version_id: versionId,
      input_bindings: node.config.input_bindings ?? {},
      scope: node.config.scope ?? "default",
      route_condition: node.config.route_condition ?? null,
    })
    const first = seen.get(fingerprint)
    if (first) {
      conflicts.set(first, "它与另一个节点使用了相同版本、输入和作用范围。")
      conflicts.set(node.node_instance_id, "它与另一个节点使用了相同版本、输入和作用范围。")
    } else {
      seen.set(fingerprint, node.node_instance_id)
    }
  }
  return conflicts
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/connector-contract-form.tsx =====
import { Plus, Warning } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMemo, useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import {
  createConnectorContract,
  createConnectorContractVersion,
  listConnectorAdapters,
} from "./api"
import type {
  ConnectorAdapterOption,
  ConnectorContractDraft,
  ConnectorContractSummary,
} from "./types"

type CategoryOption = { category_key: string; display_name: string }

/** Registering an interface, without anyone having to know what a 64-hex
 *  structure digest is. The operator picks a connection type; the platform
 *  fills in what only it can know, and says plainly when it cannot. */
export function ConnectorContractForm({
  categories,
  existing,
  onDone,
}: {
  categories: CategoryOption[]
  existing: ConnectorContractSummary[]
  onDone: () => void
}) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const adapters = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["connector-adapters"],
    queryFn: listConnectorAdapters,
  })

  const [contractKey, setContractKey] = useState("")
  const [adapterKey, setAdapterKey] = useState("")
  const [categoryKey, setCategoryKey] = useState("")
  const [locator, setLocator] = useState("logical.")
  const [owner, setOwner] = useState("")
  const [fingerprint, setFingerprint] = useState("")
  const [mapping, setMapping] = useState("{}")
  const [batchSize, setBatchSize] = useState(1)
  const [timeoutSeconds, setTimeoutSeconds] = useState(30)

  const adapter = useMemo<ConnectorAdapterOption | undefined>(
    () => (adapters.data?.items ?? []).find((item) => item.adapter_key === adapterKey),
    [adapters.data?.items, adapterKey],
  )
  // A key that already exists becomes a new version of that interface, which is
  // the only way to change a live contract - contracts are immutable once made.
  const priorVersions = useMemo(
    () => existing.filter((item) => item.contract_key === contractKey.trim()),
    [existing, contractKey],
  )
  const addsVersion = priorVersions.length > 0
  const locked = addsVersion ? priorVersions[0] : undefined

  const mappingError = useMemo(() => {
    if (!mapping.trim()) return "请填写字段映射，没有映射就填 {}"
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(mapping)
      if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object" || Array.isArray(parsed)) {
        return "字段映射要是一个对象，例如 {\"content_id\": \"source.id\"}"
      }
      return ""
    } catch {
      return "字段映射不是合法 JSON"
    }
  }, [mapping])

  const needsFingerprint = Boolean(adapter && !adapter.fingerprint_supplied_by_platform)
  const effectiveDirection = locked?.direction ?? adapter?.direction
  const effectiveCategory = locked?.category_key ?? categoryKey

  const problems: string[] = []
  if (!contractKey.trim()) problems.push("接口标识")
  if (!adapter) problems.push("连接方式")
  if (!effectiveCategory) problems.push("类目")
  if (!locator.trim() || locator.trim() === "logical.") problems.push("连接位置")
  if (!owner.trim()) problems.push("负责人")
  if (needsFingerprint && fingerprint.trim().length !== 64) problems.push("结构指纹")
  if (mappingError) problems.push("字段映射")

  const submit = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => {
      const draft: Omit<ConnectorContractDraft, "contract_key"> = {
        direction: effectiveDirection as "upstream" | "downstream",
        category_key: effectiveCategory,
        logical_locator: locator.trim(),
        adapter_key: adapterKey,
        field_mapping: JSON.parse(mapping),
        cursor_contract: {},
        idempotency_contract: {},
        batch_size: batchSize,
        timeout_seconds: timeoutSeconds,
        owner: owner.trim(),
        status: "draft",
        ...(needsFingerprint ? { schema_fingerprint: fingerprint.trim() } : {}),
      }
      return addsVersion
        ? createConnectorContractVersion(contractKey.trim(), draft)
        : createConnectorContract({ ...draft, contract_key: contractKey.trim() })
    },
    onSuccess: async (row) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["connector-contracts"] })
      toast.success(`已登记 ${row.contract_key} v${row.version}，默认草稿状态`)
      onDone()
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  return (
    <div data-testid="connector-contract-form" className="space-y-4 visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-5 py-5">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-bold">{addsVersion ? "为已有接口新增版本" : "登记新接口"}</h3>
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={onDone}>取消</Button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
        <Field label="接口标识" hint="全小写，用点分段，例如 factory.prod.downstream">
          <Input value={contractKey} onChange={(event) => setContractKey(event.target.value)} placeholder="factory.prod.downstream" />
          {addsVersion && (
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
              已存在 v{priorVersions[0].version}，本次将新增 v{priorVersions[0].version + 1}；类目与方向沿用旧版本，不可更改。
            </p>
          )}
        </Field>

        <Field label="连接方式" hint="决定平台用哪套投递或读取逻辑">
          <select
            className="visual-native-select w-full"
            value={adapterKey}
            onChange={(event) => { setAdapterKey(event.target.value); setFingerprint("") }}
          >
            <option value="">请选择</option>
            {(adapters.data?.items ?? [])
              .filter((item) => !locked || item.direction === locked.direction)
              .map((item) => (
                <option key={item.adapter_key} value={item.adapter_key}>
                  {item.label}（{item.direction === "upstream" ? "读取上游" : "写入下游"}）{item.executable ? "" : " · 占位"}
                </option>
              ))}
          </select>
          {adapter && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{adapter.summary}</p>}
          {adapter && !adapter.executable && (
            <p className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-alias-content-038">
              <Warning />这是占位合同，登记后平台不会真正读写任何数据。
            </p>
          )}
        </Field>

        <Field label="类目" hint={locked ? "沿用首个版本，不可更改" : "这个接口服务哪个业务类目"}>
          {locked ? (
            <div className="flex h-11 items-center"><Badge>{locked.category_key}</Badge></div>
          ) : (
            <select className="visual-native-select w-full" value={categoryKey} onChange={(event) => setCategoryKey(event.target.value)}>
              <option value="">请选择</option>
              {categories.map((item) => <option key={item.category_key} value={item.category_key}>{item.display_name}</option>)}
            </select>
          )}
        </Field>

        <Field label="连接位置" hint={adapter?.locator_hint ?? "以 logical. 开头的逻辑引用；真实地址与口令留在部署侧"}>
          <Input value={locator} onChange={(event) => setLocator(event.target.value)} placeholder="logical.factory.prod.submit" />
          {adapter?.environment_hint && (
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">部署侧需要配置：<code className="visual-code">{adapter.environment_hint}</code></p>
          )}
        </Field>

        <Field label="负责人" hint="出问题时找谁">
          <Input value={owner} onChange={(event) => setOwner(event.target.value)} placeholder="产品-老大 / 研发-xxx" />
        </Field>

        <Field label="结构指纹" hint={
          !adapter ? "先选择连接方式"
            : adapter.fingerprint_supplied_by_platform ? "平台自动填写，无需手填"
              : "该连接方式要从真实表结构读取，请由研发提供"
        }>
          {adapter?.fingerprint_supplied_by_platform ? (
            <div className="flex h-11 items-center text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">由平台自动填写</div>
          ) : (
            <Input value={fingerprint} onChange={(event) => setFingerprint(event.target.value)} placeholder="64 位十六进制" disabled={!adapter} />
          )}
        </Field>

        <Field label="单批处理量" hint="1 到 1000">
          <Input type="number" min={1} max={1000} value={batchSize} onChange={(event) => setBatchSize(Number(event.target.value))} />
        </Field>

        <Field label="超时秒数" hint="1 到 600">
          <Input type="number" min={1} max={600} value={timeoutSeconds} onChange={(event) => setTimeoutSeconds(Number(event.target.value))} />
        </Field>
      </div>

      <Field label="字段映射" hint="描述字段怎么对应；没有就填 {}">
        <textarea
          className="visual-native-textarea min-h-24 w-full font-data"
          value={mapping}
          onChange={(event) => setMapping(event.target.value)}
          spellCheck={false}
        />
        {mappingError && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-alias-content-047">{mappingError}</p>}
      </Field>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4">
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          登记后是<strong>草稿</strong>状态，不会立刻投递。确认无误后在列表里重新检查并启用。
        </p>
        <Button
          disabled={problems.length > 0 || submit.isPending}
          onClick={() => submit.mutate()}
        >
          <Plus />{addsVersion ? "新增版本" : "登记接口"}
        </Button>
      </div>
      {problems.length > 0 && (
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">还需填写：{problems.join("、")}</p>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function Field({ label, hint, children }: { label: string; hint?: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">
      <span className="mb-2 block">{label}</span>
      {children}
      {hint && <span className="mt-1 block font-normal">{hint}</span>}
    </label>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/doubao-endpoint-panel.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { Link } from "react-router-dom"
import { ArrowRight, LinkSimple, LinkSimpleBreak } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import type { WorkflowDefinitionSummary } from "@/features/workflow-manager/types"
import { api, jsonBody } from "@/lib/api"
import type { DoubaoEndpointListResponse, DoubaoInferenceEndpoint } from "@/lib/types"

const root = "/api/doubao-endpoints"
export const doubaoEndpointsQueryKey = ["doubao-endpoints"]

const verificationLabels = { unverified: "未验收", verified: "已验收", failed: "验收失败" } as const

/**
 * 展示工作流当前默认的豆包推理方式及其接入点；保留历史批量配置。
 * 接入点本身在「系统管理 → 豆包接入点」登记；这里只做绑定与解绑。
 */
export function DoubaoEndpointBindingPanel({ categoryKey, workflows }: { categoryKey: string; workflows: WorkflowDefinitionSummary[] }) {
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const endpoints = useQuery({
    queryKey: [...doubaoEndpointsQueryKey, categoryKey],
    queryFn: () => api<DoubaoEndpointListResponse>(`${root}?${new URLSearchParams({ category_key: categoryKey })}`),
    enabled: Boolean(categoryKey),
  })
  const [intent, setIntent] = useState<{ endpoint: DoubaoInferenceEndpoint; mode: "bind" | "unbind"; workflowId: number | null } | null>(null)
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const canManage = Boolean(endpoints.data?.can_manage)
  const items = endpoints.data?.items ?? []
  const routingEnabled = Boolean(endpoints.data?.endpoint_routing_enabled)
  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => {
      if (!intent) throw new Error("请先选择操作")
      if (!intent.workflowId) throw new Error("请选择工作流")
      const body = { workflow_definition_id: intent.workflowId, expected_revision: intent.endpoint.revision, reason: reason.trim() }
      return api(`${root}/${intent.endpoint.id}/${intent.mode}`, { method: "POST", ...jsonBody(body) })
    },
    onSuccess: async () => {
      toast.success(intent?.mode === "bind" ? "接入点已绑定到该工作流" : "已解除该工作流的接入点绑定")
      setIntent(null)
      setReason("")
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: doubaoEndpointsQueryKey })
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["incremental-automation-cards"] })
    },
    onError: async (error) => {
      toast.error(error.message)
      await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: doubaoEndpointsQueryKey })
    },
  })
  const workflowName = (id: number | null) => workflows.find((item) => item.id === id)?.name ?? (id ? `工作流 #${id}` : null)
  // 可绑定候选：本类目内尚未绑定到该接入点的工作流（已绑定其他接入点的工作流会直接改绑）
  const bindableWorkflows = (endpoint: DoubaoInferenceEndpoint) => workflows.filter((item) => !endpoint.workflow_definition_ids.includes(item.id))

  return (
    <section aria-label="豆包推理接入点绑定" className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-5 py-5">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">豆包 2.0 接入点</p>
          <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-bold">工作流推理接入点</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 max-w-[70ch] text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">
            新运行默认在线推理；接入点可由多条同类目工作流共用。改绑只影响之后创建的运行，已冻结的运行继续沿用原配置。本面板只管理豆包接入点，本地 Qwen 使用节点中的模型配置。
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <Badge tone={routingEnabled ? "active" : "neutral"}>{routingEnabled ? "接入点路由已启用" : "接入点路由未启用"}</Badge>
          <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" asChild><Link to="/workflow/governance/doubao-endpoints">去登记接入点<ArrowRight /></Link></Button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {endpoints.isError && <p role="alert" className="mt-4 text-sm text-danger">接入点读取失败：{endpoints.error.message}</p>}
      {endpoints.isPending && <p role="status" className="mt-4 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">正在读取接入点…</p>}
      {endpoints.data && (
        <div className="mt-4 divide-y divide-[var(--line)] border-y border-[var(--line)]" aria-label="工作流推理方式与接入点状态">
          {workflows.map((workflow) => {
            const route = endpoints.data.workflow_routes?.find((item) => item.workflow_definition_id === workflow.id)
            const batch = items.find((item) => item.id === route?.batch_endpoint_row_id)
            const online = items.find((item) => item.id === route?.online_endpoint_row_id)
            const usesOnline = route?.inference_mode === "online"
            const onlineReady = routingEnabled && online?.status === "active" && online.verification_status === "verified"
            const ready = routingEnabled && route?.fallback_ready
            // 接入点就绪 ≠ 会转在线：升级动作另有部署开关，关掉时页面必须说出来。
            const escalates = route?.online_escalation_enabled !== false
            return <div key={workflow.id} className="space-y-2 py-4">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><h3 className="min-w-0 break-words font-bold">{workflow.name}</h3><Badge className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap" tone={(usesOnline ? onlineReady : ready) ? "success" : "neutral"}>{usesOnline ? onlineReady ? "豆包在线已就绪" : "豆包在线待配置" : ready ? "双接入点已就绪" : "批量兜底未就绪"}</Badge>{!usesOnline && !escalates && <Badge tone="neutral">转在线兜底已停用</Badge>}</div>
              <div className="grid gap-2 text-sm md:grid-cols-2">
                <p className="min-w-0 break-all">{usesOnline ? "保留的批量配置" : "批量接入点"}：{batch ? `${batch.display_name} · ${batch.endpoint_id}` : "未绑定"}</p>
                <p className="min-w-0 break-all">在线接入点：{online ? `${online.display_name} · ${online.endpoint_id}` : "未绑定"}</p>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">默认方式：{route?.inference_mode === "online" ? "在线（新运行直接在线）" : route ? "批量优先" : "状态待刷新"}。在下方对应接入点行绑定或修改。</p>
              {usesOnline ? <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">新建运行直接使用在线接入点，不启用批量转在线的 24 小时兜底。{onlineReady ? "在线接入点已启用并验收。" : "使用豆包的工作流需绑定启用且已验收的在线接入点；本地 Qwen 无需此绑定。"}历史运行仍按各自冻结配置执行。</p>
                : ready ? <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">批量评测失败后最多重试 {(route?.batch_max_attempts ?? 3) - 1} 次，仍失败或素材原入队满 {(route?.deadline_seconds ?? 86400) / 3600} 小时，{escalates ? "未完成节点转在线" : "未完成节点按普通失败收尾进失败待处理，不转在线（转在线兜底已在服务端停用）"}。429 单列资源等待，不消耗评测重试次数，按 1／2／4／8／15 分钟退避并遵守供应商更长的等待要求；在线遇到 429 也保留排队。各节点思考开关和冻结参数保持不变。等待不占执行槽、不写预设结果，已完成素材独立推进。所有真实请求及未决费用仍计入预算，额度不足时保留任务等待处理。在线按在线费率计费。接入点绑定修改仅影响新建运行。</p>
                : <p role="status" className="text-xs text-warning">{!routingEnabled ? "接入点路由未启用" : route?.blocker ?? "服务未返回兜底状态，请刷新或检查服务版本"}。需绑定启用、已验收且模型版本一致的在线与批量接入点。</p>}
            </div>
          })}
        </div>
      )}
      {endpoints.data && !items.length && (
        <p className="mt-4 border border-dashed border-[var(--line-strong)] px-4 py-4 text-sm text-[var(--muted)] rounded-md">
          该类目还没有登记接入点。请先在火山方舟控制台创建接入点，再到「系统管理 → 豆包接入点」手工登记 ID，然后回到这里绑定。{routingEnabled ? "接入点路由已启用，使用豆包 2.0 的工作流在绑定前无法发起真实调用。" : ""}
        </p>
      )}
      {items.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-4 divide-y divide-[var(--line)] border-y border-[var(--line)]">
          {items.map((endpoint) => (
            <div key={endpoint.id} className="grid gap-3 py-3 text-sm md:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.4fr)_minmax(0,1.2fr)_auto] md:items-start">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <p className="break-words font-semibold">{endpoint.display_name} · {endpoint.endpoint_id.startsWith("ep-bi-") ? "批量" : "在线"}</p>
                <p className="font-data mt-1 break-all text-xs text-[var(--muted)]"><span className="visual-code">{endpoint.endpoint_id}{endpoint.foundation_model ? ` · ${endpoint.foundation_model}` : ""}</span>{endpoint.provisioned_by_platform ? ` · 平台创建 ${endpoint.remote_name}` : ""}</p>
                <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                  <Badge tone={endpoint.status === "active" ? "success" : "neutral"}>{endpoint.status === "active" ? "生效中" : "已停用"}</Badge>
                  <Badge tone={endpoint.verification_status === "verified" ? "success" : endpoint.verification_status === "failed" ? "danger" : "warning"}>{verificationLabels[endpoint.verification_status]}</Badge>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0 text-xs">
                {endpoint.bindings.length === 0 && <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">未绑定工作流</p>}
                {endpoint.bindings.length > 0 && (
                  <ul className="space-y-1" aria-label={`${endpoint.display_name} 已绑定的工作流`}>
                    {endpoint.bindings.map((binding) => (
                      <li key={binding.workflow_definition_id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 basis-40 break-words" title={binding.workflow_key}>{workflowName(binding.workflow_definition_id) ?? binding.workflow_name}</span>
                        <select aria-label={`${binding.workflow_name} 默认推理方式`} value={binding.inference_mode ?? "auto"} disabled={!canManage}
                          onChange={async (event) => {
                            try {
                              await api(`${root}/workflows/${binding.workflow_definition_id}/inference-mode`, {method:"PUT", ...jsonBody({mode:event.target.value,expected_mode:binding.inference_mode ?? "auto"})})
                              await client.invalidateQueries({queryKey:doubaoEndpointsQueryKey})
                              toast.success("默认推理方式已更新，仅影响新运行")
                            } catch (error) { toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "更新失败") }
                          }} className="visual-native-select">
                          <option value="auto">默认：批量优先</option><option value="online">默认：在线</option><option value="batch">默认：批量</option>
                        </select>
                        <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!canManage || mutation.isPending} onClick={() => { setIntent({ endpoint, mode: "unbind", workflowId: binding.workflow_definition_id }); setReason("") }}><LinkSimpleBreak />解绑</Button>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!canManage || endpoint.status !== "active" || mutation.isPending || !bindableWorkflows(endpoint).length} onClick={() => { setIntent({ endpoint, mode: "bind", workflowId: bindableWorkflows(endpoint)[0]?.id ?? null }); setReason("") }}><LinkSimple />绑定工作流</Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      {intent && (
        <form className="mt-4 space-y-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4" onSubmit={(event) => { event.preventDefault(); if (reason.trim()) mutation.mutate() }}>
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{intent.mode === "bind" ? `把 ${intent.endpoint.display_name} 绑定到工作流` : `解除 ${workflowName(intent.workflowId) ?? "该工作流"} 与 ${intent.endpoint.display_name} 的绑定`}</p>
          {intent.mode === "bind" && (
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold">目标工作流
              <select className="visual-native-select mt-2 w-full" value={intent.workflowId ?? ""} onChange={(event) => setIntent({ ...intent, workflowId: Number(event.target.value) })}>
                {bindableWorkflows(intent.endpoint).map((workflow) => <option key={workflow.id} value={workflow.id}>{workflow.name} · {workflow.workflow_key}</option>)}
              </select>
            </label>
          )}
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold">操作原因<Input className="mt-2" value={reason} maxLength={2000} required placeholder="记录本次绑定或解绑的原因" onChange={(event) => setReason(event.target.value)} /></label>
          <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">一个工作流分别保留在线和批量接入点，同一方式下重新绑定会替换原绑定；一个接入点可同时服务多条同类目工作流；已在运行中的任务不受影响。</p>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Button size="sm" disabled={!reason.trim() || mutation.isPending}>{mutation.isPending ? "提交中…" : "确认"}</Button>
            <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" type="button" disabled={mutation.isPending} onClick={() => setIntent(null)}>取消</Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      )}
      {!canManage && endpoints.data && <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">绑定与解绑需要系统管理员权限。</p>}
    </section>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/foreach-contract.ts =====
const safeWorkflowDotPath = /^\$\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]*(?:\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]*)*$/

export function isSafeWorkflowDotPath(value: unknown): value is string {
  return typeof value === "string" && safeWorkflowDotPath.test(value)
}

export function defaultForeachImageConfig({
  modelRegistryId,
  maxTokens,
  visionInputPolicy,
}: {
  modelRegistryId: number
  maxTokens: number
  visionInputPolicy?: unknown
}): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    items_path: "$.case.images",
    item_identity_path: "$.cloud_id",
    max_items: 1000,
    max_parallel_items: 4,
    failure_policy: "fail_open",
    item_executor: {
      kind: "prompt",
      asset_key: "",
      input_modality: "image",
      prompt_version_id: 0,
      model_registry_id: modelRegistryId,
      max_tokens: maxTokens,
      thinking_level: "none",
      thinking_enabled: false,
      ...(visionInputPolicy ? { vision_input_policy: visionInputPolicy } : {}),
    },
  }
}

export function defaultAggregateCaseConfig(
  foreachNodeId: string,
): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    foreach_node_id: foreachNodeId,
    included_statuses: ["succeeded"],
    score_path: "$.score",
    feature_paths: {},
    empty_policy: "fail",
    failure_projection: "include",
  }
}

export function foreachItemExecutor(
  config: Record<string, unknown>,
): Record<string, unknown> {
  const value = config.item_executor
  return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
    : {}
}

export function patchForeachItemExecutor(
  config: Record<string, unknown>,
  patch: Record<string, unknown>,
): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    ...config,
    item_executor: {
      ...foreachItemExecutor(config),
      ...patch,
    },
  }
}

export function parseFeaturePaths(value: string): Record<string, string> {
  return Object.fromEntries(
    value
      .split(/\r?\n/)
      .map((line) => line.trim())
      .filter(Boolean)
      .map((line) => {
        const delimiter = line.indexOf("=")
        return delimiter > 0
          ? [line.slice(0, delimiter).trim(), line.slice(delimiter + 1).trim()]
          : [line, ""]
      })
      .filter(([key, path]) => key && path),
  )
}

export function formatFeaturePaths(value: unknown): string {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return ""
  return Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
    .map(([key, path]) => `${key}=${String(path)}`)
    .join("\n")
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/incremental-activation-panel.tsx =====
import { ArrowCounterClockwise, CheckCircle, LockKey, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQueries, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"
import {
  getIncrementalMechanism,
  listIncrementalMechanismEvents,
  listWorkflowVersions,
  rollbackIncrementalMechanism,
  setIncrementalMechanism,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/api"
import {
  incrementalBindingExecutionIssue,
  mechanismDiffRows,
  mechanismVersionActivationIssue,
  mechanismVersionEligible,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/incremental-mechanism"
import { visibleIncrementalMechanismVersions } from "@/features/workflow-manager/incremental-version-presentation"
import type {
  WorkflowDefinitionSummary,
  WorkflowVersionSummary,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/types"
import { ApiError, api } from "@/lib/api"
import type { User } from "@/lib/types"
import { formatPlatformTime } from "../../lib/platform-time"

export function IncrementalActivationPanel({
  categoryKey,
  workflows,
}: {
  categoryKey: string
  workflows: WorkflowDefinitionSummary[]
}) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const me = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["me"],
    queryFn: () => api<User>("/api/auth/me"),
  })
  const pointer = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["incremental-mechanism", categoryKey],
    queryFn: () => getIncrementalMechanism(categoryKey),
    enabled: Boolean(categoryKey),
  })
  const events = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["incremental-mechanism-events", categoryKey],
    queryFn: () => listIncrementalMechanismEvents(categoryKey),
    enabled: Boolean(categoryKey),
  })
  const versionQueries = useQueries({
    queries: workflows.map((workflow) => ({
      queryKey: ["workflow-versions", workflow.workflow_key],
      queryFn: () => listWorkflowVersions(workflow.workflow_key),
    })),
  })
  const versions = useMemo(
    () => versionQueries
      .flatMap((query) => query.data?.items ?? [])
      .filter((version) => version.workflow_schema_version === "workflow-v3")
      .sort((left, right) => right.id - left.id),
    [versionQueries],
  )
  const [selectedVersionId, setSelectedVersionId] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [switchReason, setSwitchReason] = useState("")
  const [confirmed, setConfirmed] = useState(false)
  const [executionMode, setExecutionMode] = useState<"real" | "simulated">("simulated")
  const [showAllVersions, setShowAllVersions] = useState(false)
  const current = pointer.data?.binding ?? null
  const currentIssue = current ? incrementalBindingExecutionIssue(current) : null
  const candidate = versions.find((version) => version.id === selectedVersionId) ?? null
  const candidatePolicy = candidate?.manifest.resource_policy as { incremental_autorun?: { model_execution_mode?: string } } | undefined
  const simulationOnly = candidatePolicy?.incremental_autorun?.model_execution_mode === "simulated"
  const canActivate = Boolean(
    me.data?.is_admin
    || me.data?.permissions?.includes("*")
    || me.data?.permissions?.includes("incremental_mechanisms:activate"),
  )
  const diffRows = candidate
    ? mechanismDiffRows(current?.workflow_manifest ?? null, candidate.manifest)
    : []
  const visibleVersions = useMemo(
    () => visibleIncrementalMechanismVersions(versions, {
      selectedVersionId,
      currentVersionId: current?.workflow_version_id ?? null,
      expanded: showAllVersions,
    }),
    [current?.workflow_version_id, selectedVersionId, showAllVersions, versions],
  )

  useEffect(() => {
    setShowAllVersions(false)
    setExecutionMode("simulated")
    setConfirmed(false)
  }, [categoryKey])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedVersionId != null && versions.some((item) => item.id === selectedVersionId)) return
    const firstEligible = versions.find(mechanismVersionEligible)
    setSelectedVersionId(firstEligible?.id ?? null)
    setConfirmed(false)
  }, [selectedVersionId, versions])

  async function refreshPointer() {
    await Promise.all([
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["incremental-mechanism", categoryKey] }),
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["incremental-mechanism-events", categoryKey] }),
    ])
  }

  const activate = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => {
      if (!candidate) throw new Error("请先选择一个完整评测机制版本")
      return setIncrementalMechanism(categoryKey, {
        workflow_version_id: candidate.id,
        model_execution_mode: executionMode,
        expected_lock_version: current?.lock_version ?? null,
        reason: switchReason.trim(),
      })
    },
    onSuccess: async () => {
      await refreshPointer()
      setSwitchReason("")
      setConfirmed(false)
      toast.success("该类目的增量评测当前启用版本已切换")
    },
    onError: async (error) => {
      if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 409) await refreshPointer()
      toast.error(error.message)
    },
  })
  const rollback = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (eventId: number) => rollbackIncrementalMechanism(categoryKey, {
      event_id: eventId,
      expected_lock_version: current?.lock_version ?? 0,
      reason: switchReason.trim(),
    }),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      await refreshPointer()
      setSwitchReason("")
      setConfirmed(false)
      toast.success("已回滚到上一完整评测机制版本")
    },
    onError: async (error) => {
      if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 409) await refreshPointer()
      toast.error(error.message)
    },
  })
  const busy = activate.isPending || rollback.isPending
  const canSubmit = Boolean(
    candidate
    && mechanismVersionEligible(candidate)
    && (candidate.id !== current?.workflow_version_id || executionMode !== current?.model_execution_mode)
    && !(simulationOnly && executionMode === "real")
    && switchReason.trim()
    && confirmed
    && !busy,
  )

  return (
    <section className="space-y-5 visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-5 py-5" data-testid="incremental-activation-panel">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">增量自动化</p>
          <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-bold">每个类目只启用一个完整评测机制</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">存量回归仍可每次自由选择；只有增量接单会固定使用这里指定的版本。</p>
        </div>
        <Badge tone={current && !currentIssue ? "success" : "warning"}>
          {current ? (currentIssue ? "已阻断" : "已设置") : "尚未设置"}
        </Badge>
      </header>

      <div className={`rounded-md border px-4 py-3 ${currentIssue ? "border-warning bg-warning-wash" : "border-line bg-surface-sunken"}`}>
        <p className="text-xs font-bold">当前增量机制使用</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm">
          {current
            ? `评测机制版本 ${current.workflow_version} · 由 ${current.updated_by} 于 ${formatTime(current.updated_at)} 设置`
            : "尚未设置；新到达的增量内容会安全阻止，不会误用存量回归选择。"}
        </p>
        {currentIssue && (
          <p className="mt-2 flex items-start gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-warning">
            <WarningCircle className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
            <span>当前绑定已不能安全执行：{currentIssue}。新到达的增量内容会被安全阻止，请切换到兼容版本。</span>
          </p>
        )}
        {current && <p className="mt-2 text-xs">执行模式：{current.model_execution_mode === "real" ? "真实调用，按模型用量计费" : current.model_execution_mode === "simulated" ? "模拟执行，不调用真实模型" : "沿用历史机制策略，未新增真实调用授权"}</p>}
      </div>

      <div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"><h3 className="text-sm font-bold">可选的评测机制版本</h3><span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{versions.length} 个版本</span></div>
        <div className={`mt-3 grid gap-2 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 ${showAllVersions ? "max-h-[520px] overflow-y-auto pr-1" : ""}`}>
          {visibleVersions.map((version) => {
            const activationIssue = mechanismVersionActivationIssue(version)
            const eligible = activationIssue === null
            return (
              <label key={version.id} className={`visual-native-choice cursor-pointer border rounded-md px-4 py-3 ${selectedVersionId === version.id ? "border-primary bg-alias-surface-053" : "border-[var(--line)] bg-white"}`}>
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <input type="radio" name="incremental-mechanism-version" className="visual-native-radio mt-1" checked={selectedVersionId === version.id} disabled={!eligible} onChange={() => { setSelectedVersionId(version.id); setConfirmed(false) }} />
                  <div className="min-w-0"><p className="text-sm font-bold">评测机制版本 {version.version}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{Array.isArray(version.manifest.nodes) ? version.manifest.nodes.length : 0} 个流程步骤 · {formatTime(version.created_at)}</p><Badge className="mt-2" tone={eligible ? "success" : "warning"}>{eligible ? "可用于增量评测" : activationIssue}</Badge></div>
                </div>
              </label>
            )
          })}
          {!versions.length && <p className="border border-dashed border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-6 text-sm text-[var(--muted)] rounded-md">还没有完整评测机制版本，请先进入流程图生成。</p>}
        </div>
        {versions.length > 6 && (
          <Button className="mt-3" size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => setShowAllVersions((currentValue) => !currentValue)}>
            {showAllVersions ? "收起历史版本" : `查看全部 ${versions.length} 个版本`}
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>

      {candidate && (
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold">完整机制差异</h3>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">逐节点核对提示词、规则模块、模型、最大 Token 与思考层级。</p>
          <div className="mt-3 overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full min-w-[760px] border-collapse text-left text-xs">
              <thead><tr className="border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-040"><th className="px-3 py-2">流程步骤</th><th className="px-3 py-2">当前</th><th className="px-3 py-2">准备启用</th><th className="px-3 py-2">变化</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>{diffRows.map((row) => <tr key={row.node_instance_id} className="border-b border-[var(--line)]"><td className="px-3 py-3 font-semibold">{row.label}</td><td className="px-3 py-3 text-[var(--muted)]">{row.current}</td><td className="px-3 py-3">{row.candidate}</td><td className="px-3 py-3"><Badge tone={row.changed ? "warning" : "neutral"}>{row.changed ? "有变化" : "相同"}</Badge></td></tr>)}</tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {canActivate ? (
        <div className="grid gap-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_auto] lg:items-end">
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <fieldset className="space-y-2 text-xs">
              <legend className="mb-2 font-bold">后续增量内容的执行模式</legend>
              <label className="visual-native-choice flex items-start gap-2"><input className="visual-native-radio" type="radio" name="incremental-execution-mode" checked={executionMode === "simulated"} onChange={() => { setExecutionMode("simulated"); setConfirmed(false) }} /><span>模拟执行：验证流程，不调用真实模型。</span></label>
              <label className="visual-native-choice flex items-start gap-2"><input className="visual-native-radio" type="radio" name="incremental-execution-mode" checked={executionMode === "real"} disabled={simulationOnly} onChange={() => { setExecutionMode("real"); setConfirmed(false) }} /><span>真实执行：之后到达的内容将自动调用模型并产生费用；用量随新增内容和模型价格变化。</span></label>
              {simulationOnly && <p className="font-semibold">该冻结机制明确限定模拟执行，不能改为真实调用。</p>}
            </fieldset>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold">切换原因<Textarea className="mt-2 min-h-20" value={switchReason} placeholder="例如：空间图回归集 200 条通过，知识图谱字段合同校验通过" onChange={(event) => setSwitchReason(event.target.value)} /></label>
            <label className="visual-native-choice flex items-start gap-2 leading-5"><input type="checkbox" className="visual-native-checkbox mt-1" checked={confirmed} onChange={(event) => setConfirmed(event.target.checked)} /><span>我已核对完整机制差异，确认之后到达的增量内容使用所选版本{executionMode === "real" ? "并自动真实调用模型，承担相应费用" : "进行模拟执行"}；已接单内容仍保留原快照。</span></label>
          </div>
          <Button disabled={!canSubmit} onClick={() => activate.mutate()}><CheckCircle />设为该类目增量评测当前启用</Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex gap-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]"><LockKey className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" /><p>当前账号可以查看版本和切换历史；只有系统管理员可以改变增量评测当前启用版本。</p></div>
      )}

      <details className="visual-native-details border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4">
        <summary className="font-bold">查看切换与回滚记录</summary>
        <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">
          {(events.data?.items ?? []).slice().reverse().map((event) => (
            <article key={event.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-y border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-3 text-xs">
              <div><p className="font-semibold">{event.action === "rollback" ? "回滚" : "切换"}到版本 ID {event.next_workflow_version_id} · {event.next_model_execution_mode === "real" ? "真实执行" : event.next_model_execution_mode === "simulated" ? "模拟执行" : "历史机制策略"}</p><p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">{event.reason} · {event.actor} · {formatTime(event.created_at)}</p></div>
              {canActivate && event.previous_workflow_version_id != null && <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={busy || !switchReason.trim() || !confirmed} onClick={() => {
                const mode = event.previous_model_execution_mode === "real" ? "真实执行，之后到达的内容会自动调用模型并产生费用" : event.previous_model_execution_mode === "simulated" ? "模拟执行" : "历史机制策略，可能按该版本配置产生模型调用费用"
                if (window.confirm(`确认回滚到版本 ID ${event.previous_workflow_version_id}，恢复${mode}？`)) rollback.mutate(event.id)
              }}><ArrowCounterClockwise />回滚到该记录的上一版本</Button>}
            </article>
          ))}
          {!events.isLoading && !events.data?.items.length && <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]"><WarningCircle />还没有切换记录。</p>}
        </div>
      </details>
    </section>
  )
}

function formatTime(value: string) {
  return formatPlatformTime(value, value)
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/incremental-mechanism.ts =====
import type {
  IncrementalMechanismBinding,
  WorkflowV3Manifest,
  WorkflowV3Node,
  WorkflowVersionSummary,
} from "./types.ts"
import { ruleModuleLabels } from "./runtime-node-details.ts"

export type MechanismDiffRow = {
  node_instance_id: string
  label: string
  current: string
  candidate: string
  changed: boolean
}

function objectValue(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
    : {}
}

function thinkingLabel(value: unknown): string {
  return ({
    none: "关闭思考",
    auto: "自动思考",
    low: "快速思考",
    medium: "平衡思考",
    high: "深度思考",
  } as Record<string, string>)[String(value)] ?? "思考层级未记录"
}

function componentVersion(config: Record<string, unknown>): string {
  const component = objectValue(config.component_snapshot)
  const value = component.version
    ?? component.version_id
    ?? config.prompt_version_id
    ?? config.rule_module_version_id
  return value == null ? "未选择" : String(value)
}

export function mechanismNodeSummary(node: WorkflowV3Node): string {
  const config = objectValue(node.config)
  if (node.kind === "prompt") {
    const model = objectValue(config.model_snapshot)
    const modelName = model.name ?? model.model_id ?? "模型未记录"
    const maxTokens = typeof config.max_tokens === "number"
      ? `${config.max_tokens} Token`
      : "Token 未记录"
    const thinkingLevel = config.thinking_enabled === true ? config.thinking_level : "none"
    return `提示词版本 ${componentVersion(config)} · 模型 ${String(modelName)} · ${maxTokens} · ${thinkingLabel(thinkingLevel)}`
  }
  if (node.kind === "rule_module") {
    const moduleTypeKey = typeof config.module_type_key === "string"
      ? config.module_type_key
      : ""
    return `${ruleModuleLabels[moduleTypeKey] ?? "规则模块"} · 模块版本 ${componentVersion(config)}`
  }
  if (node.kind === "upstream_connector") return "读取上游数据"
  if (node.kind === "downstream_projection") return "写入下游系统"
  if (node.kind === "branch") return "分成多条路线"
  if (node.kind === "join") return "汇合多条路线"
  if (node.kind === "image_first_seen_lookup") return "查询图片最早出现时间"
  if (node.kind === "foreach_image") return "按案例图片逐项运行冻结提示词"
  if (node.kind === "aggregate_case") return "聚合案例图片结果"
  return "工作流步骤"
}

function flowPositionSummary(
  node: WorkflowV3Node,
  manifest: WorkflowV3Manifest,
): string {
  const names = new Map(
    manifest.nodes.map((item) => [item.node_instance_id, item.display_name]),
  )
  const incoming = manifest.edges
    .filter((edge) => edge.to === node.node_instance_id)
    .map((edge) => names.get(edge.from) ?? edge.from)
    .sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right, "zh-CN"))
  const outgoing = manifest.edges
    .filter((edge) => edge.from === node.node_instance_id)
    .map((edge) => {
      const target = names.get(edge.to) ?? edge.to
      return edge.branch ? `${edge.branch}：${target}` : target
    })
    .sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right, "zh-CN"))
  return `流程位置：${incoming.length ? incoming.join("、") : "开始"} → ${outgoing.length ? outgoing.join("、") : "结束"}`
}

function mechanismNodeWithFlowSummary(
  node: WorkflowV3Node,
  manifest: WorkflowV3Manifest,
): string {
  return `${mechanismNodeSummary(node)} · ${flowPositionSummary(node, manifest)}`
}

function v3Manifest(value: WorkflowV3Manifest | Record<string, unknown>): WorkflowV3Manifest | null {
  if (
    value.schema_version !== "workflow-v3"
    || !Array.isArray(value.nodes)
    || !Array.isArray(value.edges)
    || !Array.isArray(value.run_modes)
  ) return null
  return value as WorkflowV3Manifest
}

const nativeIncrementalNodeKinds = new Set([
  "upstream_connector",
  "prompt",
  "foreach_image",
  "aggregate_case",
  "rule_module",
  "branch",
  "join",
  "image_first_seen_lookup",
  "downstream_projection",
])

function nativeIncrementalExecutionIssue(
  manifest: WorkflowV3Manifest,
  laneLabel: "真实增量" | "真实存量",
): string | null {
  const flowLabel = `${laneLabel}流程`
  const byId = new Map(
    manifest.nodes.map((node) => [node.node_instance_id, node]),
  )
  const incoming = new Map(manifest.nodes.map((node) => [node.node_instance_id, [] as string[]]))
  const outgoing = new Map(manifest.nodes.map((node) => [node.node_instance_id, [] as string[]]))
  for (const edge of manifest.edges) {
    if (!byId.has(edge.from) || !byId.has(edge.to)) return "流程连线引用了不存在的步骤"
    outgoing.get(edge.from)?.push(edge.to)
    incoming.get(edge.to)?.push(edge.from)
  }
  const entries = manifest.nodes.filter(
    (node) => incoming.get(node.node_instance_id)?.length === 0,
  )
  if (entries.length !== 1) return `${flowLabel}必须有且只有一个入口`
  const unsupported = manifest.nodes.find(
    (node) => !nativeIncrementalNodeKinds.has(node.kind),
  )
  if (unsupported) return `${flowLabel}还不能运行“${unsupported.display_name}”这类步骤`

  const initialInputs = manifest.nodes.filter((node) => {
    const config = objectValue(node.config)
    return node.kind === "upstream_connector"
      && (config.input_mode ?? "initial_intake") === "initial_intake"
  })
  if (initialInputs.length !== 1) {
    return `${flowLabel}必须有且只有一个“读取上游：本轮最初输入”步骤`
  }
  if (entries[0]?.node_instance_id !== initialInputs[0]?.node_instance_id) {
    return `${flowLabel}必须从“读取上游：本轮最初输入”步骤开始`
  }
  if (!manifest.nodes.some((node) => node.kind === "downstream_projection")) {
    return `${flowLabel}至少需要一个“写入下游”步骤`
  }

  const visited = new Set<string>()
  const visiting = new Set<string>()
  const visit = (nodeId: string): boolean => {
    if (visiting.has(nodeId)) return false
    if (visited.has(nodeId)) return true
    visiting.add(nodeId)
    for (const next of outgoing.get(nodeId) ?? []) {
      if (!visit(next)) return false
    }
    visiting.delete(nodeId)
    visited.add(nodeId)
    return true
  }
  if (!visit(entries[0].node_instance_id)) return `${flowLabel}不能包含循环`
  if (visited.size !== manifest.nodes.length) return "流程中还有没有接入主链路的步骤"

  const waits = new Map<string, WorkflowV3Node[]>()
  const resumes = new Map<string, WorkflowV3Node[]>()
  for (const node of manifest.nodes) {
    const config = objectValue(node.config)
    if (
      node.kind === "downstream_projection"
      && (config.completion_mode ?? "continue") === "await_follow_up"
    ) {
      const key = typeof config.continuation_key === "string"
        ? config.continuation_key.trim()
        : ""
      if (!key) return "等待外部处理的步骤还没有填写续跑标识"
      waits.set(key, [...(waits.get(key) ?? []), node])
    }
    if (
      node.kind === "upstream_connector"
      && (config.input_mode ?? "initial_intake") === "continuation_event"
    ) {
      const key = typeof config.continuation_key === "string"
        ? config.continuation_key.trim()
        : ""
      if (!key) return "接收外部处理结果的步骤还没有填写续跑标识"
      if ((incoming.get(node.node_instance_id) ?? []).length === 0) {
        return "接收外部处理结果不能作为流程入口"
      }
      resumes.set(key, [...(resumes.get(key) ?? []), node])
    }
  }
  for (const key of new Set([...waits.keys(), ...resumes.keys()])) {
    const waitRows = waits.get(key) ?? []
    const resumeRows = resumes.get(key) ?? []
    if (waitRows.length !== 1 || resumeRows.length !== 1) {
      return `续跑标识“${key}”的等待步骤与接收步骤必须一一配对`
    }
    const wait = waitRows[0]
    const resume = resumeRows[0]
    const waitConfig = objectValue(wait.config)
    if (
      waitConfig.resume_node_instance_id !== resume.node_instance_id
      || (outgoing.get(wait.node_instance_id) ?? []).length !== 1
      || outgoing.get(wait.node_instance_id)?.[0] !== resume.node_instance_id
      || (incoming.get(resume.node_instance_id) ?? []).length !== 1
      || incoming.get(resume.node_instance_id)?.[0] !== wait.node_instance_id
    ) {
      return `续跑标识“${key}”的等待步骤与接收步骤配对不正确`
    }
  }
  return null
}

const nativeStockNodeKinds = new Set([
  "upstream_connector",
  "prompt",
  "foreach_image",
  "aggregate_case",
  "rule_module",
  "branch",
  "join",
  "image_first_seen_lookup",
  "downstream_projection",
])

function nativeStockExecutionIssue(manifest: WorkflowV3Manifest): string | null {
  if (manifest.lane_key === "knowledge_graph" && manifest.category_key !== "knowledge_graph") {
    return "历史 3D&SU 知识图谱链路仅供查看；新回归请使用知识图谱打标类目"
  }
  const unsupported = manifest.nodes.find(
    (node) => !nativeStockNodeKinds.has(node.kind),
  )
  if (unsupported) {
    return `原生存量执行器尚未注册“${unsupported.display_name}”这类步骤`
  }
  return null
}

export function mechanismVersionActivationIssue(
  version: WorkflowVersionSummary,
): string | null {
  const manifest = v3Manifest(version.manifest)
  if (version.workflow_schema_version !== "workflow-v3" || !manifest) {
    return "不是工作流管理器生成的完整机制"
  }
  if (version.validation_report.ok !== true) return "流程检查尚未通过"
  if (!manifest.run_modes.includes("incremental")) return "需在流程用途勾选正式增量后重新生成"
  return nativeIncrementalExecutionIssue(manifest, "真实增量")
}

export function incrementalBindingExecutionIssue(
  binding: IncrementalMechanismBinding,
): string | null {
  const manifest = v3Manifest(binding.workflow_manifest)
  if (!manifest) return "当前绑定不是工作流管理器生成的完整机制"
  if (binding.validation_report.ok !== true) return "当前绑定的流程检查已经失效"
  if (!manifest.run_modes.includes("incremental")) return "当前绑定没有声明正式增量用途"
  return nativeIncrementalExecutionIssue(manifest, "真实增量")
}

export function mechanismVersionStockIssue(
  version: WorkflowVersionSummary,
): string | null {
  const manifest = v3Manifest(version.manifest)
  if (version.workflow_schema_version !== "workflow-v3" || !manifest) {
    return "不是工作流管理器生成的完整机制"
  }
  if (version.validation_report.ok !== true) return "流程检查尚未通过"
  if (!manifest.run_modes.includes("stock_regression")) return "没有声明存量回归用途"
  // run178：旧版本冻结的模型 ID 已在模型管理里改掉，400 条素材全部未调模型即失败。
  // 服务端算好的偏差直接在下拉框里标出来，别让运营选到必失败的版本。
  if (version.model_snapshot_issue) return `模型已更新，需重新生成版本：${version.model_snapshot_issue}`
  return nativeStockExecutionIssue(manifest)
}

export function mechanismVersionEligible(version: WorkflowVersionSummary): boolean {
  return mechanismVersionActivationIssue(version) === null
}

export function mechanismDiffRows(
  currentManifest: WorkflowV3Manifest | Record<string, unknown> | null,
  candidateManifest: WorkflowV3Manifest | Record<string, unknown>,
): MechanismDiffRow[] {
  const current = currentManifest ? v3Manifest(currentManifest) : null
  const candidate = v3Manifest(candidateManifest)
  if (!candidate) return []
  const currentById = new Map(
    (current?.nodes ?? []).map((node) => [node.node_instance_id, node]),
  )
  const candidateById = new Map(
    candidate.nodes.map((node) => [node.node_instance_id, node]),
  )
  const candidateRows = candidate.nodes.map((node) => {
    const previous = currentById.get(node.node_instance_id)
    const currentSummary = previous
      ? mechanismNodeWithFlowSummary(previous, current as WorkflowV3Manifest)
      : "当前机制没有这个步骤"
    const candidateSummary = mechanismNodeWithFlowSummary(node, candidate)
    return {
      node_instance_id: node.node_instance_id,
      label: node.display_name,
      current: currentSummary,
      candidate: candidateSummary,
      changed: currentSummary !== candidateSummary,
    }
  })
  const removedRows = (current?.nodes ?? [])
    .filter((node) => !candidateById.has(node.node_instance_id))
    .map((node) => ({
      node_instance_id: node.node_instance_id,
      label: node.display_name,
      current: mechanismNodeWithFlowSummary(node, current as WorkflowV3Manifest),
      candidate: "准备启用机制没有这个步骤",
      changed: true,
    }))
  return [...candidateRows, ...removedRows]
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/incremental-version-presentation.ts =====
export function visibleIncrementalMechanismVersions<T extends { id: number }>(
  versions: readonly T[],
  options: {
    selectedVersionId: number | null
    currentVersionId: number | null
    expanded: boolean
    limit?: number
  },
): T[] {
  if (options.expanded || versions.length <= (options.limit ?? 6)) return [...versions]

  const limit = Math.max(1, options.limit ?? 6)
  const pinnedIds = [options.selectedVersionId, options.currentVersionId]
    .filter((value): value is number => value != null)
  const visible: T[] = []
  const seen = new Set<number>()
  const add = (item: T | undefined) => {
    if (!item || seen.has(item.id)) return
    visible.push(item)
    seen.add(item.id)
  }

  for (const item of versions.slice(0, limit)) add(item)
  for (const id of pinnedIds) add(versions.find((item) => item.id === id))
  return visible
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/json-schema-editor.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from "react"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import {
  DEFAULT_OUTPUT_CONTRACT,
  normalizeSchemaDraft,
  normalizeSchemaNode,
  parseSchemaJson,
  providerResponseFormat,
} from "./json-schema-model"
import type {
  JsonSchemaNode,
  JsonSchemaType,
  PromptOutputContractDraft,
} from "./types"

type OutputMode = "none" | "json_object" | "json_schema"
type SavedOutputContract = PromptOutputContractDraft | { mode: "json_object" }

type Props = {
  nodeKey: string
  value: unknown
  onChange: (value: SavedOutputContract | undefined) => void
}

function valueMode(value: unknown): OutputMode {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return "none"
  const mode = (value as Record<string, unknown>).mode
  return mode === "json_schema" || mode === "json_object" ? mode : "none"
}

function valueDraft(value: unknown): PromptOutputContractDraft {
  if (valueMode(value) !== "json_schema") return normalizeSchemaDraft(DEFAULT_OUTPUT_CONTRACT)
  const source = value as Record<string, unknown>
  try {
    return normalizeSchemaDraft({
      mode: "json_schema",
      name: typeof source.name === "string" ? source.name : "workflow_result",
      description: typeof source.description === "string" ? source.description : "",
      strict: true,
      schema: normalizeSchemaNode(source.schema),
    })
  } catch {
    return normalizeSchemaDraft(DEFAULT_OUTPUT_CONTRACT)
  }
}

export function JsonSchemaEditor({ nodeKey, value, onChange }: Props) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<OutputMode>(() => valueMode(value))
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<PromptOutputContractDraft>(() => valueDraft(value))
  const [advanced, setAdvanced] = useState(false)
  const [rawSchema, setRawSchema] = useState(() => JSON.stringify(valueDraft(value).schema, null, 2))
  const [rawError, setRawError] = useState("")

  useEffect(() => {
    const nextMode = valueMode(value)
    const nextDraft = valueDraft(value)
    setMode(nextMode)
    setDraft(nextDraft)
    setRawSchema(JSON.stringify(nextDraft.schema, null, 2))
    setRawError("")
    setAdvanced(false)
    // nodeKey is the identity boundary. Omitting value preserves an in-progress
    // schema locally when the operator temporarily switches to another mode.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [nodeKey])

  function commitDraft(next: PromptOutputContractDraft) {
    const normalized = normalizeSchemaDraft(next)
    setDraft(normalized)
    setRawSchema(JSON.stringify(normalized.schema, null, 2))
    setRawError("")
    if (mode === "json_schema") onChange(normalized)
  }

  function changeMode(next: OutputMode) {
    setMode(next)
    if (next === "json_schema") onChange(draft)
    else if (next === "json_object") onChange({ mode: "json_object" })
    else onChange(undefined)
  }

  function applyRawSchema() {
    try {
      commitDraft({ ...draft, schema: parseSchemaJson(rawSchema) })
    } catch (error) {
      setRawError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Schema 无法解析")
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="@container/schema min-w-0 space-y-4 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-3 py-4">
      <div>
        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-foreground">结构化输出</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
          输出字段在这里配置，提示词正文无需再手写 JSON 格式说明。
        </p>
      </div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">
        <span className="mb-2 block">输出约束方式</span>
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={mode}
          onChange={(event) => changeMode(event.target.value as OutputMode)}
        >
          <option value="none">不额外约束</option>
          <option value="json_object">只要求 JSON 对象（兼容旧机制）</option>
          <option value="json_schema">严格 JSON Schema（推荐）</option>
        </select>
      </label>

      {mode === "json_schema" && (
        <>
          <div className="grid gap-3 @[24rem]/schema:grid-cols-2">
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">
              <span className="mb-2 block">合同名称</span>
              <Input
                value={draft.name}
                maxLength={64}
                onChange={(event) => commitDraft({ ...draft, name: event.target.value })}
                placeholder="例如 aesthetic_result"
              />
            </label>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">
              <span className="mb-2 block">合同说明</span>
              <Input
                value={draft.description}
                onChange={(event) => commitDraft({ ...draft, description: event.target.value })}
                placeholder="说明本节点产出什么"
              />
            </label>
          </div>
          <div className="border border-[var(--line)] bg-white p-3 rounded-md">
            <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold">输出字段</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">严格模式固定启用 strict=true，所有对象都禁止未声明字段。</p>
              </div>
              <span className="rounded-md bg-alias-surface-011 px-2 py-1 font-data text-xs font-bold">STRICT</span>
            </div>
            <ObjectFieldsEditor
              schema={draft.schema}
              onChange={(schema) => commitDraft({ ...draft, schema })}
            />
          </div>
          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setAdvanced((current) => !current)}>
            {advanced ? "收起高级 JSON" : "编辑高级 JSON"}
          </Button>
          {advanced && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <textarea
                className="visual-native-textarea min-h-64 w-full font-data"
                value={rawSchema}
                spellCheck={false}
                onChange={(event) => setRawSchema(event.target.value)}
              />
              {rawError && <p className="text-xs text-danger">{rawError}</p>}
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={applyRawSchema}>应用 JSON</Button>
            </div>
          )}
          <details className="visual-native-details">
            <summary className="font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">查看供应商参数预览</summary>
            <pre className="mt-2 max-h-64 overflow-auto rounded-md bg-alias-surface-001 p-3 font-data text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-050">
              {JSON.stringify(providerResponseFormat(draft), null, 2)}
            </pre>
          </details>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function ObjectFieldsEditor({
  schema,
  onChange,
  depth = 0,
}: {
  schema: JsonSchemaNode
  onChange: (schema: JsonSchemaNode) => void
  depth?: number
}) {
  const properties = schema.properties ?? {}
  const required = new Set(schema.required ?? [])

  function replaceFields(nextProperties: Record<string, JsonSchemaNode>, nextRequired = required) {
    onChange({
      ...schema,
      type: "object",
      properties: nextProperties,
      required: Array.from(nextRequired).filter((key) => key in nextProperties),
      additionalProperties: false,
    })
  }

  function addField() {
    let index = Object.keys(properties).length + 1
    while (`field_${index}` in properties) index += 1
    const key = `field_${index}`
    replaceFields({ ...properties, [key]: { type: "string" } }, new Set([...required, key]))
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {Object.entries(properties).map(([key, field]) => (
        <SchemaFieldEditor
          key={key}
          fieldKey={key}
          schema={field}
          required={required.has(key)}
          depth={depth}
          onRename={(nextKey) => {
            const normalizedKey = nextKey.trim()
            if (!normalizedKey || (normalizedKey !== key && normalizedKey in properties)) return
            const entries = Object.entries(properties).map(([currentKey, current]) => (
              currentKey === key ? [normalizedKey, current] : [currentKey, current]
            ))
            const nextRequired = new Set(Array.from(required).map((item) => item === key ? normalizedKey : item))
            replaceFields(Object.fromEntries(entries), nextRequired)
          }}
          onRequired={(checked) => {
            const nextRequired = new Set(required)
            checked ? nextRequired.add(key) : nextRequired.delete(key)
            replaceFields(properties, nextRequired)
          }}
          onChange={(next) => replaceFields({ ...properties, [key]: next })}
          onRemove={() => {
            const next = { ...properties }
            delete next[key]
            const nextRequired = new Set(required)
            nextRequired.delete(key)
            replaceFields(next, nextRequired)
          }}
        />
      ))}
      <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={addField}>+ 添加字段</Button>
    </div>
  )
}

function SchemaFieldEditor({
  fieldKey,
  schema,
  required,
  depth,
  onRename,
  onRequired,
  onChange,
  onRemove,
}: {
  fieldKey: string
  schema: JsonSchemaNode
  required: boolean
  depth: number
  onRename: (key: string) => void
  onRequired: (required: boolean) => void
  onChange: (schema: JsonSchemaNode) => void
  onRemove: () => void
}) {
  const type = primaryType(schema.type)
  const itemType = primaryType(schema.items?.type ?? "string")
  return (
    <div className="@container/field min-w-0 space-y-3 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken p-3" style={{ marginLeft: depth * 6 }}>
      <div className="grid gap-2 @[22rem]/field:grid-cols-[minmax(100px,1fr)_110px_auto]">
        <Input value={fieldKey} onChange={(event) => onRename(event.target.value)} aria-label="字段名" />
        <select
          className="visual-native-select"
          value={type}
          onChange={(event) => onChange(schemaForType(event.target.value as JsonSchemaType, schema))}
        >
          {(["string", "integer", "number", "boolean", "array", "object"] as JsonSchemaType[]).map((item) => (
            <option key={item} value={item}>{item}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <button type="button" className="visual-native-button text-danger" onClick={onRemove}>删除</button>
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-2 @[22rem]/field:grid-cols-2">
        <Input value={schema.title ?? ""} onChange={(event) => onChange({ ...schema, title: event.target.value || undefined })} placeholder="字段显示名（可选）" />
        <Input value={schema.description ?? ""} onChange={(event) => onChange({ ...schema, description: event.target.value || undefined })} placeholder="字段说明（可选）" />
      </div>
      <label className="visual-native-choice flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold">
        <input className="visual-native-checkbox" type="checkbox" checked={required} onChange={(event) => onRequired(event.target.checked)} />
        必填字段
      </label>
      {type === "string" && (
        <Input
          value={(schema.enum ?? []).join("，")}
          onChange={(event) => onChange({
            ...schema,
            enum: event.target.value.split(/[，,]/).map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean),
          })}
          placeholder="枚举值（可选，用逗号分隔）"
        />
      )}
      {type === "array" && (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">
            <span className="mb-1 block">数组元素类型</span>
            <select
              className="visual-native-select w-full"
              value={itemType}
              onChange={(event) => onChange({ ...schema, items: schemaForType(event.target.value as JsonSchemaType, schema.items) })}
            >
              {(["string", "integer", "number", "boolean", "object"] as JsonSchemaType[]).map((item) => <option key={item} value={item}>{item}</option>)}
            </select>
          </label>
          {itemType === "object" && schema.items && (
            <ObjectFieldsEditor schema={schema.items} depth={depth + 1} onChange={(items) => onChange({ ...schema, items })} />
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      {type === "object" && (
        <ObjectFieldsEditor schema={schema} depth={depth + 1} onChange={onChange} />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function primaryType(type: JsonSchemaNode["type"]): JsonSchemaType {
  if (!Array.isArray(type)) return type
  return type.find((item) => item !== "null") ?? type[0] ?? "string"
}

function schemaForType(type: JsonSchemaType, previous?: JsonSchemaNode): JsonSchemaNode {
  const common = {
    ...(previous?.title ? { title: previous.title } : {}),
    ...(previous?.description ? { description: previous.description } : {}),
  }
  if (type === "object") {
    return {
      ...common,
      type,
      properties: previous?.properties ?? {},
      required: previous?.required ?? [],
      additionalProperties: false,
    }
  }
  if (type === "array") {
    return { ...common, type, items: previous?.items ?? { type: "string" } }
  }
  return { ...common, type }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/json-schema-model.ts =====
import type {
  JsonSchemaNode,
  JsonSchemaType,
  PromptOutputContractDraft,
} from "./types.ts"

const JSON_TYPES = new Set<JsonSchemaType>([
  "object",
  "array",
  "string",
  "integer",
  "number",
  "boolean",
  "null",
])

export const EMPTY_OBJECT_SCHEMA: JsonSchemaNode = {
  type: "object",
  properties: {},
  required: [],
  additionalProperties: false,
}

export const DEFAULT_OUTPUT_CONTRACT: PromptOutputContractDraft = {
  mode: "json_schema",
  name: "workflow_result",
  description: "提示词节点结构化输出",
  strict: true,
  schema: EMPTY_OBJECT_SCHEMA,
}

function record(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) {
    throw new Error("JSON Schema 节点必须是对象")
  }
  return value as Record<string, unknown>
}

function normalizeType(value: unknown): JsonSchemaType | JsonSchemaType[] {
  if (typeof value === "string" && JSON_TYPES.has(value as JsonSchemaType)) {
    return value as JsonSchemaType
  }
  if (
    Array.isArray(value)
    && value.length > 0
    && value.every((item) => typeof item === "string" && JSON_TYPES.has(item as JsonSchemaType))
  ) {
    return Array.from(new Set(value)) as JsonSchemaType[]
  }
  throw new Error("JSON Schema type 不受支持")
}

function includesType(value: JsonSchemaNode["type"], expected: JsonSchemaType) {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.includes(expected) : value === expected
}

function optionalText(source: Record<string, unknown>, key: "title" | "description" | "pattern") {
  const value = source[key]
  return typeof value === "string" && value ? value : undefined
}

function optionalFiniteNumber(source: Record<string, unknown>, key: keyof JsonSchemaNode) {
  const value = source[key]
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : undefined
}

function optionalNonNegativeInteger(
  source: Record<string, unknown>,
  key: "minItems" | "maxItems" | "minLength" | "maxLength",
) {
  if (!(key in source)) return undefined
  const value = source[key]
  if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isInteger(value) || value < 0) {
    throw new Error(`${key} 必须是非负整数`)
  }
  return value
}

export function normalizeSchemaNode(value: unknown): JsonSchemaNode {
  const source = record(value)
  const type = normalizeType(source.type)
  const normalized: JsonSchemaNode = { type }
  const title = optionalText(source, "title")
  const description = optionalText(source, "description")
  if (title) normalized.title = title
  if (description) normalized.description = description
  if (Array.isArray(source.enum) && source.enum.length) {
    normalized.enum = source.enum.filter(
      (item): item is string | number | boolean | null => (
        item === null
        || typeof item === "string"
        || typeof item === "boolean"
        || (typeof item === "number" && Number.isFinite(item))
      ),
    )
  }
  if ("const" in source) {
    const constant = source.const
    if (
      constant === null
      || typeof constant === "string"
      || typeof constant === "boolean"
      || (typeof constant === "number" && Number.isFinite(constant))
    ) {
      normalized.const = constant
    }
  }

  if (includesType(type, "object")) {
    const rawProperties = source.properties && typeof source.properties === "object" && !Array.isArray(source.properties)
      ? source.properties as Record<string, unknown>
      : {}
    const properties = Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(rawProperties)
        .filter(([key]) => Boolean(key.trim()))
        .map(([key, child]) => [key.trim(), normalizeSchemaNode(child)]),
    )
    const required = Array.isArray(source.required)
      ? Array.from(new Set(source.required.filter(
        (key): key is string => typeof key === "string" && key in properties,
      )))
      : []
    normalized.properties = properties
    normalized.required = required
    normalized.additionalProperties = false
  }

  if (includesType(type, "array")) {
    normalized.items = normalizeSchemaNode(source.items ?? { type: "string" })
  }

  for (const key of ["minItems", "maxItems", "minLength", "maxLength"] as const) {
    const number = optionalNonNegativeInteger(source, key)
    if (number !== undefined) Object.assign(normalized, { [key]: number })
  }
  for (const [minimumKey, maximumKey] of [
    ["minItems", "maxItems"],
    ["minLength", "maxLength"],
  ] as const) {
    const minimum = normalized[minimumKey]
    const maximum = normalized[maximumKey]
    if (minimum !== undefined && maximum !== undefined && minimum > maximum) {
      throw new Error(`${minimumKey}/${maximumKey} 的最小值不能大于最大值`)
    }
  }
  for (const key of [
    "minimum",
    "maximum",
    "exclusiveMinimum",
    "exclusiveMaximum",
  ] as const) {
    const number = optionalFiniteNumber(source, key)
    if (number !== undefined) Object.assign(normalized, { [key]: number })
  }
  const pattern = optionalText(source, "pattern")
  if (pattern) normalized.pattern = pattern
  return normalized
}

export function normalizeSchemaDraft(
  draft: PromptOutputContractDraft,
): PromptOutputContractDraft {
  const schema = normalizeSchemaNode(draft.schema)
  if (!includesType(schema.type, "object")) {
    throw new Error("严格结构化输出的顶层必须是 object")
  }
  return {
    mode: "json_schema",
    name: draft.name.trim() || "workflow_result",
    description: draft.description.trim(),
    strict: true,
    schema,
  }
}

export function parseSchemaJson(raw: string): JsonSchemaNode {
  let parsed: unknown
  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(raw)
  } catch (error) {
    throw new Error("高级 JSON 不是合法 JSON", { cause: error })
  }
  return normalizeSchemaNode(parsed)
}

export function providerResponseFormat(contract: PromptOutputContractDraft) {
  const normalized = normalizeSchemaDraft(contract)
  return {
    type: "json_schema" as const,
    json_schema: {
      name: normalized.name,
      description: normalized.description,
      strict: true as const,
      schema: normalized.schema,
    },
  }
}

import { ArrowLeft, CaretDown, CaretRight, Check, Sparkle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import type { ReactNode } from "react"

import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell"

export function NodeEditorShell({
  index,
  title,
  description,
  backHref,
  typeLabel,
  categoryLabel,

  workingRevision,

  description: string

                >
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="visual-native-button block w-full border-0 bg-transparent p-0 text-left hover:bg-transparent"
                    onClick={() => setExpandedVersionId((current) => (current === version.id ? null : version.id))}
                    data-testid={`version-card-${version.id}`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <p className="font-data flex min-w-0 items-start gap-1 text-sm font-bold">
                        {expandedVersionId === version.id ? <CaretDown size={12} className="mt-1 shrink-0" /> : <CaretRight size={12} className="mt-1 shrink-0" />}

            <div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <Badge tone="active">{typeLabel}</Badge>
                <Badge tone="neutral">{categoryLabel}</Badge>
                <Badge tone="neutral">{canEdit ? "正在编辑下一版本" : "只读查看"}</Badge>
              </div>
              <p className="font-data mt-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]"><span className="visual-code">{assetKey}</span> · 工作区第 {workingRevision ?? "—"} 次修改</p>
            </div>
            <p className="max-w-md text-right text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">已有版本保持不变；工作流只会引用明确选择的版本。</p>
          </div>

            <div className="mt-5 rounded-md border border-alias-edge-034 bg-alias-surface-079 px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033">
