          )}

          {!canEdit && <p className="mt-5 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">当前角色仅可查看内容，编辑请联系系统管理员授权。</p>}
          <fieldset disabled={!canEdit} className="mt-7 min-w-0">{children}</fieldset>
          {secondaryContent && <section className="mt-10">{secondaryContent}</section>}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function VersionContentView({ version }: { version: ComponentVersionSummary }) {
  const content = version.content
  if (!content) {
    return (
      <p className="mt-3 border-t border-dashed border-[var(--line)] pt-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
        该版本未返回内容，请刷新后重试。
      </p>
    )
  }
  const sections: { label: string; text: string }[] = []
  if (version.kind === "prompt") {
    const systemPrompt = typeof content.system_prompt === "string" ? content.system_prompt : ""
    const userPrompt = typeof content.user_prompt === "string" ? content.user_prompt : ""
    sections.push({ label: `系统提示词 · ${systemPrompt.length} 字符`, text: systemPrompt || "（空）" })
    sections.push({ label: `用户提示词 · ${userPrompt.length} 字符`, text: userPrompt || "（空）" })
  } else {
    sections.push({ label: "模块内容摘要", text: operatorRuleVersionSummary(content) })
  }
  return (
    <div className="mt-3 space-y-3 border-t border-dashed border-[var(--line)] pt-3">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
        <span>{version.created_by}</span>
        <span>{formatPlatformTime(version.created_at)}</span>
      </div>
      {sections.map((section) => (
        <div key={section.label}>
          <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{section.label}</p>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="visual-native-button border-0 bg-transparent px-2 cursor-pointer text-[var(--muted)] underline decoration-dotted hover:text-[var(--ink)]"
              onClick={() => { void navigator.clipboard?.writeText(section.text) }}
            >
              复制
            </button>
          </div>
          <pre className="max-h-64 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-2 py-2 text-xs leading-4 scrollbar-thin rounded-md">
            {section.text}
          </pre>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/node-inspector.tsx =====
import { ArrowSquareOut, CaretLeft, CaretRight, Trash, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { Link } from "react-router-dom"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import {
  useComponentVersions,
  useConnectorContracts,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/api"
import { JsonSchemaEditor } from "@/features/workflow-manager/json-schema-editor"
import {
  foreachItemExecutor,
  formatFeaturePaths,
  isSafeWorkflowDotPath,
  parseFeaturePaths,
  patchForeachItemExecutor,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/foreach-contract"
import { ShortCircuitEditor } from "@/features/workflow-manager/short-circuit-editor"

import type {

const thinkingLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  none: "关闭思考（默认）",
  auto: "自动选择",
  low: "快速思考",
  medium: "平衡思考",
  high: "深度思考",
}

type Props = {
  node: WorkflowV3Node | null
  nodes: WorkflowV3Node[]
  assets: EvaluationComponentAsset[]
  models: ModelRegistryOption[]
  categoryKey: string
  levelOptions: WorkflowLevelOption[]
  conflictMessage?: string | null
  onRename: (name: string) => void
  onPatchConfig: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => void
  onPatchErrorPolicy: (policy: Record<string, unknown>) => void
  onPatchRetryPolicy: (policy: Record<string, unknown>) => void
  onRemove: () => void
  collapsed?: boolean
  onCollapsedChange?: (collapsed: boolean) => void
  children?: ReactNode
  readOnly?: boolean
}

export function WorkflowNodeInspector({
  node,
  nodes,
  assets,
  models,
  categoryKey,
  levelOptions,
  conflictMessage,
  onRename,
  onPatchConfig,
  onPatchErrorPolicy,
  onPatchRetryPolicy,
  onRemove,
  collapsed = false,
  onCollapsedChange,
  children,
  readOnly = false,
}: Props) {
  const selectedConfig = node?.kind === "foreach_image"
    ? foreachItemExecutor(node.config)
    : node?.config
  const selectedAsset = node?.kind === "prompt" || node?.kind === "rule_module" || node?.kind === "foreach_image"
    ? assets.find((asset) => asset.asset_key === selectedConfig?.asset_key) ?? null
    : null
  const versions = useComponentVersions(selectedAsset?.id ?? null)
  const connectorContracts = useConnectorContracts(categoryKey)
  const setup = node ? workflowNodeSetupState(node) : null

  if (collapsed) {
    return (
      <aside data-testid="workflow-node-inspector" className="min-h-0 overflow-hidden border-l border-[var(--line)] bg-white py-3">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="visual-control min-w-hit font-semibold flex min-h-32 w-full flex-col items-center gap-3 px-2 py-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)] hover:bg-alias-surface-051 hover:text-foreground"
          aria-label="展开节点设置"
          onClick={() => onCollapsedChange?.(false)}
        >
          <CaretLeft />
          <span className="[writing-mode:vertical-rl]">节点设置</span>
        </button>
      </aside>
    )
  }

  return (
    <aside data-testid="workflow-node-inspector" className="max-h-[520px] min-h-0 overflow-y-auto border-l border-[var(--line)] bg-white xl:max-h-none">
      <div className="border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">正在设置</p>
            <h2 className="mt-1 truncate text-lg font-bold">{node?.display_name ?? "请选择一个步骤"}</h2>
          </div>
          {onCollapsedChange && (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="visual-native-button size-11 shrink-0 p-0 text-[var(--muted)] hover:bg-alias-surface-051 hover:text-foreground"
              aria-label="收起节点设置"
              onClick={() => onCollapsedChange(true)}
            >
              <CaretRight />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        {setup && (
          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <Badge tone={setup.state === "configured" ? "success" : "warning"}>{setup.label}</Badge>
            <span className="text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]">{setup.summary}</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      {!node ? (
        <p className="px-5 py-8 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">在画布上点选一个步骤，即可配置它调用的提示词、规则、模型或接口。</p>
      ) : (
        <fieldset disabled={readOnly} className="@container/inspector min-w-0 space-y-5 px-5 py-5">
          <div className="rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-3">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold">这个节点做什么</p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{nodePurpose(node)}</p>
          </div>
          <Field label="步骤名称">
            <Input value={node.display_name} onChange={(event) => onRename(event.target.value)} />
          </Field>

          {node.kind === "prompt" && (
            <PromptConfiguration
              node={node}
              assets={assets}
              models={models}
              categoryKey={categoryKey}
              selectedAsset={selectedAsset}
              versions={versions.data?.items ?? []}
              onPatch={onPatchConfig}
            />
          )}
          {node.kind === "foreach_image" && (
            <ForeachImageConfiguration
              node={node}
              assets={assets}
              models={models}
              categoryKey={categoryKey}
              selectedAsset={selectedAsset}
              versions={versions.data?.items ?? []}
              onPatch={onPatchConfig}
            />
          )}
          {node.kind === "aggregate_case" && (
            <AggregateCaseConfiguration
              node={node}
              nodes={nodes}
              onPatch={onPatchConfig}
            />
          )}
          {node.kind === "rule_module" && (
            <RuleConfiguration
              node={node}
              assets={assets}
              selectedAsset={selectedAsset}
              versions={versions.data?.items ?? []}
              conflictMessage={conflictMessage}
              onPatch={onPatchConfig}
            />
          )}
          {(node.kind === "upstream_connector" || node.kind === "downstream_projection") && (
            <ConnectorConfiguration
              node={node}
              nodes={nodes}
              contracts={connectorContracts.data?.items ?? []}
              isLoading={connectorContracts.isLoading}
              onPatch={onPatchConfig}
            />
          )}
          {node.kind === "image_first_seen_lookup" && (
            <FirstSeenConfiguration
              node={node}
              contracts={connectorContracts.data?.items ?? []}
              isLoading={connectorContracts.isLoading}
              onPatch={onPatchConfig}
            />
          )}
          {node.kind === "branch" && (
            <>
              <Field label="这些路线怎样执行？">
                <select
                  className="visual-native-select w-full"
                  value={String(node.config.mode ?? "parallel")}
                  onChange={(event) => onPatchConfig({ mode: event.target.value })}
                >
                  <option value="parallel">同时执行多条路线</option>
                  <option value="exclusive">按条件选择一条路线</option>
                </select>
              </Field>
              <Field label="各条路线名称（用逗号分开）">
                <Input
                  value={Array.isArray(node.config.branches) ? node.config.branches.join("，") : ""}
                  onChange={(event) => onPatchConfig({
                    branches: event.target.value.split(/[，,]/).map((value) => value.trim()).filter(Boolean),
                  })}
                />
              </Field>
              <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                {node.config.mode === "exclusive"
                  ? "至少填写两条不重名路线；连好下一步后，再为每条路线设置容易理解的执行条件。"
                  : "至少填写两条不重名路线，再分别连接后续步骤；这些路线会同时执行。"}
              </p>
            </>
          )}
          {node.kind === "join" && (
            <>
              <Field label="汇合哪一组路线？">
                <select
                  className="visual-native-select w-full"
                  value={String(node.config.join_for ?? "")}
                  onChange={(event) => onPatchConfig({ join_for: event.target.value })}
                >
                  <option value="">请选择分路线步骤</option>
                  {nodes.filter((item) => item.kind === "branch").map((item) => (
                    <option key={item.node_instance_id} value={String(item.config.branch_key ?? "")}>
                      {item.display_name}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </Field>
              <Field label="什么时候继续？">
                <select
                  className="visual-native-select w-full"
                  value={String(node.config.mode ?? "all")}
                  onChange={(event) => onPatchConfig({ mode: event.target.value })}
                >
                  <option value="all">等待所有路线完成</option>
                </select>
              </Field>
            </>
          )}

          <Field label="这个节点的运行角色">
            <Input
              value={String(node.config.execution_role ?? "")}
              onChange={(event) => onPatchConfig({
                execution_role: event.target.value.trim() || undefined,
              })}
              placeholder="例如 classification、grading、level_matching"
            />
          </Field>
          {(node.kind === "prompt" || node.kind === "foreach_image" || node.kind === "rule_module" || node.kind === "image_first_seen_lookup") && (
            <ShortCircuitEditor
              node={node}
              levels={levelOptions}
              onPatch={onPatchConfig}
            />
          )}

          <Field label="失败后怎样处理？">
            <select
              className="visual-native-select w-full"
              value={String(node.error_policy.kind ?? "fail")}
              onChange={(event) => onPatchErrorPolicy({ kind: event.target.value })}
            >
              <option value="fail">结束本条并记录失败</option>
              <option value="manual_review">转人工处理</option>
              <option value="continue">记录失败证据后继续</option>
              <option value="skip">记录失败证据并跳过</option>
            </select>
          </Field>
          <Field label="失败后是否重试？">
            <select
              className="visual-native-select w-full"
              value={String(node.retry_policy.kind ?? "fixed")}
              onChange={(event) => onPatchRetryPolicy({
                kind: event.target.value,
                max_attempts: Math.min(5, Math.max(1, Number(node.retry_policy.max_attempts) || 1)),
                delay_seconds: Math.min(86400, Math.max(0, Number(node.retry_policy.delay_seconds) || 0)),
              })}
            >
              <option value="none">不自动重试</option>
              <option value="fixed">固定间隔重试</option>
              <option value="exponential">递增间隔重试</option>
            </select>
          </Field>
          {node.retry_policy.kind !== "none" && (
            <>
              <Field label="失败前最多尝试几次">
                <Input
                  type="number"
                  min={1}
                  max={5}
                  value={Number(node.retry_policy.max_attempts ?? 1)}
                  onChange={(event) => onPatchRetryPolicy({
                    kind: node.retry_policy.kind === "exponential" ? "exponential" : "fixed",
                    max_attempts: Math.min(5, Math.max(1, Number(event.target.value) || 1)),
                    delay_seconds: Math.min(86400, Math.max(0, Number(node.retry_policy.delay_seconds) || 0)),
                  })}
                />
              </Field>
              <Field label="首次重试等待秒数">
                <Input
                  type="number"
                  min={0}
                  max={86400}
                  value={Number(node.retry_policy.delay_seconds ?? 0)}
                  onChange={(event) => onPatchRetryPolicy({
                    kind: node.retry_policy.kind === "exponential" ? "exponential" : "fixed",
                    max_attempts: Math.min(5, Math.max(1, Number(node.retry_policy.max_attempts) || 1)),
                    delay_seconds: Math.min(86400, Math.max(0, Number(event.target.value) || 0)),
                  })}
                />
              </Field>
            </>
          )}

          {children}
          <Button className="w-full" variant="ghost" onClick={onRemove}>
            <Trash />删除这个步骤
          </Button>
        </fieldset>
      )}
    </aside>
  )
}

function ConnectorConfiguration({
  node,
  nodes,
  contracts,
  isLoading,
  onPatch,
}: {
  node: WorkflowV3Node
  nodes: WorkflowV3Node[]
  contracts: import("@/features/workflow-manager/types").ConnectorContractSummary[]
  isLoading: boolean
  onPatch: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => void
}) {
  const direction = node.kind === "upstream_connector" ? "upstream" : "downstream"
  const selectedId = Number(node.config.connector_contract_version_id ?? 0)
  const options = contracts.filter(
    (item) => item.direction === direction
      && (item.status === "active" || item.id === selectedId),
  )
  const selected = options.find((item) => item.id === selectedId)
  const isUpstream = node.kind === "upstream_connector"
  const inputMode = String(node.config.input_mode ?? "initial_intake")
  const completionMode = String(node.config.completion_mode ?? "continue")
  const continuationNodes = nodes.filter((item) => (
    item.kind === "upstream_connector"
    && item.node_instance_id !== node.node_instance_id
    && item.config.input_mode === "continuation_event"
  ))
  return (
    <>
      {node.kind === "downstream_projection" && <KnowledgeGraphFieldVersion node={node} onPatch={onPatch} />}
      <Field label="调用哪个接口版本？">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={selectedId || ""}
          onChange={(event) => {
            const contract = options.find(
              (item) => item.id === Number(event.target.value),
            )
            onPatch(contract ? {
              connector_key: contract.contract_key,
              connector_contract_version_id: contract.id,
              connector_snapshot: undefined,
            } : {
              connector_key: `logical.unconfigured.${direction === "upstream" ? "input" : "output"}`,
              connector_contract_version_id: undefined,
              connector_snapshot: undefined,
            })
          }}
        >
          <option value="">{isLoading ? "正在读取可用接口" : "请选择已登记接口"}</option>
          {options.map((contract) => (
            <option key={contract.id} value={contract.id}>
              {direction === "upstream" ? "读取上游数据" : "写入下游"}
              {` · ${contract.contract_key} · 合同版本 ${contract.version}`}
              {contract.status === "active" ? "" : "（历史已选版本）"}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </Field>
      {selected ? (
        <div className="rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-3 py-3 text-xs leading-5">
          <p className="font-bold">已选择合同版本 {selected.version}</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">已通过结构检查 · {selected.pause?.state === "paused" ? "当前已暂停" : "当前可用"}</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">尚未选择接口。存量回归仍使用导入素材；正式增量运行前必须选择已登记版本。</p>
      )}
      <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" asChild>
        <Link to="/workflow/governance/connectors"><ArrowSquareOut />前往接口管理</Link>
      </Button>
      {isUpstream ? (
        <>
          <Field label="这一步从哪里取得数据？">
            <select
              className="visual-native-select w-full"
              value={inputMode}
              onChange={(event) => onPatch({
                input_mode: event.target.value,
                ...(event.target.value === "initial_intake"
                  ? { continuation_key: undefined }
                  : {}),
              })}
            >
              <option value="initial_intake">读取本轮最初输入</option>
              <option value="continuation_event">接收外部处理结果</option>
            </select>
          </Field>
          {inputMode === "continuation_event" && (
            <Field label="对应哪次外部处理？">
              <Input
                value={String(node.config.continuation_key ?? "")}
                onChange={(event) => onPatch({ continuation_key: event.target.value.trim() })}
                placeholder="例如：内容去重处理"
              />
            </Field>
          )}
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <Field label="写入后流程怎样继续？">
            <select
              className="visual-native-select w-full"
              value={completionMode}
              onChange={(event) => onPatch({
                completion_mode: event.target.value,
                ...(event.target.value === "continue"
                  ? {
                      continuation_key: undefined,
                      resume_node_instance_id: undefined,
                      wait_timeout_seconds: undefined,
                      timeout_policy: undefined,
                    }
                  : {
                      wait_timeout_seconds: Number(node.config.wait_timeout_seconds ?? 3600),
                      timeout_policy: String(node.config.timeout_policy ?? "manual_review"),
                    }),
              })}
            >
              <option value="continue">写完继续处理</option>
              <option value="await_follow_up">等对方处理后再继续</option>
            </select>
          </Field>
          {completionMode === "await_follow_up" && (
            <>
              <Field label="这次外部处理叫什么？">
                <Input
                  value={String(node.config.continuation_key ?? "")}
                  onChange={(event) => onPatch({ continuation_key: event.target.value.trim() })}
                  placeholder="例如：内容去重处理"
                />
              </Field>
              <Field label="对方返回后从哪一步继续？">
                <select
                  className="visual-native-select w-full"
                  value={String(node.config.resume_node_instance_id ?? "")}
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    const continuation = continuationNodes.find(
                      (item) => item.node_instance_id === event.target.value,
                    )
                    onPatch({
                      resume_node_instance_id: event.target.value,
                      ...(continuation?.config.continuation_key
                        ? { continuation_key: continuation.config.continuation_key }
                        : {}),
                    })
                  }}
                >
                  <option value="">请选择“接收外部处理结果”步骤</option>
                  {continuationNodes.map((item) => (
                    <option key={item.node_instance_id} value={item.node_instance_id}>
                      {item.display_name}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </Field>
              {!continuationNodes.length && (
                <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-040">
                  请先拖入一个“读取上游”步骤，并把它改为“接收外部处理结果”。
                </p>
              )}
              <WaitConfiguration node={node} onPatch={onPatch} />
            </>
          )}
        </>
      )}
      <AdvancedJsonField
        label="字段映射（mapping，JSON）"
        hint="上下游字段与工作流输出字段的对应关系。此前只能由发布器代码写死；留空沿用合同默认映射。"
        value={node.config.mapping}
        onChange={(value) => onPatch({ mapping: value })}
      />
    </>
  )
}

function FirstSeenConfiguration({
  node,
  contracts,
  isLoading,
  onPatch,
}: {
  node: WorkflowV3Node
  contracts: import("@/features/workflow-manager/types").ConnectorContractSummary[]
  isLoading: boolean
  onPatch: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => void
}) {
  const requestId = Number(node.config.request_contract_version_id ?? 0)
  const responseId = Number(node.config.response_contract_version_id ?? 0)
  const requestOptions = contracts.filter((item) => (
    item.direction === "downstream" && (item.status === "active" || item.id === requestId)
  ))
  const responseOptions = contracts.filter((item) => (
    item.direction === "upstream" && (item.status === "active" || item.id === responseId)
  ))
  return (
    <>
      <div className="rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-3 py-3 text-xs leading-5">
        <p className="font-bold">查询图片最早出现时间的含义</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">输出的是供应商最早发现时间，不是图片真实发布时间，平台也不会自动判断图片是否过时。</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">超时或没有匹配结果时保持未知；未知不会阻塞工作流，也不会被当成新图或旧图。</p>
      </div>
      <Field label="使用哪个图片查询服务？">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={requestId || ""}
          onChange={(event) => {
            const contract = requestOptions.find((item) => item.id === Number(event.target.value))
            onPatch(contract ? {
              provider_connector_key: contract.contract_key,
              request_contract_version_id: contract.id,
            } : {
              provider_connector_key: "",
              request_contract_version_id: 0,
            })
          }}
        >
          <option value="">{isLoading ? "正在读取可用服务" : "请选择发送查询请求的接口"}</option>
          {requestOptions.map((contract) => (
            <option key={contract.id} value={contract.id}>
              {contract.contract_key} · 查询请求合同版本 {contract.version}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </Field>
      <Field label="查询结果合同版本">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={responseId || ""}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ response_contract_version_id: Number(event.target.value) })}
        >
          <option value="">请选择接收查询结果的接口</option>
          {responseOptions.map((contract) => (
            <option key={contract.id} value={contract.id}>
              {contract.contract_key} · 查询结果合同版本 {contract.version}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </Field>
      <WaitConfiguration node={node} onPatch={onPatch} />
      <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">存量回归不会联网：有导入证据时回放，没有证据时明确显示“本轮未联网检测”。</p>
      <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" asChild>
        <Link to="/workflow/governance/connectors"><ArrowSquareOut />前往接口管理</Link>
      </Button>
    </>
  )
}

function WaitConfiguration({
  node,
  onPatch,
}: {
  node: WorkflowV3Node
  onPatch: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => void
}) {
  const waitHours = Math.max(1, Math.round(Number(node.config.wait_timeout_seconds ?? 3600) / 3600))
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-3 @[26rem]/inspector:grid-cols-2">
      <Field label="等待多久算超时？">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Input
            type="number"
            min={1}
            max={168}
            value={waitHours}
            onChange={(event) => onPatch({ wait_timeout_seconds: Number(event.target.value) * 3600 })}
          />
          <span className="shrink-0 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">小时</span>
        </div>
      </Field>
      <Field label="超时后怎么办？">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={String(node.config.timeout_policy ?? "manual_review")}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ timeout_policy: event.target.value })}
        >
          <option value="manual_review">转人工处理</option>
          <option value="fail">结束本条并记录失败</option>
        </select>
      </Field>
    </div>
  )
}

function ForeachImageConfiguration({
  node,
  assets,
  models,
  categoryKey,
  selectedAsset,
  versions,
  onPatch,
}: {
  node: WorkflowV3Node
  assets: EvaluationComponentAsset[]
  models: ModelRegistryOption[]
  categoryKey: string
  selectedAsset: EvaluationComponentAsset | null
  versions: Array<{ id: number; version: string }>
  onPatch: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => void
}) {
  const executor = foreachItemExecutor(node.config)
  const nestedNode: WorkflowV3Node = {
    ...node,
    node_instance_id: `${node.node_instance_id}.item_executor`,
    kind: "prompt",
    display_name: `${node.display_name} · 逐图提示词`,
    config: executor,
  }
  const patchExecutor = (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => {
    const next = patchForeachItemExecutor(node.config, patch)
    onPatch({ item_executor: next.item_executor })
  }
  const itemsPath = String(node.config.items_path ?? "")
  const identityPath = String(node.config.item_identity_path ?? "")
  return (
    <>
      <Field label="逐图来源路径">
        <Input
          value={itemsPath}
          aria-invalid={!isSafeWorkflowDotPath(itemsPath)}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ items_path: event.target.value })}
          placeholder="$.case.images"
        />
      </Field>
      <Field label="图片稳定身份路径">
        <Input
          value={identityPath}
          aria-invalid={!isSafeWorkflowDotPath(identityPath)}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ item_identity_path: event.target.value })}
          placeholder="$.cloud_id"
        />
      </Field>
      <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
        只允许 $.字段.子字段；不执行 JSONPath 表达式、脚本或数组选择器。身份在同一案例内必须非空且唯一。
      </p>
      <div className="grid gap-3 @[26rem]/inspector:grid-cols-2">
        <Field label="单案例图片硬上限">
          <Input
            type="number"
            min={1}
            max={10000}
            value={Number(node.config.max_items ?? 1000)}
            onChange={(event) => onPatch({ max_items: Math.min(10000, Math.max(1, Number(event.target.value) || 1)) })}
          />
        </Field>
        <Field label="逐图内部并发">
          <Input
            type="number"
            min={1}
            max={64}
            value={Number(node.config.max_parallel_items ?? 4)}
            onChange={(event) => onPatch({ max_parallel_items: Math.min(64, Math.max(1, Number(event.target.value) || 1)) })}
          />
        </Field>
      </div>
      <Field label="单图失败后">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={String(node.config.failure_policy ?? "fail_open")}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ failure_policy: event.target.value })}
        >
          <option value="fail_open">保留失败事实，继续案例级流程</option>
          <option value="fail_closed">终止案例级流程</option>
        </select>
      </Field>
      <div className="rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-3 py-3">
        <p className="text-xs font-bold">逐图提示词</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">每张图片独立调用下面同一个冻结提示词、模型、严格输出和图片策略。</p>
      </div>
      <PromptConfiguration
        node={nestedNode}
        assets={assets}
        models={models}
        categoryKey={categoryKey}
        selectedAsset={selectedAsset}
        versions={versions}
        onPatch={patchExecutor}
      />
    </>
  )
}

function AggregateCaseConfiguration({
  node,
  nodes,
  onPatch,
}: {
  node: WorkflowV3Node
  nodes: WorkflowV3Node[]
  onPatch: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => void
}) {
  const included = Array.isArray(node.config.included_statuses)
    ? node.config.included_statuses.map(String)
    : ["succeeded"]
  const toggleStatus = (status: string) => {
    const next = included.includes(status)
      ? included.filter((value) => value !== status)
      : [...included, status]
    onPatch({ included_statuses: next })
  }
  return (
    <>
      <Field label="案例聚合来源">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={String(node.config.foreach_node_id ?? "")}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ foreach_node_id: event.target.value })}
        >
          <option value="">请选择逐图片节点</option>
          {nodes.filter((item) => item.kind === "foreach_image").map((item) => (
            <option key={item.node_instance_id} value={item.node_instance_id}>{item.display_name}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </Field>
      <Field label="参与聚合的状态">
        <span className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          {[["succeeded", "成功"], ["failed", "失败"], ["skipped", "跳过"]].map(([value, label]) => (
            <label key={value} className="visual-native-choice inline-flex items-center gap-2 font-normal text-foreground">
              <input className="visual-native-checkbox" type="checkbox" checked={included.includes(value)} onChange={() => toggleStatus(value)} />
              {label}
            </label>
          ))}
        </span>
      </Field>
      <Field label="分数路径">
        <Input
          value={String(node.config.score_path ?? "$.score")}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ score_path: event.target.value })}
          placeholder="$.score"
        />
      </Field>
      <Field label="特征路径（每行 名称=$.字段.子字段）">
        <textarea
          className="visual-native-textarea min-h-28 w-full"
          value={formatFeaturePaths(node.config.feature_paths)}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ feature_paths: parseFeaturePaths(event.target.value) })}
          placeholder={"style=$.features.style\nroom=$.features.room"}
        />
      </Field>
      <Field label="没有成功图片时">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={String(node.config.empty_policy ?? "fail")}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ empty_policy: event.target.value })}
        >
          <option value="fail">明确失败</option>
          <option value="emit_empty">输出空聚合结果</option>
        </select>
      </Field>
      <Field label="失败图片投影">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={String(node.config.failure_projection ?? "include")}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ failure_projection: event.target.value })}
        >
          <option value="include">保留失败图片清单和证据</option>
          <option value="exclude">不投影失败详情（仍保留计数）</option>
        </select>
      </Field>
      <AdvancedJsonField
        label="分数分桶（score_buckets，JSON）"
        hint="案例聚合时把逐图分数归入的区间定义。此前只能由发布器代码写死；留空沿用默认分桶。"
        value={node.config.score_buckets}
        onChange={(value) => onPatch({ score_buckets: value })}
      />
    </>
  )
}

function PromptConfiguration({
  node,
  assets,
  models,
  categoryKey,
  selectedAsset,
  versions,
  onPatch,
}: {
  node: WorkflowV3Node
  assets: EvaluationComponentAsset[]
  models: ModelRegistryOption[]
  categoryKey: string
  selectedAsset: EvaluationComponentAsset | null
  versions: Array<{ id: number; version: string }>
  onPatch: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => void
}) {
  const promptAssets = assets.filter((asset) => asset.kind === "prompt" && asset.is_discoverable)
  const selectedModel = models.find(
    (model) => model.id === Number(node.config.model_registry_id ?? 0),
  )
  const visionCapability = selectedModel
    ? resolveVisionCapabilityProfile(
        selectedModel.vision_capability_profile,
        selectedModel.provider,
        selectedModel.model_id,
      )
    : null
  const inputModality = String(node.config.input_modality ?? "image")
  const historicalVisionPolicy = Boolean(
    node.config.vision_input_policy
    && typeof node.config.vision_input_policy === "object"
    && (node.config.vision_input_policy as Record<string, unknown>).version === "legacy-default",
  )
  const maxTokenLimit = Math.min(selectedModel?.max_tokens || 65536, 65536)
  return (
    <>
      <Field label="输入方式">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={inputModality}
          onChange={(event) => {
            const nextModality = event.target.value
            onPatch({
              input_modality: nextModality,
              ...(nextModality === "image" && visionCapability
                ? { vision_input_policy: defaultVisionInputPolicy(visionCapability) }
                : { vision_input_policy: undefined }),
              vision_capability_snapshot: undefined,
              vision_policy_hash: undefined,
            })
          }}
        >
          <option value="image">图片素材（发送图片）</option>
          <option value="text">纯文本汇总（不发送图片）</option>
        </select>
      </Field>
      <Field label="使用哪个提示词节点？">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={String(node.config.asset_key ?? "")}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ asset_key: event.target.value, prompt_version_id: 0 })}
        >
          <option value="">请选择提示词节点</option>
          {promptAssets.map((asset) => <option key={asset.id} value={asset.asset_key}>{asset.display_name}</option>)}
        </select>
      </Field>
      <Field label="使用哪个提示词版本？">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={Number(node.config.prompt_version_id ?? 0)}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ prompt_version_id: Number(event.target.value) })}
        >
          <option value={0}>请选择版本</option>
          {versions.map((version) => <option key={version.id} value={version.id}>版本 {version.version}</option>)}
        </select>
      </Field>
      {selectedAsset && (
        <Link className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold underline" to={`/workflow/governance/workflow-manager/prompts?category_key=${encodeURIComponent(categoryKey)}&asset_key=${encodeURIComponent(selectedAsset.asset_key)}`}>
          编辑提示词内容与版本 <ArrowSquareOut />
        </Link>
      )}
      {!versions.length && selectedAsset && <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-040">这个提示词还没有可选版本，请先进入内容页生成版本。</p>}
      <Field label="使用哪个模型？">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={Number(node.config.model_registry_id ?? 0)}
          onChange={(event) => {
            const model = models.find((item) => item.id === Number(event.target.value))
            const capability = model
              ? resolveVisionCapabilityProfile(model.vision_capability_profile, model.provider, model.model_id)
              : null
            let nextVisionPolicy: unknown = undefined
            if (inputModality === "image" && capability) {
              try {
                nextVisionPolicy = normalizeVisionInputPolicy(
                  node.config.vision_input_policy,
                  capability,
                  { historical: historicalVisionPolicy },
                )
              } catch {
                nextVisionPolicy = defaultVisionInputPolicy(capability)
              }
            }
            onPatch({
              model_registry_id: Number(event.target.value),
              ...(model ? { max_tokens: Math.min(model.max_tokens || 4096, 65536) } : {}),
              vision_input_policy: nextVisionPolicy,
              vision_capability_snapshot: undefined,
              vision_policy_hash: undefined,
            })
          }}
        >
          <option value={0}>请选择模型</option>
          {models.map((model) => <option key={model.id} value={model.id}>{model.name} · {model.model_id}</option>)}
        </select>
      </Field>
      <div className="grid gap-3 @[26rem]/inspector:grid-cols-2">
        <Field label="最大输出长度（Token）">
          <Input
            type="number"
            min={1}
            max={maxTokenLimit}
            value={Number(node.config.max_tokens ?? 4096)}
            onChange={(event) => onPatch({ max_tokens: Number(event.target.value) })}
          />
        </Field>
        <Field label="思考层级">
          <select
            className="visual-native-select w-full"
            value={
              node.config.thinking_enabled === true
                ? String(node.config.thinking_level ?? "auto")
                : "none"
            }
            onChange={(event) => {
              const thinkingLevel = event.target.value
              onPatch({
                thinking_level: thinkingLevel,
                thinking_enabled: thinkingLevel !== "none",
              })
            }}
          >
            {Object.entries(thinkingLabels).map(([value, label]) => <option key={value} value={value}>{label}</option>)}
          </select>
        </Field>
      </div>
      {selectedModel && (
        <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
          当前模型最多允许 {maxTokenLimit.toLocaleString()} Token；超过上限时流程检查会阻止生成版本。
        </p>
      )}
      {selectedModel && (!selectedModel.structured_output || !selectedModel.capabilities.includes("structured_output")) && (
        <div className="rounded-md border border-alias-edge-032 bg-alias-surface-071 px-3 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033">
          当前模型没有声明结构化输出能力；选择严格 JSON Schema 后，流程检查会阻止生成版本。
        </div>
      )}
      {inputModality === "image" && selectedModel && (
        <VisionInputPolicyEditor
          value={node.config.vision_input_policy}
          capability={visionCapability}
          historical={historicalVisionPolicy}
          onChange={(visionInputPolicy) => onPatch({
            vision_input_policy: visionInputPolicy,
            vision_capability_snapshot: undefined,
            vision_policy_hash: undefined,
          })}
        />
      )}
      {String(node.config.output_contract_key ?? "").startsWith("kg.knowledge_graph.") ? (
        <KnowledgeGraphFieldVersion node={node} onPatch={onPatch} />
      ) : <JsonSchemaEditor
        nodeKey={node.node_instance_id}
        value={node.config.output_contract ?? (
          (node.config.structured_output ?? selectedModel?.structured_output)
            ? { mode: "json_object" }
            : undefined
        )}
        onChange={(outputContract) => onPatch({
          output_contract: outputContract,
          structured_output: outputContract !== undefined,
        })}
      />}
      <Link className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold underline" to="/workflow/governance/model-registry">
        去模型管理页查看可用模型 <ArrowSquareOut />
      </Link>
    </>
  )
}

export function KnowledgeGraphFieldVersion({ node, onPatch }: {
  node: WorkflowV3Node
  onPatch: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => void
}) {
  const keyField = node.kind === "prompt" ? "output_contract_key" : "field_contract_key"
  const key = String(node.config[keyField] ?? "")
  if (!/^kg\.knowledge_graph\.(domestic|overseas)\.(whole|single)\.v[12]$/.test(key)) return null
  const route = key.slice(0, key.lastIndexOf("."))
  const selectVersion = (version: string) => onPatch({
    [keyField]: `${route}.${version}`,
    ...(node.kind === "prompt" ? {
      output_contract: undefined,
      output_contract_snapshot: undefined,
      structured_output: true,
    } : { field_contract_snapshot: undefined }),
  })
  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <Field label="知识图谱字段版本">
        <select className="visual-native-select w-full" value={key.split(".").at(-1)}
          onChange={(event) => selectVersion(event.target.value)}>
          <option value="v1">v1 · 原有字段</option>
          <option value="v2">v2 · 原有字段＋视角</option>
        </select>
      </Field>
      <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
        提示词节点与对应下游节点须选择同一版本。字段结构由已登记版本生成；保存并生成新机制后生效，历史运行不变。
        v2 视角支持七种取值，无法判断时留空。
      </p>
      {node.kind === "prompt" && node.config.output_contract != null && (
        <Button type="button" variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => selectVersion(key.split(".").at(-1) ?? "v1")}>
          重置为所选版本的字段结构
        </Button>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function RuleConfiguration({
  node,
  assets,
  selectedAsset,
  versions,
  conflictMessage,
  onPatch,
}: {
  node: WorkflowV3Node
  assets: EvaluationComponentAsset[]
  selectedAsset: EvaluationComponentAsset | null
  versions: Array<{ id: number; version: string }>
  conflictMessage?: string | null
  onPatch: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => void
}) {
  const ruleAssets = assets.filter((asset) => asset.kind === "rule_module" && asset.is_discoverable)
  return (
    <>
      <Field label="使用哪个规则模块？">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={String(node.config.asset_key ?? "")}
          onChange={(event) => {
            const asset = ruleAssets.find((item) => item.asset_key === event.target.value)
            onPatch({
              asset_key: event.target.value,
              module_type_key: asset?.module_type_key ?? "",
              rule_module_version_id: 0,
            })
          }}
        >
          <option value="">请选择规则模块</option>
          {ruleAssets.map((asset) => <option key={asset.id} value={asset.asset_key}>{asset.display_name}</option>)}
        </select>
      </Field>
      <Field label="使用哪个模块版本？">
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={Number(node.config.rule_module_version_id ?? 0)}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ rule_module_version_id: Number(event.target.value) })}
        >
          <option value={0}>请选择版本</option>
          {versions.map((version) => <option key={version.id} value={version.id}>版本 {version.version}</option>)}
        </select>
      </Field>
      {selectedAsset && (
        <Link className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold underline" to={`/workflow/governance/workflow-manager/components/${selectedAsset.id}`}>
          编辑模块规则与版本 <ArrowSquareOut />
        </Link>
      )}
      {!versions.length && selectedAsset && <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-040">这个模块还没有可选版本，请先进入规则页完成配置并生成版本。</p>}
      <Field label="这次规则作用范围">
        <Input
          value={String(node.config.scope ?? "default")}
          onChange={(event) => onPatch({ scope: event.target.value || "default" })}
          placeholder="例如：默认结果或路线 A"
        />
      </Field>
      {conflictMessage && (
        <div className="flex gap-2 rounded-md border border-alias-edge-032 bg-alias-surface-071 px-3 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033">
          <WarningCircle className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          <p><strong>重复执行同一规则版本。</strong>{conflictMessage} 可为其中一个节点选择不同模块版本，或调整输入和作用范围。</p>
        </div>
      )}
      <AdvancedJsonField
        label="输入字段绑定（input_bindings，JSON）"
        hint={'规则读取上游节点哪些字段（形如 {"aesthetic_score": "$.prompt_b.aesthetic_score"}）。此前只能由种子代码写死；留空沿用模块默认绑定。'}
        value={node.config.input_bindings}
        onChange={(value) => onPatch({ input_bindings: value })}
      />
    </>
  )
}

function AdvancedJsonField({
  label,
  hint,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string
  hint: string
  value: unknown
  onChange: (value: unknown) => void
}) {
  // 这些键此前只能由种子/发布器代码写死，前端不可见。这里按 JSON 原样暴露，
  // 保存时先本地解析，解析失败不落盘并提示，避免把坏 JSON 写进工作副本。
  const serialized = value === undefined ? "" : JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)
  const [text, setText] = useState(serialized)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  useEffect(() => { setText(serialized); setError(null) }, [serialized])
  return (
    <Field label={label}>
      <textarea
        className="visual-native-textarea min-h-28 w-full font-mono"
        value={text}
        placeholder="留空表示不设置"
        onChange={(event) => setText(event.target.value)}
        onBlur={() => {
          const raw = text.trim()
          if (!raw) { setError(null); if (value !== undefined) onChange(undefined); return }
          try {
            const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw)
            setError(null)
            if (JSON.stringify(parsed) !== JSON.stringify(value)) onChange(parsed)
          } catch (parseError) {
            setError(`JSON 无法解析：${(parseError as Error).message}`)
          }
        }}
      />
      <span className="mt-1 block text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{hint}</span>
      {error && <span className="mt-1 block text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-040">{error}</span>}
    </Field>
  )
}

function Field({ label, children }: { label: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]"><span className="mb-2 block">{label}</span>{children}</label>
}

function nodePurpose(node: WorkflowV3Node): string {
  return ({
    upstream_connector: "从上游业务或存量回归导入素材中取得本轮输入。",
    prompt: "调用选定的提示词版本和模型，把素材转成后续节点可使用的结构化判断。",
    foreach_image: "从冻结案例输入中读取图片列表，按稳定身份逐张调用同一个严格提示词，并保留每张图片的成功或失败事实。",
    aggregate_case: "只读取逐图片节点的冻结输出，确定性汇总分数、特征、失败图片和完整图片级结果。",
    rule_module: "按选定的规则模块版本过滤、修正或撮合上一步结果。",
    branch: "根据前面已经得到的结果，把流程分到一条或多条路线。",
    join: "等待指定路线完成，再把各路结果汇总给下一步。",
    image_first_seen_lookup: "向已登记的外部服务查询图片最早被发现的时间；存量回归不会联网。",
    downstream_projection: "按输出合同整理最终字段；存量回归只写入平台证据，不会触碰生产目标表。",
  } as Record<string, string>)[node.kind] ?? "按当前工作流顺序处理数据，并保留可回放的运行证据。"
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/node-palette.tsx =====
import { CaretLeft, CaretRight, MagnifyingGlass } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMemo, useState } from "react"
import type { DragEvent, KeyboardEvent } from "react"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import {
  buildWorkflowPaletteGroups,
  isWorkflowPaletteActivationKey,
  workflowPaletteGroupDefinitions,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/operator-presentation"
import type {
  EvaluationComponentAsset,
  ModuleTypeDefinition,
  WorkflowPaletteNode,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/types"

type Props = {
  assets: EvaluationComponentAsset[]
  moduleTypes: ModuleTypeDefinition[]
  onAdd: (node: WorkflowPaletteNode) => void
  collapsed?: boolean
  onCollapsedChange?: (collapsed: boolean) => void
  readOnly?: boolean
}

export function WorkflowNodePalette({
  assets,
  moduleTypes,
  onAdd,
  collapsed = false,
  onCollapsedChange,
  readOnly = false,
}: Props) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("")
  const groups = useMemo(
    () => buildWorkflowPaletteGroups(assets, moduleTypes, query),
    [assets, moduleTypes, query],
  )

  function beginDrag(event: DragEvent<HTMLButtonElement>, node: WorkflowPaletteNode) {
    if (readOnly) { event.preventDefault(); return }
    event.dataTransfer.setData("application/x-workflow-node", JSON.stringify(node))
    event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = "copy"
  }

  function addWithKeyboard(event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>, node: WorkflowPaletteNode) {
    if (readOnly) return
    if (!isWorkflowPaletteActivationKey(event.key)) return
    event.preventDefault()
    onAdd(node)
  }

  if (collapsed) {
    return (
      <aside data-testid="workflow-node-palette" className="min-h-0 overflow-hidden border-r border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 py-3">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="visual-control min-w-hit font-semibold flex min-h-32 w-full flex-col items-center gap-3 px-2 py-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)] hover:bg-white hover:text-foreground"
          aria-label="展开步骤库"
          onClick={() => onCollapsedChange?.(false)}
        >
          <CaretRight />
          <span className="[writing-mode:vertical-rl]">步骤库</span>
        </button>
      </aside>
    )
  }

  return (
    <aside data-testid="workflow-node-palette" className="max-h-[420px] min-h-0 overflow-y-auto border-r border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 xl:max-h-none">
      <div className="border-b border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-4">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">可用步骤</p>
            <h2 className="mt-1 text-lg font-bold">步骤库</h2>
          </div>
          {onCollapsedChange && (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="visual-native-button size-11 shrink-0 p-0 text-[var(--muted)] hover:bg-white hover:text-foreground"
              aria-label="收起步骤库"
              onClick={() => onCollapsedChange(true)}
            >
              <CaretLeft />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">拖到画布中；也可以双击快速添加。</p>
      </div>
      <div className="px-4 pb-5">
      <label className="relative mt-4 block">
        <MagnifyingGlass className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[var(--muted)]" />
        <Input
          className="pl-9"
          value={query}
          onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
          placeholder="搜索步骤"
        />
      </label>

      <div className="mt-5 space-y-6">
        {workflowPaletteGroupDefinitions.map((group) => (
          <section key={group.key}>
            <div className="mb-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold">{group.label}</h3>
                <span className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{groups[group.key].length}</span>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]">{group.hint}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {groups[group.key].map((node) => (
                <button
                  key={`${node.kind}:${node.asset_id ?? node.display_name}`}
                  type="button"
                  draggable={!readOnly}
                  disabled={readOnly}
                  className="visual-native-button block w-full px-3 py-3 text-left shadow-card transition-transform duration-[180ms] ease-[var(--visual-ease-out)] hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-raise motion-reduce:translate-y-0"
                  onDragStart={(event) => beginDrag(event, node)}
                  onDoubleClick={() => onAdd(node)}
                  onKeyDown={(event) => addWithKeyboard(event, node)}
                  aria-label={`添加步骤：${node.display_name}`}
                >
                  <span className="block text-sm font-bold">{node.display_name}</span>
                  <span className="mt-1 block text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]">{node.description}</span>
                </button>
              ))}
              {!groups[group.key].length && (
                <p className="border border-dashed border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-4 text-center text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)] rounded-md">
                  {query ? "没有匹配步骤" : group.key === "ai" ? "还没有提示词节点" : group.key === "rules" ? "还没有规则模块" : "暂无步骤"}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          </section>
        ))}
      </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  )
}

import type {

  ModuleTypeDefinition,
  WorkflowPaletteNode,
  WorkflowV3Node,
} from "./types.ts"

export type PaletteGroupKey = "ingress" | "ai" | "rules" | "flow" | "external" | "output"

export const workflowPaletteGroupDefinitions: Array<{
  key: PaletteGroupKey
  label: string
  hint: string
}> = [
  { key: "ingress", label: "数据接入", hint: "读取或接收需要处理的内容" },
  { key: "ai", label: "AI 评测", hint: "调用这个类目已经维护的提示词" },
  { key: "rules", label: "规则与合同", hint: "过滤、降权、区分赛道、扣分或撮合等级" },
  { key: "flow", label: "流程控制", hint: "按条件分路，或等待多条路线完成" },
  { key: "external", label: "外部识别", hint: "调用外部证据服务补充可追溯字段" },
  { key: "output", label: "结果输出", hint: "把结果交给下游业务系统" },
]

const structuralNodes: Record<PaletteGroupKey, WorkflowPaletteNode[]> = {
  ingress: [{
    kind: "upstream_connector",
    display_name: "读取上游",
    description: "从业务系统取得待打标或待评测内容",
  }],
  ai: [{
    kind: "foreach_image",
    display_name: "逐图片 AI 评测",
    description: "对案例中的每张图片分别调用同一个严格提示词",
  }],

      display_name: "汇合路线",

    },

      kind: "aggregate_case",
      display_name: "聚合案例结果",
      description: "确定性汇总逐图片分数、特征和失败事实",
    },
  ],

    kind: "image_first_seen_lookup",

    description: "查询供应商最早发现图片的时间，供下游判断时效",
  }],
  output: [{
    kind: "downstream_projection",
    display_name: "写入下游",
    description: "把结果交给知识图谱或其他业务系统",
  }],
}

export function buildWorkflowPaletteGroups(
  assets: readonly EvaluationComponentAsset[],
  moduleTypes: readonly ModuleTypeDefinition[],
  query: string,
): Record<PaletteGroupKey, WorkflowPaletteNode[]> {
  const prompts = assets
    .filter((asset) => asset.kind === "prompt" && asset.is_discoverable)
    .map<WorkflowPaletteNode>((asset) => ({
      kind: "prompt",
      display_name: asset.display_name,
      description: asset.description || `调用提示词节点 ${asset.asset_key}`,
      asset_id: asset.id,
      asset_key: asset.asset_key,
    }))
  const rules = assets
    .filter((asset) => asset.kind === "rule_module" && asset.is_discoverable)
    .map<WorkflowPaletteNode>((asset) => {
      const definition = moduleTypes.find((item) => item.key === asset.module_type_key)
      return {
        kind: "rule_module",
        display_name: asset.display_name,
        description: asset.description || definition?.description || "按已维护的规则模块处理结果",
        asset_id: asset.id,
        asset_key: asset.asset_key,
        module_type_key: asset.module_type_key ?? undefined,
      }
    })
  const groups: Record<PaletteGroupKey, WorkflowPaletteNode[]> = {
    ...structuralNodes,
    ai: [...structuralNodes.ai, ...prompts],
    rules,
  }
  const normalizedQuery = query.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("zh-CN")
  if (!normalizedQuery) return groups
  return Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(groups).map(([key, items]) => [
      key,
      items.filter((item) => (
        `${item.display_name} ${item.description} ${item.asset_key ?? ""} ${item.module_type_key ?? ""}`
          .toLocaleLowerCase("zh-CN")
          .includes(normalizedQuery)
      )),
    ]),
  ) as Record<PaletteGroupKey, WorkflowPaletteNode[]>
}

export type WorkflowManagerTab = {
  key: "workflow" | "versions" | "prompts" | "rules" | "stock"
  label: string
  href: string
  count: number | null
  domain: "governance" | "stock"
}

export function buildWorkflowManagerTabs({
  categoryKey,
  workflowKey,
  workflowVersionCount,
  promptCount,
  ruleCount,
}: {
  categoryKey: string
  workflowKey?: string
  workflowVersionCount: number
  promptCount: number
  ruleCount: number
}): WorkflowManagerTab[] {
  const encodedCategory = encodeURIComponent(categoryKey)
  const workflowQuery = workflowKey
    ? `&workflow_key=${encodeURIComponent(workflowKey)}`
    : ""
  const workspaceHref = `/workflow/governance/workflow-manager?category_key=${encodedCategory}`
  return [
    {
      key: "workflow",
      label: "工作流",
      href: `${workspaceHref}${workflowQuery}`,
      count: null,
      domain: "governance",
    },
    {
      key: "versions",
      label: "评测机制版本",
      href: `${workspaceHref}#evaluation-mechanisms`,
      count: workflowVersionCount,
      domain: "governance",
    },
    {
      key: "prompts",
      label: "提示词节点",
      href: `${workspaceHref}#prompt-assets`,
      count: promptCount,
      domain: "governance",
    },
    {
      key: "rules",
      label: "规则模块",
      href: `${workspaceHref}#rule-assets`,
      count: ruleCount,
      domain: "governance",
    },
    {
      key: "stock",
      label: "存量回归与纠偏",
      href: `/workflow/optimization/baseline-regression?category_key=${encodedCategory}`,
      count: null,
      domain: "stock",
    },
  ]
}

export function adaptiveWorkflowCanvasSize({
  viewportWidth,
  viewportHeight,
  nodePositions,
}: {
  viewportWidth: number
  viewportHeight: number
  nodePositions: ReadonlyArray<{ x: number; y: number }>
}): { width: number; height: number } {
  const contentWidth = nodePositions.reduce(
    (maximum, position) => Math.max(maximum, position.x + 196 + 24),
    0,
  )
  const contentHeight = nodePositions.reduce(
    (maximum, position) => Math.max(maximum, position.y + 92 + 40),
    0,
  )
  return {
    width: Math.max(640, Math.round(viewportWidth), contentWidth),
    height: Math.max(540, Math.round(viewportHeight), contentHeight),
  }
}

export function workflowCanvasFocusScroll({
  viewportWidth,
  viewportHeight,
  canvasWidth,
  canvasHeight,
  focusPosition,
}: {
  viewportWidth: number
  viewportHeight: number
  canvasWidth: number
  canvasHeight: number
  focusPosition: { x: number; y: number }
}): { left: number; top: number } {
  const nodeCenterX = focusPosition.x + 196 / 2
  const nodeCenterY = focusPosition.y + 92 / 2
  return {
    left: Math.round(clampNumber(
      nodeCenterX - viewportWidth / 2,
      0,
      Math.max(0, canvasWidth - viewportWidth),
    )),
    top: Math.round(clampNumber(
      nodeCenterY - viewportHeight / 2,
      0,
      Math.max(0, canvasHeight - viewportHeight),
    )),
  }
}

export type WorkflowNodeSetupState = {
  state: "configured" | "needs_setup"
  label: "已配置" | "待配置"
  summary: string
}

export function workflowNodeSetupState(node: WorkflowV3Node): WorkflowNodeSetupState {
  if (node.kind === "prompt") {
    const versionId = positiveNumber(node.config.prompt_version_id)
    const modelId = positiveNumber(node.config.model_registry_id)
    const maxTokens = positiveNumber(node.config.max_tokens)
    const configured = Boolean(node.config.asset_key && versionId && modelId && maxTokens)
    const fieldCount = recordPropertyCount(node.output_schema)
    return {
      state: configured ? "configured" : "needs_setup",
      label: configured ? "已配置" : "待配置",
      summary: [
        versionId ? `提示词版本 #${versionId}` : "未选择提示词版本",
        maxTokens ? `${maxTokens.toLocaleString("zh-CN")} Token` : "未设置输出长度",
        fieldCount ? `${fieldCount} 个输出字段` : "输出字段由合同决定",
      ].join(" · "),
    }
  }
  if (node.kind === "foreach_image") {
    const executor = node.config.item_executor && typeof node.config.item_executor === "object"
      ? node.config.item_executor as Record<string, unknown>
      : {}
    const versionId = positiveNumber(executor.prompt_version_id)
    const modelId = positiveNumber(executor.model_registry_id)
    const configured = Boolean(
      node.config.items_path
      && node.config.item_identity_path
      && executor.asset_key
      && versionId
      && modelId,
    )
    return {
      state: configured ? "configured" : "needs_setup",
      label: configured ? "已配置" : "待配置",
      summary: configured
        ? `最多 ${Number(node.config.max_items ?? 0).toLocaleString("zh-CN")} 张 · 内部并发 ${Number(node.config.max_parallel_items ?? 0)}`
        : "请配置图片来源、稳定身份和逐图提示词",
    }
  }
  if (node.kind === "aggregate_case") {
    const configured = Boolean(node.config.foreach_node_id && node.config.score_path)
    return {
      state: configured ? "configured" : "needs_setup",
      label: configured ? "已配置" : "待配置",
      summary: configured
        ? `聚合 ${String(node.config.foreach_node_id)} 的冻结结果`
        : "请选择逐图来源并配置分数路径",
    }
  }
  if (node.kind === "rule_module") {
    const versionId = positiveNumber(node.config.rule_module_version_id)
    const configured = Boolean(node.config.asset_key && versionId)
    return {
      state: configured ? "configured" : "needs_setup",
      label: configured ? "已配置" : "待配置",
      summary: versionId ? `规则模块版本 #${versionId}` : "未选择规则模块版本",
    }
  }
  if (node.kind === "branch") {
    const branches = Array.isArray(node.config.branches) ? node.config.branches : []
    const configured = branches.length >= 2
    return {
      state: configured ? "configured" : "needs_setup",
      label: configured ? "已配置" : "待配置",
      summary: configured ? `${branches.length} 条路线 · ${node.config.mode === "exclusive" ? "按条件选择" : "同时执行"}` : "至少需要两条路线",
    }
  }
  if (node.kind === "join") {
    const configured = Boolean(node.config.join_for)
    return {
      state: configured ? "configured" : "needs_setup",
      label: configured ? "已配置" : "待配置",
      summary: configured ? "等待所需路线完成" : "未选择要汇合的路线",
    }
  }
  if (node.kind === "image_first_seen_lookup") {
    const requestVersion = positiveNumber(node.config.request_contract_version_id)
    const responseVersion = positiveNumber(node.config.response_contract_version_id)
    const configured = Boolean(
      node.config.provider_connector_key && requestVersion && responseVersion,
    )
    return {
      state: configured ? "configured" : "needs_setup",
      label: configured ? "已配置" : "待配置",
      summary: configured
        ? "已锁定查询与回传合同版本"
        : "请选择图片查询服务的接口版本",
    }
  }
  if (node.kind === "upstream_connector" || node.kind === "downstream_projection") {
    const connectorKey = String(node.config.connector_key ?? "")
    const contractVersionId = Number(node.config.connector_contract_version_id ?? 0)
    const configured = Boolean(
      connectorKey
      && !connectorKey.includes("unconfigured")
      && Number.isInteger(contractVersionId)
      && contractVersionId > 0,
    )
    return {
      state: configured ? "configured" : "needs_setup",
      label: configured ? "已配置" : "待配置",
      summary: configured
        ? `${connectorKey} · 已锁定接口版本`
        : "请选择已登记接口版本；存量回归继续使用导入素材",
    }
  }
  return {
    state: "configured",
    label: "已配置",
    summary: "按工作流依赖执行",
  }
}

function positiveNumber(value: unknown): number {
  const parsed = Number(value)
  return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed > 0 ? parsed : 0
}

function clampNumber(value: number, minimum: number, maximum: number): number {
  return Math.min(maximum, Math.max(minimum, value))
}

function recordPropertyCount(schema: Record<string, unknown>): number {
  const properties = schema.properties
  return properties && typeof properties === "object" && !Array.isArray(properties)
    ? Object.keys(properties).length
    : 0
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/rule-module-presentation.ts =====
function recordValue(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  return value != null && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
    : null
}

function namedItems(value: unknown[]): string[] {
  return value
    .map((item) => {
      const label = recordValue(item)?.label
      return typeof label === "string" && label.trim() ? label.trim() : null
    })
    .filter((label): label is string => Boolean(label))
}

const DIFFERENCE_FIELD_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {

  score: "分数",

  level: "等级",
  min_score: "最低分",
  weight: "权重",
  formula: "计算方式",
  merge_strategy: "汇总方式",
  real_photo: "实拍图",
  render_3d: "3D 渲染图",
}

const DIFFERENCE_VALUE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  "redline-policy-v1": "红线规则格式 v1",
  "category-level-scale-v1": "等级区间格式 v1",
  "subcategory-dimensions-v1": "维度合同格式 v1",
  "anchor-mechanism-v1": "锚点校准格式 v1",
}

function operatorDifferenceSegment(segment: string): string {
  if (/^\d+$/.test(segment)) return `第 ${Number(segment) + 1} 条`
  if (DIFFERENCE_FIELD_LABELS[segment]) return DIFFERENCE_FIELD_LABELS[segment]
  if (/^L[1-5]$/i.test(segment)) return segment.toUpperCase()
  return `自定义项「${segment.replaceAll("_", " ")}」`
}

export function operatorDifferenceLabel(path: string): string {
  if (!path || path === "全部内容") return "全部内容"
  return path.split(".").map(operatorDifferenceSegment).join(" / ")
}

export function operatorDifferenceValue(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === undefined || value === null) return "未设置"
  if (typeof value === "boolean") return value ? "已启用" : "未启用"
  if (typeof value === "string") {
    const normalized = value.trim()
    if (!normalized) return "空"
    return DIFFERENCE_VALUE_LABELS[normalized] ?? normalized
  }
  if (typeof value === "number") return String(value)
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    if (!value.length) return "0 条"
    const labels = namedItems(value)
    const suffix = labels.length
      ? `（${labels.slice(0, 3).join("、")}${labels.length > 3 ? "等" : ""}）`
      : ""
    return `${value.length} 条规则${suffix}`
  }
  const record = recordValue(value)
  return record ? `${Object.keys(record).length} 项设置` : String(value)
}

export function operatorRuleVersionSummary(content: Record<string, unknown>): string {
  const lines: string[] = []
  if (typeof content.enabled === "boolean") {
    lines.push(content.enabled ? "已启用" : "未启用")
  }
  if (Array.isArray(content.rules)) lines.push(operatorDifferenceValue(content.rules))
  if (typeof content.hit_level === "string" && content.hit_level) {
    lines.push(`命中后判为 ${content.hit_level}`)
  }
  if (typeof content.hit_score_cap === "number") {
    lines.push(`最高保留 ${content.hit_score_cap} 分`)
  }
  if (typeof content.stop_on_match === "boolean") {
    lines.push(content.stop_on_match ? "命中后停止后续评测" : "命中后继续后续评测")
  }
  return lines.length ? lines.join("\n") : operatorDifferenceValue(content)
}
