}

export const ruleModuleLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  eligibility_redline: "准入与红线过滤",
  track_routing: "赛道区分",
  media_adjustment: "媒介加权与降权",
  quality_cap_exception: "质量上限与例外",
  dimension_scoring: "维度评分与扣分",
  anchor_calibration: "标杆锚点校准",
  score_level_matcher: "分数与等级撮合",
  dictionary_filter: "字典表撞库过滤",
  classification_level_map: "分类定级表",
  upstream_type_short_circuit: "上游类型前置短路",
}

const thinkingLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  none: "关闭思考",
  auto: "自动思考",
  low: "快速思考",
  medium: "平衡思考",
  high: "深度思考",
}

function objectValue(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
    : {}
}

export function runtimeNodeDetails(snapshot: unknown): RuntimeNodeDetail[] {
  const root = objectValue(snapshot)
  const workflow = objectValue(root.workflow)
  const manifest = objectValue(workflow.manifest)
  const nodes = Array.isArray(manifest.nodes) ? manifest.nodes : []
  return nodes.flatMap((rawNode): RuntimeNodeDetail[] => {
    const node = objectValue(rawNode)
    const nodeInstanceId = typeof node.node_instance_id === "string"
      ? node.node_instance_id
      : typeof node.key === "string"
        ? node.key
        : ""
    if (!nodeInstanceId) return []
    const kind = typeof node.kind === "string" ? node.kind : "unknown"
    const config = objectValue(node.config)
    const component = objectValue(config.component_snapshot)
    const model = objectValue(config.model_snapshot)
    const moduleTypeKey = typeof config.module_type_key === "string"
      ? config.module_type_key
      : typeof component.module_type_key === "string"
        ? component.module_type_key
        : null
    const rawVersion = component.version ?? component.version_id
    const maxTokens = typeof config.max_tokens === "number" ? config.max_tokens : null
    const thinkingLevel = config.thinking_enabled === true && typeof config.thinking_level === "string"
      ? config.thinking_level
      : "none"
    return [{
      nodeInstanceId,
      displayName: typeof node.display_name === "string" && node.display_name.trim()
        ? node.display_name
        : nodeKindLabels[kind] ?? `流程步骤 ${nodeInstanceId}`,
      kind,
      kindLabel: nodeKindLabels[kind] ?? "工作流步骤",
      componentVersion: rawVersion == null ? null : String(rawVersion),
      moduleTypeKey,
      moduleTypeLabel: moduleTypeKey ? ruleModuleLabels[moduleTypeKey] ?? "规则模块" : null,
      modelName: typeof model.name === "string"
        ? model.name
        : typeof model.model_id === "string"
          ? model.model_id
          : null,
      maxTokens,
      thinkingLabel: thinkingLevel ? thinkingLabels[thinkingLevel] ?? thinkingLevel : null,
    }]
  })
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/short-circuit-editor.tsx =====
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { Plus, Trash, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import {
  newShortCircuitLeafCondition,
  newShortCircuitRule,
  parseShortCircuitValue,
  shortCircuitValueText,
  workflowShortCircuitRules,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/short-circuit-model"
import type {
  WorkflowLevelOption,
  WorkflowShortCircuitCondition,
  WorkflowShortCircuitLeafCondition,
  WorkflowShortCircuitOperator,
  WorkflowShortCircuitRule,
  WorkflowV3Node,
} from "@/features/workflow-manager/types"

type Props = {
  node: WorkflowV3Node
  levels: WorkflowLevelOption[]
  onPatch: (patch: Record<string, unknown>) => void
}

const operatorLabels: Record<WorkflowShortCircuitOperator, string> = {
  eq: "等于",
  in: "属于其中之一",
  exists: "字段存在",
  gte: "大于或等于",
  lte: "小于或等于",
}

export function ShortCircuitEditor({ node, levels, onPatch }: Props) {
  const rules = workflowShortCircuitRules(node.config.short_circuit_rules)

  function save(next: WorkflowShortCircuitRule[]) {
    onPatch({ short_circuit_rules: next.length ? next : undefined })
  }

  function update(index: number, patch: Partial<WorkflowShortCircuitRule>) {
    save(rules.map((rule, ruleIndex) => (
      ruleIndex === index ? { ...rule, ...patch } : rule
    )))
  }

  function addRule() {
    const used = new Set(rules.map((rule) => rule.rule_key))
    let index = rules.length
    while (used.has(`short_circuit_${index + 1}`)) index += 1
    save([...rules, newShortCircuitRule(levels, index)])
  }

  return (
    <section className="@container/short-circuit border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-5" data-testid="short-circuit-editor">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 basis-40">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold">命中后直接确定等级</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            可选择品类当前启用的任意等级，并只跳过指定运行角色；写入下游和审计仍会继续。
          </p>
        </div>
        <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={!levels.length} onClick={addRule}>
          <Plus />添加
        </Button>
      </div>

      {!levels.length && (
        <div className="mt-3 flex gap-2 rounded-md border border-alias-edge-032 bg-alias-surface-071 px-3 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033">
          <WarningCircle className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          <p>正在读取品类等级，读取完成前不能新增短路规则。</p>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="mt-3 space-y-3">
        {rules.map((rule, index) => (
          <article key={`${rule.rule_key}-${index}`} className="border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-3 py-3 rounded-md">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold">短路规则 {index + 1}</p>
              <button
                type="button"
                className="visual-native-button size-11 shrink-0 p-0 text-[var(--muted)] hover:text-foreground"
                aria-label={`删除短路规则 ${index + 1}`}
                onClick={() => save(rules.filter((_, ruleIndex) => ruleIndex !== index))}
              >
                <Trash size={15} />
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 @[28rem]/short-circuit:grid-cols-2">
              <EditorField label="规则标识">
                <Input
                  className="text-xs"
                  value={rule.rule_key}
                  onChange={(event) => update(index, { rule_key: event.target.value.trim() })}
                />
              </EditorField>
              <EditorField label="优先级（越大越先）">
                <Input
                  className="text-xs"
                  type="number"
                  value={rule.priority}
                  onChange={(event) => update(index, { priority: Number(event.target.value) })}
                />
              </EditorField>
              <EditorField label="直接确定为">
                <select
                  className="visual-native-select w-full"
                  value={rule.target_level}
                  onChange={(event) => update(index, { target_level: event.target.value })}
                >
                  {levels.map((level) => (
                    <option key={level.level} value={level.level}>
                      {level.level} · {level.displayName}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </EditorField>
            </div>
            <ShortCircuitConditionEditor
              condition={rule.when}
              onChange={(when) => update(index, { when })}
            />
            <EditorField label="短路时跳过哪些运行角色（逗号分开）">
              <Input
                className="mt-3 text-xs"
                placeholder="grading，level_matching"
                value={rule.skip_roles.join("，")}
                onChange={(event) => update(index, {
                  skip_roles: event.target.value.split(/[，,]/).map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean),
                })}
              />
            </EditorField>
            <EditorField label="显式跳过哪些下游节点（节点标识，逗号分开）">
              <Input
                className="mt-3 text-xs"
                placeholder="prompt_b，level_matcher"
                value={rule.skip_nodes.join("，")}
                onChange={(event) => update(index, {
                  skip_nodes: event.target.value
                    .split(/[，,]/)
                    .map((item) => item.trim())
                    .filter(Boolean),
                })}
              />
            </EditorField>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>

      <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033">
        同优先级规则若命中不同等级，运行会停止并转人工复核；请用优先级明确裁决顺序。
      </p>
    </section>
  )
}

type ConditionMode = "leaf" | "all" | "any"

function conditionMode(condition: WorkflowShortCircuitCondition): ConditionMode {
  if ("all" in condition) return "all"
  if ("any" in condition) return "any"
  return "leaf"
}

function conditionChildren(
  condition: WorkflowShortCircuitCondition,
): WorkflowShortCircuitCondition[] {
  if ("all" in condition) return condition.all
  if ("any" in condition) return condition.any
  return []
}

function groupedCondition(
  mode: Exclude<ConditionMode, "leaf">,
  children: WorkflowShortCircuitCondition[],
): WorkflowShortCircuitCondition {
  return mode === "all" ? { all: children } : { any: children }
}

function firstLeaf(
  condition: WorkflowShortCircuitCondition,
): WorkflowShortCircuitLeafCondition {
  if ("path" in condition) return condition
  return firstLeaf(conditionChildren(condition)[0] ?? newShortCircuitLeafCondition())
}

function ShortCircuitConditionEditor({
  condition,
  onChange,
  depth = 0,
}: {
  condition: WorkflowShortCircuitCondition
  onChange: (condition: WorkflowShortCircuitCondition) => void
  depth?: number
}) {
  const mode = conditionMode(condition)
  const children = conditionChildren(condition)

  function changeMode(next: ConditionMode) {
    if (next === mode) return
    if (next === "leaf") {
      onChange(firstLeaf(condition))
      return
    }
    onChange(groupedCondition(next, mode === "leaf" ? [condition] : children))
  }

  return (
    <div className={`${depth ? "mt-2" : "mt-3"} @container/condition min-w-0 border rounded-md border-[var(--line)] bg-white p-3`}>
      <EditorField label={depth ? `子条件 ${depth}` : "条件结构"}>
        <select
          className="visual-native-select w-full"
          value={mode}
          onChange={(event) => changeMode(event.target.value as ConditionMode)}
        >
          <option value="leaf">单一条件</option>
          <option value="all">满足全部条件</option>
          <option value="any">满足任一条件</option>
        </select>
      </EditorField>

      {mode === "leaf" && "path" in condition ? (
        <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 @[36rem]/condition:grid-cols-3">
          <EditorField label="读取结果字段">
            <Input
              className="text-xs"
              placeholder="例如 output.redline"
              value={condition.path}
              onChange={(event) => onChange({
                ...condition,
                path: event.target.value.trim(),
              })}
            />
          </EditorField>
          <EditorField label="匹配方式">
            <select
              className="visual-native-select w-full"
              value={condition.op}
              onChange={(event) => {
                const op = event.target.value as WorkflowShortCircuitOperator
                onChange({
                  ...condition,
                  op,
                  value: op === "exists" ? true : condition.value,
                })
              }}
            >
              {Object.entries(operatorLabels).map(([value, label]) => (
                <option key={value} value={value}>{label}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </EditorField>
          <EditorField label="匹配值">
            <Input
              className="text-xs"
              disabled={condition.op === "exists"}
              placeholder={condition.op === "in" ? "多个值用逗号分开" : "例如 true、严重、80"}
              value={shortCircuitValueText(condition.value)}
              onChange={(event) => onChange({
                ...condition,
                value: parseShortCircuitValue(event.target.value, condition.op),
              })}
            />
          </EditorField>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">
          <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            {mode === "all" ? "满足全部条件后短路" : "满足任一条件后短路"}
          </p>
          {children.map((child, childIndex) => (
            <div key={childIndex} className="relative border-l-2 border-[var(--line-strong)] pl-3 pt-11">
              <ShortCircuitConditionEditor
                condition={child}
                depth={depth + 1}
                onChange={(nextChild) => onChange(groupedCondition(
                  mode as Exclude<ConditionMode, "leaf">,
                  children.map((item, index) => index === childIndex ? nextChild : item),
                ))}
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                className="visual-native-button absolute right-2 top-0 size-11 p-0 text-[var(--muted)] hover:text-foreground disabled:opacity-30"
                aria-label={`删除子条件 ${childIndex + 1}`}
                disabled={children.length <= 1}
                onClick={() => onChange(groupedCondition(
                  mode as Exclude<ConditionMode, "leaf">,
                  children.filter((_, index) => index !== childIndex),
                ))}
              >
                <Trash size={14} />
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant="secondary"
            onClick={() => onChange(groupedCondition(
              mode as Exclude<ConditionMode, "leaf">,
              [...children, newShortCircuitLeafCondition()],
            ))}
          >
            <Plus />添加子条件
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function EditorField({
  label,
  children,
}: {
  label: string
  children: ReactNode
}) {
  return (
    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">
      <span className="mb-1 block">{label}</span>
      {children}
    </label>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/short-circuit-model.ts =====
import type {
  ResolvedWorkflowLevelScale,
  WorkflowShortCircuitCondition,
  WorkflowShortCircuitLeafCondition,
  WorkflowLevelOption,
  WorkflowShortCircuitOperator,
  WorkflowShortCircuitRule,
} from "./types.ts"

export function workflowLevelOptionsFromScale(
  scale: ResolvedWorkflowLevelScale | null | undefined,
): WorkflowLevelOption[] {
  if (!scale || !Array.isArray(scale.enabled_levels)) return []
  const names = scale.display_names ?? {}
  return scale.enabled_levels
    .filter((level): level is string => typeof level === "string" && Boolean(level.trim()))
    .map((level) => ({
      level,
      displayName: typeof names[level] === "string" && names[level].trim()
        ? names[level]
        : level,
    }))
}

export function newShortCircuitRule(
  levels: readonly WorkflowLevelOption[],
  index: number,
): WorkflowShortCircuitRule {
  return {
    rule_version: "short-circuit-rule-v1",
    rule_key: `short_circuit_${index + 1}`,
    when: { path: "output.field", op: "eq", value: true },
    target_level: levels[0]?.level ?? "",
    priority: 50,
    skip_roles: ["grading", "level_matching"],
    skip_nodes: [],
  }
}

export function newShortCircuitLeafCondition(): WorkflowShortCircuitLeafCondition {
  return { path: "output.field", op: "eq", value: true }
}

export function workflowShortCircuitRules(value: unknown): WorkflowShortCircuitRule[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return []
  return value.flatMap((candidate) => {
    if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return []
    const rule = candidate as Partial<WorkflowShortCircuitRule>
    const when = shortCircuitCondition(rule.when)
    if (
      rule.rule_version !== "short-circuit-rule-v1"
      || typeof rule.rule_key !== "string"
      || typeof rule.target_level !== "string"
      || typeof rule.priority !== "number"
      || !Array.isArray(rule.skip_roles)
      || !Array.isArray(rule.skip_nodes ?? [])
      || !when
    ) return []
    return [{
      rule_version: "short-circuit-rule-v1" as const,
      rule_key: rule.rule_key,
      when,
      target_level: rule.target_level,
      priority: rule.priority,
      skip_roles: rule.skip_roles.filter((role): role is string => typeof role === "string"),
      skip_nodes: (rule.skip_nodes ?? []).filter(
        (node): node is string => typeof node === "string",
      ),
    }]
  })
}

function shortCircuitCondition(value: unknown): WorkflowShortCircuitCondition | null {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return null
  const candidate = value as Record<string, unknown>
  const groupModes = (["all", "any"] as const).filter((mode) => mode in candidate)
  if (groupModes.length) {
    if (groupModes.length !== 1 || Object.keys(candidate).length !== 1) return null
    const mode = groupModes[0]
    const rawChildren = candidate[mode]
    if (!Array.isArray(rawChildren) || !rawChildren.length) return null
    const children = rawChildren.map(shortCircuitCondition)
    if (children.some((child) => child === null)) return null
    const normalized = children as WorkflowShortCircuitCondition[]
    return mode === "all" ? { all: normalized } : { any: normalized }
  }
  if (
    Object.keys(candidate).some((key) => !["path", "op", "value"].includes(key))
    || typeof candidate.path !== "string"
    || !candidate.path.trim()
    || !isOperator(candidate.op)
  ) return null
  return {
    path: candidate.path,
    op: candidate.op,
    value: candidate.value,
  }
}

export function parseShortCircuitValue(
  text: string,
  operator: WorkflowShortCircuitOperator,
): unknown {
  if (operator === "exists") return true
  if (operator === "in") {
    return text.split(/[，,]/).map((item) => scalar(item.trim())).filter((item) => item !== "")
  }
  return scalar(text.trim())
}

export function shortCircuitValueText(value: unknown): string {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.join("，") : String(value ?? "")
}

function scalar(value: string): string | number | boolean {
  if (value === "true" || value === "是") return true
  if (value === "false" || value === "否") return false
  if (value !== "" && Number.isFinite(Number(value))) return Number(value)
  return value
}

function isOperator(value: unknown): value is WorkflowShortCircuitOperator {
  return value === "eq" || value === "in" || value === "exists" || value === "gte" || value === "lte"
}

  lane_key: string

  edges: WorkflowEdge[]
  queue_class: string
  input_schema: Record<string, unknown>
  output_schema: Record<string, unknown>
  resource_policy: Record<string, unknown>
  layout?: Record<string, { x: number; y: number }>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/vision-input-policy-editor.tsx =====
import { useMemo } from "react"

import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import type {
  VisionCapabilityProfile,
  VisionDetailValue,
  VisionFidelity,
  VisionInputPolicy,
  VisionPreprocess,
  VisionRolePolicy,
} from "@/lib/types"

import {
  estimateImageTokens,
  normalizeVisionInputPolicy,
  operationalRequestBodyBytes,
  operationalSingleImageBytes,
  visionPolicySummary,
} from "./vision-input-policy-model"

type Props = {
  value: unknown
  capability: VisionCapabilityProfile | null
  historical: boolean
  onChange: (policy: VisionInputPolicy) => void
}

export function VisionInputPolicyEditor({ value, capability, historical, onChange }: Props) {
  const resolved = useMemo(() => {
    if (!capability) return { policy: null, error: "当前视觉模型没有可用的供应商图片能力档案。" }
    try {
      return { policy: normalizeVisionInputPolicy(value, capability, { historical }), error: "" }
    } catch (error) {
      return { policy: null, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "图片输入策略无法读取" }
    }
  }, [capability, historical, value])

  if (!capability) {
    return <Notice tone="danger">当前模型没有完整的供应商图片能力档案，不能配置图片精度；请先到模型注册中心补齐。</Notice>
  }
  if (!resolved.policy) {
    return <Notice tone="danger">图片输入策略不合法：{resolved.error}</Notice>
  }

  const policy = resolved.policy
  const exampleTotalTokens = estimateImageTokens(capability, policy.roles.target, 2560, 1440)
    + estimateImageTokens(capability, policy.roles.anchor, 2560, 1440)
  const updateRole = (role: "target" | "anchor", next: VisionRolePolicy) => {
    if (historical || policy.version === "legacy-default") return
    onChange({
      ...policy,
      roles: { ...policy.roles, [role]: next },
      transport: { mode: "lossless", size_limit: "provider_profile" },
    })
  }

  return (
    <section className="@container/vision min-w-0 space-y-4 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-4" aria-label="图片输入策略">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h4 className="text-sm font-bold">图片精度与无损传输</h4>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">目标图和锚点图分别配置；画布仅显示“{visionPolicySummary(policy)}”。</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">按目标图、锚点图各一张 2560×1440 估算：合计 {exampleTotalTokens.toLocaleString()} 图片 Token。</p>
        </div>
        <span className="rounded-md border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold">{visionPolicySummary(policy)}</span>
      </div>

      {(historical || policy.version === "legacy-default") && (
        <Notice tone="warning">历史兼容策略（只读）：继续按冻结版本执行，不会被新默认值改写。</Notice>
      )}

      <div className="grid gap-4 @[30rem]/vision:grid-cols-2">
        <VisionRoleEditor
          label="目标图"
          role="target"
          value={policy.roles.target}
          capability={capability}
          disabled={historical || policy.version === "legacy-default"}
          onChange={(next) => updateRole("target", next)}
        />
        <VisionRoleEditor
          label="锚点图"
          role="anchor"
          value={policy.roles.anchor}
          capability={capability}
          disabled={historical || policy.version === "legacy-default"}
          onChange={(next) => updateRole("anchor", next)}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-3 text-xs leading-5 @[30rem]/vision:grid-cols-2">
        <div className="rounded-md border border-line bg-white px-3 py-3">
          <p className="font-semibold">传输固定为无损优化</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">只移除冗余元数据并做无损编码，不改变像素和视觉内容。单图运行门限 {formatBytes(operationalSingleImageBytes(capability))}，请求体运行门限 {formatBytes(operationalRequestBodyBytes(capability))}。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-md border border-alias-edge-032 bg-alias-surface-071 px-3 py-3">
          <p className="font-semibold">超限会在计费前失败</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">无损优化后仍超限将阻止调用，并保留安全错误证据；不会自动有损压缩或悄悄降级。</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <details className="visual-native-details border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
        <summary className="font-semibold text-[var(--ink)]">查看供应商能力边界</summary>
        <div className="mt-2 grid gap-2 break-words @[30rem]/vision:grid-cols-2">
          <p>档案：{capability.profile_key}</p>
          <p>文档观测日期：{capability.observed_revision_date}</p>
          <p>尺寸：至少 {capability.min_dimension_pixels}px；总像素 {capability.min_source_pixels.toLocaleString()}～{capability.max_source_pixels.toLocaleString()}</p>
          <p>格式：{capability.allowed_mime_types.join("、")}</p>
          <p>宽高比：{capability.min_aspect_ratio.toFixed(4)}～{capability.max_aspect_ratio}</p>
          <p>最多 {capability.max_frames} 帧</p>
        </div>
        <a className="mt-2 inline-block font-semibold underline" href={capability.source_document_url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">查看官方图片 Token 规则</a>
      </details>
    </section>
  )
}

function VisionRoleEditor({
  label,
  role,
  value,
  capability,
  disabled,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string
  role: "target" | "anchor"
  value: VisionRolePolicy
  capability: VisionCapabilityProfile
  disabled: boolean
  onChange: (value: VisionRolePolicy) => void
}) {
  const estimatedTokens = estimateImageTokens(capability, value, 2560, 1440)
  const fidelity = value.fidelity
  const preprocess = value.preprocess

  const changeFidelityMode = (mode: "detail" | "pixel_limit") => {
    const next: VisionFidelity = mode === "detail"
      ? { mode: "detail", value: capability.detail_modes.includes("high") ? "high" : capability.detail_modes[0] }
      : { mode: "pixel_limit", min_pixels: capability.min_source_pixels, max_pixels: Math.min(2_257_920, capability.max_source_pixels) }
    onChange({ ...value, fidelity: next })
  }
  const changePreprocessMode = (mode: VisionPreprocess["mode"]) => {
    const customEdge = Math.max(
      capability.min_dimension_pixels,
      Math.min(2048, Math.floor(Math.sqrt(capability.max_source_pixels))),
    )
    const next: VisionPreprocess = mode === "original"
      ? { mode: "original" }
      : mode === "short_edge"
        ? { mode: "short_edge", pixels: Math.max(capability.min_dimension_pixels, 600) }
        : { mode: "custom", max_width: customEdge, max_height: customEdge, allow_upscale: false }
    onChange({ ...value, preprocess: next })
  }

  return (
    <fieldset className="@container/vision-role min-w-0 space-y-3 border rounded-md border-[var(--line)] bg-white p-3" disabled={disabled} data-media-role={role}>
      <legend className="px-1 text-sm font-bold">{label}</legend>
      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold">
        图片理解精度
        <select className="visual-native-select mt-1 w-full" value={fidelity.mode} onChange={(event) => changeFidelityMode(event.target.value as "detail" | "pixel_limit")}>
          <option value="detail">detail（low / high / xhigh）</option>
          {capability.pixel_limit_supported && <option value="pixel_limit">image_pixel_limit（像素范围）</option>}
        </select>
      </label>
      {fidelity.mode === "detail" ? (
        <label className="block text-xs font-semibold">
          detail 值
          <select className="visual-native-select mt-1 w-full" value={fidelity.value} onChange={(event) => onChange({ ...value, fidelity: { mode: "detail", value: event.target.value as VisionDetailValue } })}>
            {capability.detail_modes.map((detail) => <option key={detail} value={detail}>{detail}</option>)}
          </select>
        </label>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-2 @[24rem]/vision-role:grid-cols-2">
          <NumericField label="最小像素" value={fidelity.min_pixels} min={capability.min_source_pixels} max={fidelity.max_pixels} onChange={(next) => onChange({ ...value, fidelity: { ...fidelity, min_pixels: next } })} />
          <NumericField label="最大像素" value={fidelity.max_pixels} min={fidelity.min_pixels} max={capability.max_source_pixels} onChange={(next) => onChange({ ...value, fidelity: { ...fidelity, max_pixels: next } })} />
        </div>
      )}

      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold">
        本地预处理
        <select className="visual-native-select mt-1 w-full" value={preprocess.mode} onChange={(event) => changePreprocessMode(event.target.value as VisionPreprocess["mode"])}>
          <option value="original">原始像素（仅无损优化）</option>
          <option value="short_edge">按短边缩放</option>
          <option value="custom">自定义最大宽高</option>
        </select>
      </label>
      {preprocess.mode === "short_edge" && (
        <NumericField label="短边像素" value={preprocess.pixels} min={capability.min_dimension_pixels} max={Math.floor(Math.sqrt(capability.max_source_pixels))} onChange={(next) => onChange({ ...value, preprocess: { mode: "short_edge", pixels: next } })} />
      )}
      {preprocess.mode === "custom" && (
        <div className="grid gap-2 @[24rem]/vision-role:grid-cols-2">
          <NumericField label="最大宽度" value={preprocess.max_width} min={capability.min_dimension_pixels} max={capability.max_source_pixels} onChange={(next) => onChange({ ...value, preprocess: { ...preprocess, max_width: next } })} />
          <NumericField label="最大高度" value={preprocess.max_height} min={capability.min_dimension_pixels} max={capability.max_source_pixels} onChange={(next) => onChange({ ...value, preprocess: { ...preprocess, max_height: next } })} />
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="rounded-md bg-surface-sunken px-3 py-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
        <p className="font-semibold text-[var(--ink)]">官方估算：2560×1440 示例约 {estimatedTokens.toLocaleString()} 图片 Token</p>
        {fidelity.mode === "detail" && fidelity.value === "high" && <p>high 固定 1280 图片 Token；本地缩图通常不省 Token，但可能损失文字和细节。</p>}
        {fidelity.mode === "detail" && fidelity.value === "low" && <p>low 适合整体分类与画面大意，不适合小字、密集表格、微小目标和细粒度差异。</p>}
        {fidelity.mode === "detail" && fidelity.value === "xhigh" && <p>xhigh 面向更复杂图片，预计成本可能高于 high。</p>}
        {fidelity.mode === "pixel_limit" && <p>image_pixel_limit 与 detail 互斥；这里按供应商公式估算，不代表实际账单。</p>}
      </div>
    </fieldset>
  )
}

function NumericField({ label, value, min, max, onChange }: { label: string; value: number; min: number; max: number; onChange: (value: number) => void }) {
  return (
    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold">
      {label}
      <Input className="mt-1" type="number" value={value} min={min} max={max} onChange={(event) => {
        const parsed = Number(event.target.value)
        if (Number.isInteger(parsed)) onChange(parsed)
      }} />
    </label>
  )
}

function Notice({ tone, children }: { tone: "warning" | "danger"; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return <div className={`rounded-md border px-3 py-3 text-xs leading-5 ${tone === "danger" ? "border-alias-edge-020 bg-alias-surface-064 text-alias-content-034" : "border-alias-edge-032 bg-alias-surface-071 text-alias-content-033"}`}>{children}</div>
}

function formatBytes(value: number) {
  return value >= 1_000_000 ? `${(value / 1_000_000).toFixed(2)} MB` : `${Math.round(value / 1000)} KB`
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/workflow-manager/vision-input-policy-model.ts =====
import type {
  VisionCapabilityProfile,
  VisionDetailValue,
  VisionFidelity,
  VisionInputPolicy,
  VisionPreprocess,
  VisionRolePolicy,
} from "@/lib/types"

const DETAIL_VALUES = new Set<VisionDetailValue>(["low", "high", "xhigh"])
const CAPABILITY_KEYS = [
  "version",
  "profile_key",
  "detail_modes",
  "pixel_limit_supported",
  "min_source_pixels",
  "max_source_pixels",
  "min_aspect_ratio",
  "max_aspect_ratio",
  "min_dimension_pixels",
  "allowed_mime_types",
  "max_frames",
  "max_single_image_bytes",
  "single_image_safety_margin_bytes",
  "max_request_body_bytes",
  "request_body_safety_margin_bytes",
  "token_formula",
  "source_document_url",
  "observed_revision_date",
] as const

const SEED_20_PROFILE: VisionCapabilityProfile = {
  version: "vision-capability-profile-v1",
  profile_key: "volcengine-seed-2.0-v20260901",
  detail_modes: ["low", "high", "xhigh"],
  pixel_limit_supported: true,
  min_source_pixels: 196,
  max_source_pixels: 36_000_000,
  min_aspect_ratio: 1 / 150,
  max_aspect_ratio: 150,
  min_dimension_pixels: 15,
  allowed_mime_types: ["image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp"],
  max_frames: 1,
  max_single_image_bytes: 10_000_000,
  single_image_safety_margin_bytes: 262_144,
  max_request_body_bytes: 64_000_000,
  request_body_safety_margin_bytes: 1_048_576,
  token_formula: "seed-2.0-v1",
  source_document_url: "https://docs.volcengine.com/docs/82379/1362931?lang=zh",
  observed_revision_date: "2026-09-01",
}

export function seed20VisionCapabilityProfile(): VisionCapabilityProfile {
  return {
    ...SEED_20_PROFILE,
    detail_modes: [...SEED_20_PROFILE.detail_modes],
    allowed_mime_types: [...SEED_20_PROFILE.allowed_mime_types],
  }
}

export function normalizeVisionCapabilityProfile(value: unknown): VisionCapabilityProfile {
  const source = objectValue(value, "供应商图片能力档案必须是对象")
  exactKeys(source, CAPABILITY_KEYS, "供应商图片能力档案")
  if (source.version !== "vision-capability-profile-v1") throw new Error("图片能力档案版本不受支持")
  const profileKey = textValue(source.profile_key, "profile_key 不能为空")
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}$/.test(profileKey)) throw new Error("profile_key 格式不合法")
  if (!Array.isArray(source.detail_modes) || !source.detail_modes.length) throw new Error("detail_modes 不能为空")
  const detailModes = source.detail_modes.map((value) => {
    if (!DETAIL_VALUES.has(value as VisionDetailValue)) throw new Error("detail_modes 只支持 low、high、xhigh")
    return value as VisionDetailValue
  })
  if (new Set(detailModes).size !== detailModes.length) throw new Error("detail_modes 不能重复")
  if (typeof source.pixel_limit_supported !== "boolean") throw new Error("pixel_limit_supported 必须是布尔值")
  const minSourcePixels = integerValue(source.min_source_pixels, "min_source_pixels 必须是整数")
  const maxSourcePixels = integerValue(source.max_source_pixels, "max_source_pixels 必须是整数")
  const minDimensionPixels = integerValue(source.min_dimension_pixels, "min_dimension_pixels 必须是整数")
  const maxFrames = integerValue(source.max_frames, "max_frames 必须是整数")
  if (minSourcePixels < 196 || maxSourcePixels > 36_000_000 || minSourcePixels > maxSourcePixels) throw new Error("图片像素边界不合法")
  if (minDimensionPixels < 15 || minDimensionPixels ** 2 < minSourcePixels || maxFrames < 1) throw new Error("图片尺寸或帧数边界不合法")
  const minAspectRatio = numberValue(source.min_aspect_ratio, "min_aspect_ratio 必须是数字")
  const maxAspectRatio = numberValue(source.max_aspect_ratio, "max_aspect_ratio 必须是数字")
  if (minAspectRatio <= 0 || minAspectRatio > maxAspectRatio || maxAspectRatio > 150) throw new Error("宽高比边界不合法")
  if (!Array.isArray(source.allowed_mime_types) || !source.allowed_mime_types.length) throw new Error("allowed_mime_types 不能为空")
  const allowedMimeTypes = source.allowed_mime_types.map((value) => {
    const mime = textValue(value, "MIME 类型不能为空")
    if (!mime.startsWith("image/") || mime !== mime.trim().toLowerCase()) throw new Error("MIME 类型必须规范化为 image/*")
    return mime
  })
  if (new Set(allowedMimeTypes).size !== allowedMimeTypes.length) throw new Error("MIME 类型不能重复")
  const maxSingleImageBytes = integerValue(source.max_single_image_bytes, "max_single_image_bytes 必须是整数")
  const singleImageSafetyMarginBytes = integerValue(source.single_image_safety_margin_bytes, "single_image_safety_margin_bytes 必须是整数")
  const maxRequestBodyBytes = integerValue(source.max_request_body_bytes, "max_request_body_bytes 必须是整数")
  const requestBodySafetyMarginBytes = integerValue(source.request_body_safety_margin_bytes, "request_body_safety_margin_bytes 必须是整数")
  if (maxSingleImageBytes <= singleImageSafetyMarginBytes || singleImageSafetyMarginBytes < 0) throw new Error("单图上限必须大于安全余量")
  if (maxRequestBodyBytes <= requestBodySafetyMarginBytes || requestBodySafetyMarginBytes < 0) throw new Error("请求体上限必须大于安全余量")
  const tokenFormula = textValue(source.token_formula, "token_formula 不能为空")
  const sourceDocumentUrl = textValue(source.source_document_url, "能力来源不能为空")
  if (!sourceDocumentUrl.startsWith("https://")) throw new Error("能力来源必须是 HTTPS 文档")
  const observedRevisionDate = textValue(source.observed_revision_date, "观测日期不能为空")
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(observedRevisionDate)) throw new Error("观测日期必须是 YYYY-MM-DD")
  return {
    version: "vision-capability-profile-v1",
    profile_key: profileKey,
    detail_modes: detailModes,
    pixel_limit_supported: source.pixel_limit_supported,
    min_source_pixels: minSourcePixels,
    max_source_pixels: maxSourcePixels,
    min_aspect_ratio: minAspectRatio,
    max_aspect_ratio: maxAspectRatio,
    min_dimension_pixels: minDimensionPixels,
    allowed_mime_types: allowedMimeTypes,
    max_frames: maxFrames,
    max_single_image_bytes: maxSingleImageBytes,
    single_image_safety_margin_bytes: singleImageSafetyMarginBytes,
    max_request_body_bytes: maxRequestBodyBytes,
    request_body_safety_margin_bytes: requestBodySafetyMarginBytes,
    token_formula: tokenFormula,
    source_document_url: sourceDocumentUrl,
    observed_revision_date: observedRevisionDate,
  }
}

export function isVisionCapabilityProfile(value: unknown): value is VisionCapabilityProfile {
  try {
    normalizeVisionCapabilityProfile(value)
    return true
  } catch {
    return false
  }
}

export function resolveVisionCapabilityProfile(
  value: unknown,
  provider: string,
  modelId: string,
): VisionCapabilityProfile | null {
  if (isVisionCapabilityProfile(value)) return normalizeVisionCapabilityProfile(value)
  const normalizedProvider = provider.trim().toLowerCase().replaceAll("_", "-")
  const normalizedModel = modelId.trim().toLowerCase().replaceAll("_", "-").replaceAll(".", "-")
  if (["doubao", "volcengine", "volcengine-ark", "ark"].includes(normalizedProvider) && normalizedModel.includes("doubao-seed-2-0")) {
    return seed20VisionCapabilityProfile()
  }
  return null
}

export function defaultVisionInputPolicy(capability: VisionCapabilityProfile): VisionInputPolicy {
  return {
    version: "vision-input-policy-v1",
    provider_profile: capability.profile_key,
    roles: {
      target: { fidelity: { mode: "detail", value: "high" }, preprocess: { mode: "original" } },
      anchor: { fidelity: { mode: "detail", value: "high" }, preprocess: { mode: "original" } },
    },
    transport: { mode: "lossless", size_limit: "provider_profile" },
  }
}

export function normalizeVisionInputPolicy(
  value: unknown,
  capability: VisionCapabilityProfile,
  options: { historical: boolean },
): VisionInputPolicy {
  const normalizedCapability = normalizeVisionCapabilityProfile(capability)
  if (value === undefined || value === null) {
    if (options.historical) return legacyPolicy(normalizedCapability)
    return defaultVisionInputPolicy(normalizedCapability)
  }
  const source = objectValue(value, "图片输入策略必须是对象")
  if (source.version === "legacy-default") {
    if (!options.historical) throw new Error("历史兼容策略只能只读展示")
    exactKeys(source, ["version", "provider_profile", "roles"], "历史兼容策略")
    if (source.provider_profile !== normalizedCapability.profile_key) throw new Error("图片策略与模型能力档案不一致")
    const roles = parseRoles(source.roles, normalizedCapability)
    return { version: "legacy-default", provider_profile: normalizedCapability.profile_key, roles }
  }
  exactKeys(source, ["version", "provider_profile", "roles", "transport"], "图片输入策略")
  if (source.version !== "vision-input-policy-v1") throw new Error("图片输入策略版本不受支持")
  if (source.provider_profile !== normalizedCapability.profile_key) throw new Error("图片策略与模型能力档案不一致")
  const transport = objectValue(source.transport, "transport 必须是对象")
  exactKeys(transport, ["mode", "size_limit"], "transport")
  if (transport.mode !== "lossless" || transport.size_limit !== "provider_profile") throw new Error("图片传输必须锁定 lossless/provider_profile")
  return {
    version: "vision-input-policy-v1",
    provider_profile: normalizedCapability.profile_key,
    roles: parseRoles(source.roles, normalizedCapability),
    transport: { mode: "lossless", size_limit: "provider_profile" },
  }
}

export function estimateImageTokens(
  capability: VisionCapabilityProfile,
  rolePolicy: VisionRolePolicy,
  width: number,
  height: number,
): number {
  if (!Number.isInteger(width) || !Number.isInteger(height) || width <= 0 || height <= 0) throw new Error("Token 估算宽高必须为正整数")
  if (capability.token_formula !== "seed-2.0-v1") throw new Error("当前前端不支持该图片 Token 公式")
  const fidelity = rolePolicy.fidelity
  if (fidelity.mode === "detail" && fidelity.value === "high") return 1280
  const pixels = width * height
  const effectivePixels = fidelity.mode === "pixel_limit"
    ? Math.min(Math.max(pixels, fidelity.min_pixels), fidelity.max_pixels)
    : fidelity.value === "low"
      ? Math.min(pixels, 2_257_920)
      : Math.min(Math.max(pixels, 2_257_920), 9_031_680)
  let estimate = Math.ceil(effectivePixels / 1764)
  if (fidelity.mode === "detail" && fidelity.value === "xhigh") estimate = Math.max(1280, estimate)
  const maximum = fidelity.mode === "detail" && fidelity.value !== "xhigh" ? 1280 : 5120
  return Math.max(1, Math.min(estimate, maximum))
}

export function visionPolicySummary(policy: VisionInputPolicy): string {
  if (policy.version === "legacy-default") return "历史兼容策略（只读）"
  return `目标 ${fidelityLabel(policy.roles.target.fidelity)} / 锚点 ${fidelityLabel(policy.roles.anchor.fidelity)}`
}

export function operationalSingleImageBytes(capability: VisionCapabilityProfile): number {
  return capability.max_single_image_bytes - capability.single_image_safety_margin_bytes
}

export function operationalRequestBodyBytes(capability: VisionCapabilityProfile): number {
  return capability.max_request_body_bytes - capability.request_body_safety_margin_bytes
}

function fidelityLabel(fidelity: VisionFidelity): string {
  return fidelity.mode === "detail" ? fidelity.value : "image_pixel_limit"
}

function legacyPolicy(capability: VisionCapabilityProfile): VisionInputPolicy {
  return {
    version: "legacy-default",
    provider_profile: capability.profile_key,
    roles: {
      target: { fidelity: { mode: "detail", value: "high" }, preprocess: { mode: "short_edge", pixels: 600 } },
      anchor: { fidelity: { mode: "detail", value: "high" }, preprocess: { mode: "short_edge", pixels: 600 } },
    },
  }
}

function parseRoles(value: unknown, capability: VisionCapabilityProfile): VisionInputPolicy["roles"] {
  const roles = objectValue(value, "roles 必须是对象")
  exactKeys(roles, ["target", "anchor"], "roles")
  return {
    target: parseRole(roles.target, capability, "target"),
    anchor: parseRole(roles.anchor, capability, "anchor"),
  }
}

function parseRole(value: unknown, capability: VisionCapabilityProfile, role: "target" | "anchor"): VisionRolePolicy {
  const source = objectValue(value, `${role} 策略必须是对象`)
  exactKeys(source, ["fidelity", "preprocess"], `${role} 策略`)
  return {
    fidelity: parseFidelity(source.fidelity, capability),
    preprocess: parsePreprocess(source.preprocess, capability),
  }
}

function parseFidelity(value: unknown, capability: VisionCapabilityProfile): VisionFidelity {
  const source = objectValue(value, "图片精度必须是对象")
  if (source.mode === "detail") {
    if ("min_pixels" in source || "max_pixels" in source) throw new Error("不能同时配置 detail 和 image_pixel_limit")
    exactKeys(source, ["mode", "value"], "detail")
    if (!DETAIL_VALUES.has(source.value as VisionDetailValue)) throw new Error("detail 只支持 low、high 或 xhigh")
    const detail = source.value as VisionDetailValue
    if (!capability.detail_modes.includes(detail)) throw new Error(`${detail} 不在模型能力档案中`)
    return { mode: "detail", value: detail }
  }
  if (source.mode === "pixel_limit") {
    if ("value" in source) throw new Error("不能同时配置 detail 和 image_pixel_limit")
    exactKeys(source, ["mode", "min_pixels", "max_pixels"], "image_pixel_limit")
    if (!capability.pixel_limit_supported) throw new Error("所选模型不支持 image_pixel_limit")
    const minimum = integerValue(source.min_pixels, "min_pixels 必须是整数")
    const maximum = integerValue(source.max_pixels, "max_pixels 必须是整数")
    if (minimum < capability.min_source_pixels || maximum > capability.max_source_pixels || minimum > maximum) throw new Error("image_pixel_limit 超出模型能力边界")
    return { mode: "pixel_limit", min_pixels: minimum, max_pixels: maximum }
  }
  throw new Error("精度模式必须是 detail 或 image_pixel_limit")
}

function parsePreprocess(value: unknown, capability: VisionCapabilityProfile): VisionPreprocess {
  const source = objectValue(value, "图片预处理必须是对象")
  if (source.mode === "original") {
    exactKeys(source, ["mode"], "original")
    return { mode: "original" }
  }
  if (source.mode === "short_edge") {
    exactKeys(source, ["mode", "pixels"], "short_edge")
    const pixels = integerValue(source.pixels, "短边像素必须是整数")
    if (pixels < capability.min_dimension_pixels || pixels > Math.floor(Math.sqrt(capability.max_source_pixels))) throw new Error("短边像素超出模型能力边界")
    return { mode: "short_edge", pixels }
  }
  if (source.mode === "custom") {
    exactKeys(source, ["mode", "max_width", "max_height", "allow_upscale"], "custom")
    const width = integerValue(source.max_width, "最大宽度必须是整数")
    const height = integerValue(source.max_height, "最大高度必须是整数")
    if (source.allow_upscale !== false) throw new Error("无损预处理禁止放大")
    if (width < capability.min_dimension_pixels || height < capability.min_dimension_pixels || width * height > capability.max_source_pixels) throw new Error("自定义尺寸超出模型能力边界")
    const ratio = width / height
    if (ratio < capability.min_aspect_ratio || ratio > capability.max_aspect_ratio) throw new Error("自定义宽高比超出模型能力边界")
    return { mode: "custom", max_width: width, max_height: height, allow_upscale: false }
  }
  throw new Error("图片预处理必须是原图、短边或自定义尺寸")
}

function objectValue(value: unknown, message: string): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error(message)
  return value as Record<string, unknown>
}

function exactKeys(source: Record<string, unknown>, expected: readonly string[], label: string) {
  const actual = Object.keys(source).sort()
  const target = [...expected].sort()
  if (actual.length !== target.length || actual.some((key, index) => key !== target[index])) throw new Error(`${label} 字段不合法`)
}

function integerValue(value: unknown, message: string): number {
  if (!Number.isInteger(value)) throw new Error(message)
  return value as number
}

function numberValue(value: unknown, message: string): number {
  if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error(message)
  return value
}

function textValue(value: unknown, message: string): string {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !value.trim()) throw new Error(message)
  return value.trim()
}
